# 第 4 章　明霄竞武试锋芒•下

*2.5 版本主视觉　·　图源：米游社*

> Finest Duel Under the Pristine Blue (II)　·　明霄竞武试锋芒•下

## 022　At Dawn, To War　烽举烟传，枕弧待战

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Central Starskiff Haven　|　角色：Trailblazer; Yanqing; Feixiao; Yunli; March 7th; Yukong; Huaiyan　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''Rogues on the Run'' completed

### Head to Starskiff Haven and search for Yanqing

> *The Shackling Prison has fallen, and a pack of wolves has broken free. This startling development has upset everyone's plans before the Wardance ceremony could even commence. While the Xianzhou alleyways seem as calm and peaceful as ever, wolves capable of shapeshifting have already hidden themselves in the crowd. After escaping from The Shackling Prison in one piece, you find Yanqing, who is anxiously pacing about...*

*(Talk to Yanqing)*

After the Shackling Prison Break On the Starskiff Haven streets

**Excited Outworlder Tourist:** Seriously, when is this Wardance going to start? The Skysplitter has been hovering there for ages. When can we board and see what's inside?

> **兴奋的异星游客：**说起来，这演武仪典到底什么时候开始啊？我瞧着那艘竞锋舰在天空上飘了好些天了，啥时能登上去瞧瞧呢？

**Expectant Outworlder Tourist:** Is this how long-life species live? Everything proceeds so slowly. The effectiveness of "anticipation" in my emotional center is at an all-time high. Oh, I need to drink some coolant.

> **期待的异星游客：**这就是长生种的生活方式吗？节奏如此之慢。我情绪中枢里「期待」的效用值空前高涨啊。不行，我得喝些冷却液给它降降温了。

**Xianzhou Local Resident:** Relax, the Realm-Keeping Commission will send boarding notices to all ticket holders. The gazette said the Skysplitter will fire a salute before the ceremony starts!

> **仙舟本地居民：**放心吧，只要买了票，地衡司会提前把登舰通知发给咱们的。我看邸报上说，在典礼召开前，竞锋舰会鸣礼炮示意呢！

**Excited Outworlder Tourist:** I saw the schedule for the first day of the tournament. Can you believe the ringmaster accepted four challenges? And he's just a kid! So, who are you betting on, Bardeau?

> **兴奋的异星游客：**看首日赛事安排，作为东道主的守擂剑士一口气接下了四场挑战？听说他只是个半大孩子，真的假的？巴铎，你下注哪边赢？

**Expectant Outworlder Tourist:** I'm betting on neither. I lost all my credits betting on Roboball games in Taikiyan. This time, I've decided not to rely on probability games to accomplish my target of "getting rich"...

> **期待的异星游客：**哪边都不下。我在泰科铵看机动球赛时把信用点输了个精光。这次我决定不再寄希望靠概率游戏来完成「暴富」这一任务进程……

**Yanqing:** Wardance...

> **彦卿：**演武仪典，唉……

**Yanqing:** There you are, (Trailblazer).

> **彦卿：**开拓者，你来了啊。

**Yanqing:** I heard you and Mr. Dan Heng went to meet the judges, and then a riot broke out in The Shackling Prison. I was so worried about you. Thank goodness, you both made it out safe.

> **彦卿：**听说你和丹恒先生去面见判官，结果整个幽囚狱都陷入了动乱，我还在担心你们呢。二位没事就好。

- ▸ Discuss what happened...

> ▸ 谈谈发生的事…

**(Trailblazer):** There was a revolt in The Shackling Prison... Fortunately, we were protected by the guards until the Cloud Knights arrived to rescue us.

> **开拓者：**幽囚狱里暴动了…幸好我们被狱卒保护了起来，等来了云骑军的救援。

- ▸ What happened on your end?

> ▸ 你那边发生了什么？

**Yanqing:** I was planning to take Miss March and Yunli to Stargazer Navalia to see the Skysplitter up close, but we stumbled upon a group of suspicious foxians carrying official identities.

> **彦卿：**我本打算带三月和云璃一同去迴星港观望竞锋舰，但没想到，那地方竟出现了一群来历不明，却持有官方身份的狐人。

**Yanqing:** They were acting strange, so out of curiosity, we decided to follow them. It turns out that they were actually borisin disguised as foxians!

> **彦卿：**他们举止古怪。出于好奇，我们追踪了那几人，结果发现他们竟然是伪装成狐人的步离人！

**Yanqing:** It seems they infiltrated Stargazer Navalia in order to prepare for Hoolay's escape, and we foiled their plans by pure chance.

> **彦卿：**从一些线索来看，他们进入迴星港是为呼雷的逃亡做准备的，却阴差阳错被我们撞破了。

**Yanqing:** I wonder who provided them with those disguises, and how many more borisin have taken advantage of the Wardance to infiltrate the ship?

> **彦卿：**这些家伙的伪装身份是由谁提供的？借着演武仪典，还有多少相同的伪装者混进了仙舟？

- ▸ What's your plan now?

> ▸ 神策府这边打算怎么办？

**Yanqing:** The borisin traveled all the way here to infiltrate the Luofu and attempt to rescue their leader with a crafty scheme... This is completely unlike them. To be honest, it reminds me of what the Disciples of Sanctus Medicus did during the Ambrosial Arbor Crisis.

> **彦卿：**步离人千里迢迢潜入罗浮，试图用阴谋诡计救出他们的领袖…这整件事都太过蹊跷，不像步离所为，倒与此前建木灾异时药王乱党的行动有几分相似。

**Yanqing:** At present, all three generals are on guard and taking action to hunt down the fugitives and the mastermind behind it all, in order to prevent further trouble.

> **彦卿：**三位将军心有警惕，全都行动了起来，打算将逃犯和祸首连根拔起，以免再生事端。

**Yanqing:** However, if these borisin can disguise themselves as foxians... where do we even begin to search for them?

> **彦卿：**可是，如果这些步离人能伪装成狐人…我们又该从哪儿开始将这些家伙挖出来呢？

- ▸ Those tourists over there are talking about you...

> ▸ 那些游客在谈论你呢…

**Yanqing:** Yeah, I heard them, too, but I'm afraid I'll have to disappoint them. I've already reported to General Jing Yuan that I'll give up my role as the ringmaster.

> **彦卿：**是啊，我也听到了。不过，要让他们失望了。我已经禀报了将军，放弃了守擂剑士的身份。

- ▸ What? You're not going to defend the ring?

> ▸ 什么？你不去守擂了？

- ▸ But haven't you been preparing for it for so long?

> ▸ 你不是为此准备了很久吗？

**Yanqing:** The security of the Luofu is more important to me than anything else. I need to fulfill my duties as a Cloud Knight Lieutenant before taking on the honor of being a ringmaster.

> **彦卿：**对我而言，罗浮眼下的安全更优先。在担起守擂剑士这份荣耀前，我必须先履行作为云骑骁卫的职责。

**Yanqing:** Those tourists just want to see some good sword fights. Anyone can participate in this kind of tournament. It doesn't have to be me.

> **彦卿：**那些来来往往的游客，他们想要的只是欣赏一场精彩剑斗罢了。这样的比武，谁去都可以，又何必非得是我。

**Yanqing:** The real challenge lies beyond the ring on the Skysplitter. If we don't capture the fugitives soon, Hoolay will wreak havoc.

> **彦卿：**真正的胜负不在竞锋舰的擂台上，在这儿。如果不能尽快将逃犯抓捕归案，以呼雷穷凶极恶的素行，他一定会闹个天翻地覆。

**Yanqing:** Obviously, there is someone behind the scenes orchestrating this prison break, intending to spread chaos. If we fail to thwart their evil plan, what honor will be left for us to uphold?

> **彦卿：**这次逃狱事件的背后显然有人在精心谋划，试图掀起乱局，要是让这些人得逞了，云骑还有什么荣誉可言？

**???:** Well said.

> **？？？：**说得不错。

### Talk to Feixiao and prepare to hunt down the wolves

> *The Shackling Prison has fallen, and a pack of wolves has broken free. This startling development has upset everyone's plans before the Wardance ceremony could even commence. While the Xianzhou alleyways seem as calm and peaceful as ever, wolves capable of shapeshifting have already hidden themselves in the crowd. To the Merlin's Claw, hunting them down is of paramount importance.*

**Feixiao:** You may be tiny in stature, but your ambition matches that of the Yaoqing's warriors.

> **飞霄：**虽然身高高不过折凳，但罗浮的小娃娃也有不输曜青战士的志气啊。

**Yanqing:** Hey, this has nothing to do with my height.

> **彦卿：**这和身高没关系吧。

- ▸ What brings you here, General?

> ▸ 将军，你在这做什么？

- ▸ I thought all the generals were on the move already?

> ▸ 将军们不是行动起来了吗？

**Feixiao:** Yes, I suggested to Yanqing that we find a place to have a decent meal. A hunter must be well-fed before "the hunt."

> **飞霄：**是我让彦卿挑个能吃饱喝足的地方。在「巡猎」之前，猎人也得做好万全准备。

**Yanqing:** You still have a good appetite even in the face of disaster — you truly are the Merlin's Claw... Please, finish the food quickly so we can get to work, General Feixiao.

> **彦卿：**到底是「天击将军」，大难临头还有这么好的胃口…还请飞霄将军速战速决。

**Feixiao:** Well, that's not up to me... Dig in. I called you here so you could enjoy the food.

> **飞霄：**那恐怕由不得我——动筷子吧，我让你来这儿，是要看着你吃光它们。

**Yanqing:** Me? This isn't the time for a leisurely meal... and this is way too much for me!

> **彦卿：**我？现在哪是悠闲吃饭的时候…而且这也太多了吧！

**Feixiao:** Oh? You'd give me time to enjoy a meal but not yourself?

> **飞霄：**怎么，我吃就可以，你吃就不行？

**Feixiao:** Ever since you encountered those borisin spies, you've been so busy that you've hardly eaten anything. You can't defeat Hoolay on an empty stomach.

> **飞霄：**自从见了步离间谍，你就一直心神不宁，忙前走后水米不进。呼雷可不是瘪着肚子就能打赢的对手。

**Feixiao:** Take your time. Enjoy the meal and calm yourself.

> **飞霄：**别太心急了。给你一顿饭时间，好好冷静下吧。

**Yanqing:** Hoolay's whereabouts are still unknown, and they've even taken Mr. Jiaoqiu hostage. The longer we wait, the more complicated the situation becomes.

> **彦卿：**呼雷现在还下落不明，椒丘先生也落在他手中，咱们等待的时间越长，情况就越不可控啊。

**Feixiao:** Jiaoqiu always said I'm the most impatient person among the Cloud Knights, and I can't argue with that. So there's no reason for you to be more anxious than me.

> **飞霄：**椒丘总说我是云骑里最耐不住性子的人，他的劝诫向来是对的，所以你没理由比我更着急。

**Feixiao:** I've fought against borisin abominations for years, and I know their ferocity and cunning well. The borisin were clearly well-prepared for this prison break, and now they are staying in the shadows. They have no reason to rush out into the open.

> **飞霄：**我和步离孽物对抗多年，深知他们的凶残，也知晓那凶残背后的狡诈。这次劫狱事件，步离人显然是有备而来。敌在暗处，没理由贸然走上台前。

**Feixiao:** When facing cunning and ferocious prey, the hunter must be more patient, biding their time to seize the golden chance for a decisive blow. Once Hoolay loses his patience and reveals himself, that's when we'll strike.

> **飞霄：**猎物狡诈凶蛮，猎人更要以耐心撑持。犹如挽弓射敌，蓄力满弦，方能一击必杀。一旦呼雷失去耐心，露出爪牙，便是解决它的最佳时机。

**Yanqing:** But when will that time come?

> **彦卿：**这最佳时机什么时候才会出现？

**Feixiao:** Like I said... it's only one meal away.

> **飞霄：**我说过了…只需一顿饭的时间。

### Discuss what to do next with General Huaiyan

> *The Shackling Prison has fallen, and a pack of wolves has broken free. This startling development has upset everyone's plans before the Wardance ceremony could even commence. While the Xianzhou alleyways seem as calm and peaceful as ever, wolves capable of shapeshifting have already hidden themselves in the crowd. Meanwhile, March 7th and Yunli are at the Seat of Divine Foresight, listening attentively to General Huaiyan as he breaks down the situation at hand.*

After the Shackling Prison Break In the Seat of Divine Foresight

**Yukong:** General Huaiyan, the Sky-Faring Commission has finished its preparations. Do you have any other instructions?

> **驭空：**怀炎大人，天舶司已完成了相关准备。不知您是否另有指示？

**Huaiyan:** Representing the consensus between the Merlin's Claw and the Divine Foresight, I'll step in as the temporary commanding officer of the Seat of Divine Foresight and oversee the Six Commissions' affairs on the Luofu.

> **怀炎：**我将代表天击、神策二位将军的共识，暂时代行神策府与罗浮六司上下事务的指挥工作。

**Yukong:** What is the situation at The Shackling Prison?

> **驭空：**不知幽囚狱那边的情况如何？

**Huaiyan:** Hoolay made a quick escape and even sealed the gates of the prison. As of now, the Cloud Knights have re-established contact with the staff inside.

> **怀炎：**呼雷这厮倒是逃得飞快，还将门关紧紧闭锁。如今云骑军已经重新与内部取得了联系。

**Huaiyan:** The good news is, the two Nameless who were trapped in The Shackling Prison are safe and sound.

> **怀炎：**不幸中的万幸，被困幽囚狱的两位无名客安然无事。

**March 7th:** Um, I know I shouldn't use the word "great" given the current situation... but I'm relieved that (Trailblazer) and Dan Heng are safe.

> **三月七：**呃，虽然以眼下的情况不知道该不该说「太好了」三个字…但开拓者和丹恒没事，我就放心了。

**Huaiyan:** We've lost contact with one of the messengers from the Xianzhou Yaoqing. The borisin have likely taken him hostage.

> **怀炎：**曜青仙舟的使者中有一人生死不明，应当是遭到了步离人的劫持。

**Huaiyan:** Don't worry, Yukong. Feixiao is leading the Wolf Hunt operation, and you know how capable she is.

> **怀炎：**驭空，不必担心，飞霄一力承担起猎狼行动，对她的实力，你应该很清楚才是。

**Yukong:** I have no doubt about General Feixiao's capabilities, General Huaiyan. I'm more concerned about the Wardance.

> **驭空：**大人，我不怀疑飞霄将军的能力，我更关心的是演武仪典。

**Yukong:** According to the plan, the ceremony will begin in six hours. The Skysplitter will be activated, and all visitors will board the ship to watch the contest.

> **驭空：**依照计划，仪典将于三个时辰后召开，竞锋舰会即刻启动，允许观众登舰观赛。

**Yukong:** But now, with Hoolay's whereabouts unknown, everything is filled with uncertainty.

> **驭空：**可如今呼雷下落不明，一切充满了变数。

- ▸ Should the Wardance really be held as scheduled?

> ▸ 演武仪典还要照常举办吗？

**Yunli:** March is right, Grandpa. I heard the escaped prisoner from The Shackling Prison is beyond formidable. In case anything goes wrong...

> **云璃：**三月说的不错。爷爷，听说那个从幽囚狱里逃走的罪犯不是一般的可怕。万一有什么闪失……

**Huaiyan:** So, Yunli, what do you think we should do?

> **怀炎：**云璃，那么眼下的情况该如何处置啊？

**Yunli:** Well... hmm... I believe we should declare martial law and allocate manpower to search for the escaped prisoners. And for the Wardance, it's better to announce an indefinite delay for now.

> **云璃：**当然是…唔，当然应该宣布戒严，将人力投入搜捕工作。至于演武仪典，还是先宣布无限期延迟吧。

**Huaiyan:** Your plan sounds solid, but unfortunately, solid plans rarely get a chance to be implemented. I believe there are at least two parties who won't accept your approach.

> **怀炎：**你说的是个万全的法子。可惜越是理想的方案，越难施展得开。依我看，你的方法至少有两方人马不会接受。

- ▸ Which two parties?

> ▸ 哪两方人马？

**Huaiyan:** The first are the many travelers who have come all the way here for the Wardance. If we declare martial law now, it's like declaring that the Luofu isn't safe. How do you think the outsiders would react? They'd be terrified, and chaos might ensue.

> **怀炎：**其一是为演武仪典而来的众多商旅游客。宣布戒严，就等于公开声明罗浮并不安全，试问外人会作何反应？当然是人心惶惶，混乱不堪。

**Huaiyan:** The second party is the staff of the Xianzhou Luofu's Six Commissions. They've invested a lot of resources and manpower into organizing the Wardance. Suddenly suspending it indefinitely would create numerous challenges for them.

> **怀炎：**其二是罗浮仙舟的六司职员。筹办演武仪典所耗的人力、物力本就不可计数，眼下突然中止，又要无限期推迟，一系列变化只怕内部也难以承受。

**Huaiyan:** But of course, not everyone disagrees with your idea.

> **怀炎：**当然了，你的法子倒也不是没人认同。

**Yunli:** ...R—Really?

> **云璃：**…真、真的吗？

**Huaiyan:** The mastermind behind this incident would fully support your opinion presumably.

> **怀炎：**此次劫狱事件的幕后操纵者，想必会高举双手赞成你的看法。

**Huaiyan:** Hoolay's escape and your discovery of borisin spies at Stargazer Navalia... Even a child could guess that these events were orchestrated.

> **怀炎：**呼雷在探视中逃出囚笼，你们和彦卿又在迴星港发现了步离间谍…若说这背后无人弄鬼，只怕刚出生的娃娃都不会相信。

**Huaiyan:** The fugitive is just one piece in the game. The one controlling the pieces wants to spread chaos and suspicion among the people on the Luofu. If we declare martial law and postpone the Wardance, we'll fall into their trap and instill fear in people even before Hoolay actually does anything.

> **怀炎：**逃犯只是一枚棋子，那只执棋的手走下这一步，想看到的是一个罗浮人人猜疑，彼此自危的乱局。宣布戒严与推迟演武仪典，就正中了对手下怀，把呼雷带来的恐惧提前放到了台面上。

**Yunli:** ...So, what do we do?

> **云璃：**…那该怎么办嘛！

**Huaiyan:** We'll search for the prisoners while maintaining order on the Luofu, acting as if nothing has happened.

> **怀炎：**那些搜捕的事情当然要做，但罗浮之上的一切必须照旧如常，至少看起来一切如常。

**Yunli:** As if nothing has happened? Yanqing said he'd assist the generals, and now he's nowhere to be found. With the host's ringmaster gone, how are we supposed to act like nothing has happened?

> **云璃：**一切如常？彦卿这家伙跑了个没影，说是打算为将军们分忧解难。这下可好，东道主的守擂剑士缺席，倒是怎么个一切如常啊？

**Huaiyan:** That's why I called the two of you here.

> **怀炎：**对喽，这就是我把你们二位叫来此处的原因了。

- ▸ Are you suggesting that I take Yanqing's place as ringmaster?

> ▸ 莫非要我代替彦卿守擂？

- ▸ Are you suggesting that Yunli takes Yanqing's place as ringmaster?

> ▸ 莫非要云璃代替彦卿守擂？

**March 7th:** Master Yunli, is General Huaiyan asking you to be the ringmaster instead of Yanqing?

> **三月七：**云璃师傅，怀炎将军这是想让你替彦卿守擂呢？

**Huaiyan:** No, that's not the case.

> **怀炎：**非也，非也。

- ▸ Are you planning to take up the role of the ringmaster yourself, General Huaiyan?

> ▸ 莫非怀炎将军打算亲自上阵？

**Huaiyan:** Of course I'll do my part, but not on the Skysplitter.

> **怀炎：**老朽当然不会闲着，但我要去的地方却不是竞锋舰。

**March 7th:** General Huaiyan... Are you asking me... to take Yanqing's place... as the ringmaster?

> **三月七：**难不成…怀炎将军想让我…代替彦卿…登台守擂…吗？

**Huaiyan:** You caught on quickly, Miss March! That's exactly what I mean.

> **怀炎：**三月小姐真是冰雪聪明！老朽的意思嘛，就是这么个意思。

**Yunli:** But Miss March is a guest invited by General Jing Yuan, Grandpa! How can an outsider represent the Luofu in the ring? It will make the Xianzhou Luofu a laughingstock.

> **云璃：**爷爷，三月是景元将军请来观礼的客人！哪有让客人代表罗浮登台守擂的！这样岂不是让人耻笑罗浮仙舟无人了？

**Huaiyan:** Dear child, the Nameless of the Astral Express are renowned throughout the cosmos! It's an honor to have them participate in the ceremony! Plus, Miss March is a disciple of the little Cloud Knight Lieutenant. How can she be regarded as an "outsider"?

> **怀炎：**小傻瓜，星穹列车的无名客蜚声星海，能请来是何等光荣！何况三月小姐是以「云骑骁卫弟子」之名出战，又怎么能算是「罗浮仙舟无人」？

**Huaiyan:** We can't afford to have any problems during the Wardance. I'm entrusting you not only with the honor of the ring, but also with the security of the Skysplitter.

> **怀炎：**两位，「演武仪典」的召开不容有失。我要托付你们的绝不只是擂台上的胜负荣辱，还有竞锋舰的安全。

**Huaiyan:** Yukong, please explain to them the rest of the arrangements.

> **怀炎：**驭空，把接下来的安排告诉她们。

Switching to Lingsha's POV...

In The Shackling Prison, Lingsha, Dan Heng, and Jing Yuan are trying to discern clues from the borisin's attacks...

## 023　A Web of Pasts Rewoven　缕析条分，故事重问

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：The Shackling Prison　|　角色：Lingsha; Hanya; Jing Yuan; Dan Heng　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''At Dawn, To War'' completed

### Investigate the Shackling Prison with General Jing Yuan

> *On the other side, Jing Yuan, Dan Heng, and Lingsha arrive at The Shackling Prison, where all are busy picking up the pieces of the breakout. The trio examine the aftermath of the borisin's surprise attack, in search of clues that may help them catch the mastermind stirring up trouble from behind the scenes. Along the way, past events are revisited...*

Three dual-hours before the Wardance convenes In The Shackling Prison

**Hanya:** I am sorry for our improper management, and I truly appreciate your assistance, General.

> **寒鸦：**幽囚狱管束无方，劳动将军大驾，感激不尽。

**Jing Yuan:** There is no need to apologize. We Cloud Knights fight on the battlefield, while you judges punish the criminals. We are two sides of the same Xianzhou Luofu, and it is my honor to serve the Ten-Lords Commission.

> **景元：**判官多礼了。云骑战阵折冲，幽府威罚罪囚，云骑与幽府同为仙舟的一体两面。能为十王司效劳，也是我的荣幸。

**Jing Yuan:** I've grasped the situation of the prison break. Now, tell me more about the current state of The Shackling Prison.

> **景元：**犯人逃离的情况我已有数，说说幽囚狱眼下的状况吧。

**Hanya:** Borisin infiltrated the prison in disguise and released the prisoners, spreading chaos.

> **寒鸦：**步离狂徒伪装潜入幽囚狱，破狱释囚，制造混乱。

**Hanya:** Among the judges on duty in the four divisions, Judge Xueyi from the Detention Division was killed and is temporarily unavailable. So, I'm taking over her duties and commanding Aurumaton Spectral Envoys and spiritfarers to quickly restore order in the affected areas.

> **寒鸦：**拘、锁、刑、问四部当值判官中，拘字部判官雪衣暂时阵亡。我将代其职责，命金人勾魂使与冥差们进入各层，争取尽快恢复失控的区域。

**Jing Yuan:** I, and the two behind me, will go deeper into the prison to investigate.

> **景元：**我与身后这两位将会下到幽狱深处探查线索。

**Hanya:** But General, the situation inside is still chaotic and perilous. Your presence would be...

> **寒鸦：**将军，如今各层的情势依旧混乱不明，您亲临险地——

**Jing Yuan:** It's common for Cloud Knight generals to face danger directly. Hoolay has escaped, and the Yaoqing envoy is being held hostage by the borisin. His fate is unknown. This is a grave matter.

> **景元：**云骑将军亲履险地是常事。呼雷出逃，曜青来使遭到步离人挟持，生死不明。这桩祸事非同小可。

**Jing Yuan:** Not only did the Merlin's Claw offer no reproach, she decided to go after Hoolay herself to prevent further calamity. I believe the Luofu owes her something in return for her fervent determination...

> **景元：**天击将军非但没有半句怨言，反而决定出手擒狼，以绝后患。如此滚热的心肠，于情于理，我想罗浮都必须给个交代——

**Jing Yuan:** How did the infiltrators learn about the location where Hoolay was held? And how did they time their plan just before the Yaoqing messengers were ready to escort him...?

> **景元：**劫狱的步离人从何处得知呼雷关押的区域？又为何如此赶巧，他们在曜青来使准备押解之前执行了计划……

**Jing Yuan:** Finding the answers to these questions shouldn't be too difficult. What truly matters is if we can gather the clues that lead us to the mastermind behind all of this.

> **景元：**在我看来，这些问题的答案并不难找。重要的是，能不能找到蛛丝马迹，牵出幕后黑手。

**Hanya:** I understand, but I'm afraid it won't be an easy task.

> **寒鸦：**我明白了。只不过将军欲行之事，恐怕很难啊。

**Jing Yuan:** For a long time, this hidden force has been pursuing their own goals and undermining the security of the Luofu. Backing down now will only encourage them to further endanger the peace here.

> **景元：**长久以来，这只幕后黑手罔顾罗浮安危，一意孤行。退让只会助长对方的气焰，进一步危及罗浮的太平。

**Hanya:** The Ten-Lords Commission will support your decision with everything we have, General.

> **寒鸦：**诚如将军所言。将军所做决断，十王司将倾力协助。

**Jing Yuan:** Dan Heng and Lingsha, I'll need both of you to accompany me on this challenging journey.

> **景元：**丹恒、灵砂，要劳烦两位随我一起走这趟苦差了。

**Lingsha:** Well, it's part of my responsibilities as the Cauldron Master. So, where would you like to start, General?

> **灵砂：**丹鼎司的分内之责罢了，便是苦药也得吃下去呵。将军想打哪儿查起？

**Jing Yuan:** Let's start with those borisin disguised as foxians.

> **景元：**就从那些伪装成狐人的步离人开始。

**Lingsha:** My people have already prepared the evidence.

> **灵砂：**我已让人将物证准备妥帖了。

### Investigate the borisin corpse in the hall

*(Approach the borisin corpse)*

**Lingsha:** According to Lieutenant Yanqing's report, he stumbled upon a few suspicious foxians at Stargazer Navalia. After following them, he discovered that they were actually borisin in disguise. This is one of the bodies.

> **灵砂：**按照彦卿骁卫的报告，他在迴星港发现几个来历不明的狐人，一番追踪后察觉对方是由步离人伪装。这是其中一人的遗体。

**Dan Heng:** Looking at him now, it's hard to imagine how he transformed into a foxian.

> **丹恒：**看他如今的样子，怎么也无法想象这家伙能伪装成狐人。

**Lingsha:** My alchemists detected some... complex ingredients in his remains, which might explain how these borisin were able to disguise themselves as foxians.

> **灵砂：**丹士们从他的遗骸中检测出了一些…复杂的药物成分，似乎能解释这些步离人为何可以变形成狐人。

**Lingsha:** Simply put, foxians and borisin share a common ancestry. Although they look completely different now, there isn't much genetic difference between them. This medicine allows borisin to temporarily change their shape into that of foxians.

> **灵砂：**简单来说，狐人与步离人同宗同源，虽然如今形貌殊异，但二者在基因层面并无太大差别。这种药物能允许步离人暂时变化形骸，以狐人的面貌示人。

**Dan Heng:** So, in other words, if they stop taking the medicine, their true form will soon be revealed?

> **丹恒：**也就是说，一旦对方停止服药，很快就会原形毕露？

- ▸ Borisin have fast metabolisms, so the effects of medicines don't last long...

> ▸ 步离人代谢很快，药效不持久…

**Lingsha:** Indeed. This means that these borisin have a steady supply of the medicine within the Luofu.

> **灵砂：**是啊，这也意味着在罗浮内部有为这些伪装者提供药物的来源。

- ▸ An infiltration technique the general is familiar with...

> ▸ 将军大人熟悉的伪装潜入手法…

**Jing Yuan:** The formula and technique seem oddly familiar.

> **景元：**熟悉的配方，熟悉的手法。

**Lingsha:** The Disciples of Sanctus Medicus. Looks like the Alchemy Commission is involved once again.

> **灵砂：**唉，「药王秘传」除之不尽，这回丹鼎司怕是又脱不了干系了。

**Lingsha:** When I was sorting through the prescriptions they used, I came across one called "Semblance Reversion Essence." It's designed to help those disciples suppress signs of their mara-struck form and maintain their normal appearance. When I compared it to the medicine found in the borisin's body...

> **灵砂：**妾身在梳理他们所使用的丹方时，发现其中有一味「还尘驻形丹」，专供探子们压制魔阴身的征兆，维持平常形貌。与步离人体内的药物两相对比——

**Dan Heng:** They're one and the same, aren't they?

> **丹恒：**想来这是同一种东西吧？

**Lingsha:** The medicinal properties and ingredients may differ, but the principle remains the same.

> **灵砂：**药性与成分虽然不同，但原理确是一致的。

**Jing Yuan:** Since ancient times, the borisin have always sought to have more powerful bodies, regarding the foxians as weaklings. Yet, in order to save their Warhead, they were willing to disguise themselves as foxians... Their determination is quite remarkable.

> **景元：**从远古时代起，步离人一直都在追求更强大的血肉之躯，将狐人视为孱弱之辈。如今为了救出战首，这些人竟愿意伪装成狐人的形貌…他们所下的决心当真不小啊。

**Lingsha:** If these infiltrators rely on the medicine to maintain their disguise, then following this lead in our investigation seems prudent. Please follow me.

> **灵砂：**如果这群步离探子全都服食了伪装药物，我建议就顺着这条线开始查起。两位，随我来。

### Follow the smoke from Lingsha's censer and keep moving

**Lingsha:** Here we are. According to the judge, this area is not yet under control, so we should proceed cautiously.

> **灵砂：**就是此处了。按判官所说，此处尚未收复，请小心行动。

**Lingsha:** I've captured the nature of the medicine in my censer.

> **灵砂：**我在香炉中记下了步离人所服药物的性质。

**Lingsha:** By following the medicinal fumes, we should be able to retrace the steps of the disguised borisin.

> **灵砂：**顺着药气寻索一番，应该能甄别出伪装的步离人留下的踪迹。

### (Optional) Investigate suspicious clues along the escape route

*(Investigate the prison guard corpse, optional)*

A prison guard is collapsed on the ground, her body twisted unnaturally.

- ▸ (Examine the remains of the warden)

> ▸ （检查武弁的遗体）

**Lingsha:** Incredible strength... The attacker shattered this warden's bones with a single blow.

> **灵砂：**好惊人的力量…凶手只用了一击便打碎了这名武弁的骨骼。

**Jing Yuan:** Such brute strength is not something an ordinary borisin possesses... This is likely the work of Hoolay.

> **景元：**这蛮力，绝不是寻常的步离人能办到的…多半是呼雷。

**Lingsha:** If I may be so bold to ask, is this borisin truly that formidable?

> **灵砂：**恕妾身冒昧一问，这个步离人真有如此凶悍？

**Jing Yuan:** I have lived a bit longer and engaged in a few more battles than you, Miss Lingsha. To the Alliance, borisin have always been the most formidable adversaries, and Hoolay is a monster feared even among his own kind.

> **景元：**我比灵砂小姐活得久些，也多经历了几场战事。对于联盟，步离人始终是最难缠的敌人。而呼雷则是连步离人自己都畏惧不已的怪物。

**Jing Yuan:** With his strength, Hoolay united numerous borisin packs, forming a grand army of Abominations of Abundance. They constantly pushed the Alliance's armies into perilous positions.

> **景元：**他以一己之力统合众多猎群，纠集丰饶孽物大军，多次将联盟的军队逼入险地。

**Jing Yuan:** Over seven centuries ago, I followed my mentor on a campaign against the abominations, and I personally witnessed the devastation on the battlefield after Hoolay appeared.

> **景元：**七百多年前，我随恩师出征讨伐孽物，亲眼目睹了那头巨兽降临后整个战场的惨况。

**Jing Yuan:** Even with medicinal pellets that suppressed the fear caused by his Lupitoxin, countless Cloud Knights succumbed to panic in the face of his murderous aura. They couldn't even raise a hand in resistance... If the former Sword Champion hadn't immobilized Hoolay with her Frost Blade, the outcome of that fateful battle could have been very different.

> **景元：**即便服下压制狼毒恐惧的丹药，但仍然有无数云骑在他的凶残气势之下，恐慌到连抬手反抗都无有余力…若不是前任剑首以霜刃封住呼雷行动，胜负仍未可知。

**Jing Yuan:** By the end of the battle, only a few of us remained. "The crimson moon casts the sheen of blood on all"... Everything I saw was painted dark red.

> **景元：**那场大战的尾声，队伍里仅剩下了寥寥数人。「赤月临照，血光飞射」…当时眼前所见的一切，只剩下满目殷红。

**Lingsha:** If that's the case, why wasn't the beast executed instead of being imprisoned?

> **灵砂：**既然如此，为何在降服这头恶兽后没将他处以极刑，反而只是关押起来？

**Lingsha:** On the Xianzhou Zhuming, the judges cast those unforgivable and nearly immortal Abominations of Abundance into the eternal flames of the stars, reducing them to ashes.

> **灵砂：**在朱明仙舟，判官们将罪无可恕又百杀不死的丰饶孽物丢进恒星的劫火中焚烧。

**Lingsha:** Their so-called "immortality" is just a facade. I mean, nothing can truly defy death, can it? I just don't understand why the Luofu kept this tumor for so long, leading to the terrible situation we're facing now.

> **灵砂：**所谓「不死」不过是个名头罢了。世上岂有真正不死不灭的东西？不知罗浮为何要将这颗毒瘤延宕压抑如此之久，导致今日难以收拾的局面？

**Lingsha:** But I guess I understand. The people of the Luofu are known for their kindness. Even when faced with the malignant tumor within the Alchemy Commission, they couldn't bring themselves to cut it out. Instead, they exiled the healer who tried to solve the problem to the Xianzhou Zhuming.

> **灵砂：**也对，罗浮人向来宅心仁厚，即使对寄生在丹鼎司的瘤子，也舍不得剜肉疗毒，倒是把想要施救的医者送去了朱明仙舟。

**Jing Yuan:** I understand if you hold grudges against me, Miss Lingsha. I take the blame for the resurgence of the Disciples of Sanctus Medicus. As for why Hoolay was only imprisoned... I can shed some light on that too.

> **景元：**看得出来，灵砂小姐对我有怨气。药王秘传死灰复燃一事，景元责无旁贷。至于呼雷这头孽物为何只被镇伏在牢狱中…我也可以为你解释一二。

**Lingsha:** I'm just a healer, and I'm not familiar with the past. I'd appreciate it if you could enlighten me, General.

> **灵砂：**妾身只是一介医士，不知旧事，还请将军点拨解惑。

**Jing Yuan:** Alright, let me tell you more about it as we go.

> **景元：**好，那我们这一路上慢慢说来。

*(Investigate the Cloud Knight corpse, optional)*

**Dan Heng:** ...Did this Cloud Knight also take the medicine?

> **丹恒：**…这名死去的云骑身上也有药气？

Lingsha peels open the Cloud Knight's armor and stretches out her hands to search for a moment.

**Lingsha:** No, this is a borisin in disguise. A guard killed him before he could return to his original form.

> **灵砂：**不，这应当是个步离人。没来得及变回原形，就被狱卒当场格杀了。

**Jing Yuan:** All these borisin are dressed in official attire. Besides the Cloud Knight, there were also two borisin disguised as members of the Sky-Faring Commission and the Artisanship Commission.

> **景元：**这群步离人都身着官方服色。除云骑之外，还有两人以天舶司和工造司的身份示人。

**Jing Yuan:** Whoever is providing them with official identities must hold a position of power... Let's search elsewhere.

> **景元：**能办妥这些伪装身份的人想必位高权重…我们去别处瞧瞧。

*(Investigate the prisoner corpse, optional)*

A prisoner is on the ground. The joy of regaining freedom and sudden shock, these two expressions are frozen on his pale face at the moment of death.

**Dan Heng:** Someone bit open his arteries and drained almost all of his blood while he was still alive. Such a cruel and ruthless act...

> **丹恒：**他气绝之前曾被人咬开动脉，吸走大量的血。真是粗暴残忍的手法……

**Lingsha:** Despite being a long-lived species, borisin are actually more like predatory beasts that must feed on raw blood and flesh. I've heard that Hoolay was deprived of food and water in The Shackling Prison...

> **灵砂：**若无生血生肉吞食，步离人便会饥渴难耐，他们虽为长生种，但却更接近掠食的兽类。听说幽囚狱中对呼雷禁绝饮食……

**Lingsha:** It's hard to imagine how he managed to suppress his hunger for over seven centuries. Will the hostage from the Yaoqing be able to avoid being his meal?

> **灵砂：**真是难以想象，七百多年不曾进食饮水，他一定压抑饥饿许久。不知道那位被他劫持的曜青人质能否逃过一劫？

**Jing Yuan:** Such is the terrible nature of the Abominations of Abundance. We subjected him to the Forest of Swords to drain his life force, but in the end, his punishment turned into a test of our patience.

> **景元：**这便是丰饶孽物的可怕之处。对他施加剑树之刑，消磨其生命力，结果他的刑期反倒成了对我们耐心的考验。

**Jing Yuan:** Just like you said, Miss Lingsha, casting a creature that can't be killed into a star would be a way to permanently get rid of it, but unfortunately...

> **景元：**诚如灵砂小姐所说，将百杀不死的怪物投入恒星，是个一了百了的法子。可惜……

**Lingsha:** ...The foxians didn't agree to that.

> **灵砂：**…可惜狐人不答应。

**Jing Yuan:** Exactly. The atrocities committed by Hoolay went beyond mere massacres. Throughout numerous wars, we made every effort to eradicate the borisin, but Hoolay, with his mysterious sorcery, turned countless foxians into his pawns, and so the borisin kept returning.

> **景元：**不错，呼雷所犯的罪行不仅是杀戮。数千场战争中，我们尽力剿灭步离人，但他凭着不知源头的邪术，将无数狐人化为受它驱策的走卒与器兽，一再卷土重来。

**Jing Yuan:** The foxians curse his name day and night, and they even use it to scare children into staying quiet. How could they grant a swift death to such a great sinner?

> **景元：**狐人一族日夜诅咒他的名字，甚至用他的名字来吓止小儿夜啼。任这样的巨恶在一夕之间痛快死去，联盟内的狐人又岂肯甘心？

**Jing Yuan:** I wonder if you know why Hoolay wasn't taken into custody by the foxian-majority Xianzhou Yaoqing, but instead imprisoned in the Xianzhou Luofu, Miss Lingsha.

> **景元：**不知灵砂小姐是否清楚，为何最后呼雷没有被狐人居多的曜青仙舟收押，却独独囚禁在了罗浮之上？

**Lingsha:** As you mentioned, your mentor was an exceptional warrior and was the one who defeated Hoolay, an extraordinary achievement. Therefore, the marshal placed this feral beast under the jurisdiction of the Xianzhou Luofu as an honor, I assume?

> **灵砂：**刚才将军说了，令师武艺超群，将呼雷击败，立下大功。因此元帅下令将这头凶兽交由罗浮处置，也算是一份荣耀？

**Jing Yuan:** It seems you have a major misunderstanding about this arrangement, Miss Lingsha. Allow me to explain it to you.

> **景元：**灵砂小姐对于这一处置有莫大的误解，容我慢慢道来。

### Follow the smoke from Lingsha's censer and keep moving

*(Approach the wolf-shaped mech)*

The wolf-shaped mech disintegrates into a pile of metal.

**Jing Yuan:** Yanqing told me that an IPC ship was attacked by borisin. Is this what they were transporting on the ship?

> **景元：**我听彦卿说过，公司的舰船遭到了步离人袭击。船上运送的就是这东西？

- ▸ A biomimetic weapon that will auto-restart even after being turned off...

> ▸ 关闭后也会自启动的生物兵器…

- ▸ The Intelligentsia Guild's research on the traits of longevity...

> ▸ 博识学会有关长生特性的研究…

**Lingsha:** Yes, the Artisanship Commission and Alchemy Commission conducted a joint examination and found that... the parts of this machine are made from specially refined borisin bio-tissue.

> **灵砂：**是。工造司和丹鼎司联手做了勘验…这机器的部件使用了特殊加工过的步离生物组织。

**Lingsha:** I heard the Intelligentsia Guild has been researching the biological properties of long-life species, hoping to make medical or combat-related discoveries. However, they haven't dared to cross any lines due to their so-called "relationship" with the Alliance.

> **灵砂：**听说一直以来博识学会都在研究长生种的生物特性，想获取能用于医疗或战斗的成果。只是碍于和联盟的表面关系，不敢做得太过出格。

**Dan Heng:** Perhaps to those scholars, borisin are no different from lab animals. Maybe the borisin attacked that ship to retaliate against the Intelligentsia Guild for experimenting on them?

> **丹恒：**也许在那些学士眼中，步离人与实验动物也没什么区别。步离人之所以会袭击那艘舰船，也许就是想报复博识学会对他们的同胞的实验？

**Lingsha:** No, if it were only about revenge, they could just wreck the ship and destroy all the cargo, instead of allowing it to end up in The Shackling Prison.

> **灵砂：**不，如果只是单纯为了向公司和学会复仇，他们大可以破坏舰船，毁掉所有的货物。这样一来，它就不会出现在幽囚狱里了。

**Lingsha:** It was a deliberate display to showcase the dangerous nature of the "cargo" in broad daylight. This way, the cargo would end up in The Shackling Prison, serving as a tool for the prison break.

> **灵砂：**这是一场刻意为之的表演，将「货物」的危险程度暴露在人前，只为了让它们能顺利被送进幽囚狱中充当劫狱的武器。

**Jing Yuan:** This skill in exploiting the blind spots of others' mindsets is so atypical of them. I'm afraid the IPC and the Intelligentsia Guild unknowingly became their accomplices.

> **景元：**如此善用人心的盲点，这与步离人一贯的作风大相径庭。公司和博识学会怕也是在不明不白的状况下，被人当了枪使。

Something seems to be hiding in the pile of metal on the ground, ready to move.

**Dan Heng:** Watch out. This thing is still alive!

> **丹恒：**两位，当心。这东西还活着！

*(Begin battle against Howling Casket ×1)*

**Dan Heng:** It can regenerate even after suffering such severe attacks?

> **丹恒：**遭到这么严重的破坏，也能自我复原吗？

**Lingsha:** Their imitation of long-life species has crossed the line.

> **灵砂：**他们对长生种的模仿真是太过头了。

*(After the Howling Casket enters the Moon Rage state)*

**Jing Yuan:** If it can still move, it means it wasn't damaged enough.

> **景元：**还能动，就说明坏得还不够彻底。

**Jing Yuan:** Let's make sure it never moves again.

> **景元：**来吧，让它再也动弹不得。

*(After the battle)*

The giant machine goes silent again. Like a wounded beast drained of blood, it can no longer move.

**Lingsha:** You haven't answered my question about why Hoolay was imprisoned in the Luofu instead of the Yaoqing, General. You've been reserved in your response. Could it be that such an arrangement wasn't an honor?

> **灵砂：**将军一直没回答我的问题呢，「为何呼雷被囚禁在罗浮而非曜青」。您如此三缄其口，莫非这一处置并不是什么荣耀？

**Jing Yuan:** The reason why the marshal didn't leave Hoolay on the Yaoqing lies in the very mech in front of us.

> **景元：**元帅没有将呼雷留在曜青的原因，就在眼前这台机器上。

**Lingsha:** ...Are you suggesting that there are people trying to understand Hoolay's secrets and use them for their own purposes, just like with this mech?

> **灵砂：**…你是说，有人想要像这台机器所代表的那样，破解呼雷的秘密，学以致用？

**Lingsha:** Well, I understand. I've heard that the foxians on the Yaoqing share a bloodline with the borisin, and just like the borisin, some of the foxians there experience an uncontrollable insanity called Moon Rage.

> **灵砂：**我明白了。我听说曜青的狐人与步离人的血脉尤为相近，其中有些狐族子裔会像步离人一样，不可遏制地陷入名为「月狂」的疯症。

**Lingsha:** The marshal believed that this would be inhumane and no different from what the borisin did, so...

> **灵砂：**元帅认为此事有非人之嫌，与步离人无异，所以……

**Jing Yuan:** Exactly. While borisin see Moon Rage as a blessing that unlocks their true potential, foxians see it as an inevitable curse within their bloodline. Countless healers of the Yaoqing have attempted to unravel this mystery, but to no avail.

> **景元：**你没猜错。在步离人看来，月狂是解放力量的恩赐；但对狐人来说，这是血脉中避之不及的疯狂。曜青的医士们世世代代都有人试图破解这一谜题，但始终不得其法。

**Jing Yuan:** "Why can borisin control the power of Moon Rage?" "Can we foxians break free from this curse?" ...These questions were asked frequently.

> **景元：**「为何步离人能控制月狂？」「狐人能否破除这一诅咒？」…总会有人问起这样的问题。

**Jing Yuan:** Each questioner had good intentions, but the road to catastrophe is paved with good intentions.

> **景元：**每个提问之人的初衷都满怀善意。但是，世上所有通往灾难的道路，都是由善念铺就的。

**Jing Yuan:** To the foxians of the Yaoqing, Hoolay was not only the Warhead of the borisin but also a monster that could be the subject of much research. Hoolay thus became a poison that corrupted people's minds without their knowing.

> **景元：**对曜青的狐人而言，「呼雷」不仅仅是步离人的战首，也是怪物，是他们的研究对象。他成了腐蚀人心却不自知的剧毒。

**Lingsha:** That's why the marshal decided to imprison Hoolay in the Luofu. It wasn't an honor but rather... a warning.

> **灵砂：**所以，元帅将呼雷囚禁在罗浮。这确实不是荣誉，而是…告诫。

**Jing Yuan:** Because such selfish pursuits in the name of "good intentions" once led to a tragedy on the Luofu that served as a warning to future generations.

> **景元：**因为同样为了一念私心，为了所谓的利人善举，罗浮上曾经发生过一桩足以警示后人的悲剧。

**Lingsha:** The Sedition of Imbibitor Lunae.

> **灵砂：**「饮月之乱」。

**Dan Heng:** ...

> **丹恒：**……

**Lingsha:** By imprisoning Hoolay in the Xianzhou Luofu, the marshal both suppressed the dangerous intentions of the Yaoqing foxians and warned the Xianzhou Luofu to refrain from repeating its mistake.

> **灵砂：**元帅借呼雷移交罗浮一事，既暂时平息了曜青狐人的恶念，也告诫了经历乱局的罗浮仙舟。

**Jing Yuan:** That was a necessary trade-off. The Xianzhou Alliance is not solely about the Xianzhou natives. Only when all three races — Foxian, Vidyadhara, and Xianzhou natives — form an alliance will there be a promising future for all.

> **景元：**这就是「权衡」，也是不得不做的一步。仙舟联盟不是只有仙舟人的一言堂。狐人，持明，仙舟人三族共盟，方有未来。

**Lingsha:** Thank you for enlightening me. Was it for the same reason that you "traded off" my mentor to the Xianzhou Zhuming, only to stand idle and allow the resurgence of the Disciples of Sanctus Medicus?

> **灵砂：**灵砂受教了。您当年也怀着这样的心态「权衡」了我的师父，将她放逐去了朱明仙舟，却坐看「药王秘传」在其中死灰复燃？

**Jing Yuan:** You said that I couldn't bear to cut out the malignant tumor within the Alchemy Commission but instead exiled the healer who tried to solve the problem to the Xianzhou Zhuming...

> **景元：**你说我舍不得对丹鼎司剜肉疗毒，倒是把想要施救的医者送去了朱明仙舟……

**Jing Yuan:** But did your mentor tell you... her "good intentions" led her to perform certain healing arts on Dan Heng, who had just finished his hatching rebirth, so that he would regain the memories of his past life?

> **景元：**那么令师有没有告诉你…她为了自己一厢情愿的善念，借探视的便利，为彼时才刚刚蜕生完毕的丹恒施展了能回忆前生知识的医术？

**Dan Heng and Lingsha:** What did you just say?

> **丹恒&灵砂：**你说什么？

**Jing Yuan:** She believed that restoring the High Elder's knowledge of his past life would allow the Vidyadhara to resume their duty as the guardians of the Ambrosial Arbor, quelling the strife within their clan and bringing everything back on the right path.

> **景元：**她以为龙尊恢复前世神智，便能重续持明族守望建木的职责，能让蠢蠢欲动的势力蛰伏，也能让族中一切纷争平息重回正轨。

**Jing Yuan:** But just as I mentioned earlier, the road to catastrophe is always paved with good intentions. Since then, the Six Charioteers decided that the Alchemy Commission would no longer have a Cauldron Master, until your arrival now.

> **景元：**但正如我方才所说：许多利人善举，不过是灾难的开始。自那之后，六御合议决定，丹鼎司不再有司鼎一职，直到你今日前来。

**Lingsha:** If that's the case, I should thank you for protecting my mentor by exiling her, General.

> **灵砂：**如此说来，妾身要感谢将军以一纸流放令保护了老师。

**Jing Yuan:** Quite the contrary. I should be the one thanking you.

> **景元：**恰恰相反，是我该谢你才对。

**Lingsha:** Thanking me?

> **灵砂：**谢我？

**Jing Yuan:** All I ever want is to have a clear conscience. However, can long-life species truly achieve that goal in their long-drawn lives? For example, you were implicated along with your mentor and forced to leave your homeland without knowing the truth.

> **景元：**我所行所求，不过是「问心无愧」四字。但长生种漫长的一生中，真能无愧吗？灵砂小姐为师父的判罚牵累，也不得不远走他乡，对个中缘由一无所知。

**Jing Yuan:** And now, with the complicated situation in the Alchemy Commission, the Alliance has spared me a lot of trouble by sending you to handle this challenging task. Shouldn't I be thanking you instead?

> **景元：**而今丹鼎司的情况错综复杂，联盟将你派来挑起这桩苦差事，是省了我的心。难道我不应该谢你吗？

**Lingsha:** ...Your eloquence is indeed impressive, the Divine Foresight. You won't even leave me any excuses to hold a grudge against you.

> **灵砂：**…不愧是神策将军，连让人兴师问罪的话茬都不留一个。

**Lingsha:** But let me make it clear: The Alliance sent me here to handle the business impartially, not to choose sides.

> **灵砂：**有言在先，联盟将我派来此处，是要我妥善处理公事，可不是让我选边站的。

**Jing Yuan:** It doesn't matter which side you choose, Miss Lingsha. At the end of the day, both you and I stand on the side of the Alliance, don't we? Let's keep going.

> **景元：**灵砂小姐想站在谁这一边不重要，毕竟你和我都站在联盟这一边，不是吗？我们走吧。

*(Approach the marked location)*

A mess of bodies, a fierce battle must have been fought here.

- ▸ (Examine the mara-struck remains)

> ▸ （检查魔阴身遗体）

**Lingsha:** These mara-struck soldiers... don't appear to be escaped prisoners.

> **灵砂：**死在此地的魔阴身…似乎并不是囚牢里逃离的犯人。

**Jing Yuan:** How can you tell?

> **景元：**何以见得？

**Lingsha:** These soldiers are fully armed. Obviously, they didn't hastily join the battle.

> **灵砂：**这些魔阴身衣甲武器齐备，显然不是仓促加入战斗的。

**Dan Heng:** The messenger named Moze did say that there were two groups of attackers. The other attackers, aside from the borisin, could hide their tracks. I believe he was referring to these people.

> **丹恒：**那个叫貊泽的曜青使者说过，来劫狱的犯人共有两拨。除去步离人外，还有一群能隐藏行迹的魔阴身。他所指的应该就是这些人了。

**Jing Yuan:** Hide their tracks?

> **景元：**「隐藏行迹」？

**Dan Heng:** Exactly. I've engaged with these attackers before, and they used cloudhymn magic to hide their presence. Without careful observation, no one can detect them.

> **丹恒：**不错，我与这些魔阴身交过手，他们使用云吟术遮掩身形。如果不谨慎观察，无人能察觉他们的行踪。

**Lingsha:** You once warned me to be cautious of the Vidyadhara Elders' influence within the Alchemy Commission. Could it be...

> **灵砂：**丹恒先生曾经警告我慎重处理持明长老对丹鼎司的干涉。难不成……

**Jing Yuan:** What's on your mind, Miss Lingsha?

> **景元：**灵砂小姐看出什么来了？

**Lingsha:** Well, it seems that someone provided the attackers with a map of The Shackling Prison. And considering the Vidyadhara's involvement in the prison's construction, it raises suspicions about their role in this.

> **灵砂：**其一，以步离劫狱者逃离幽囚狱的路线来看，应当是有人将幽囚狱的地形透露给了他们。在幽囚狱的修建过程中，持明族出力不少。

**Lingsha:** Additionally, the fact that the borisin need medicine to disguise themselves suggests that there are still moles within the Alchemy Commission assisting them covertly.

> **灵砂：**其二，伪装的步离人需要服药才能维持狐人形貌。显然丹鼎司中仍有余孽在暗中帮助。

**Lingsha:** Moreover, forging official identities for the undercover borisin would require someone with significant authority.

> **灵砂：**其三，能为潜伏的步离人伪造官方的身份，显然非身居高位之人无法办到。

**Lingsha:** And the presence of assassins capable of using cloudhymn magic deepens my suspicions about the Preceptors.

> **灵砂：**至于这些使用云吟的刺客，让龙师身上的这份嫌疑又加重了一些。

**Lingsha:** But why would the Vidyadhara collude with the borisin and aid in Hoolay's escape?

> **灵砂：**可是他们身为持明，为何要勾结步离人，协助呼雷逃脱？

**Dan Heng:** They aim to spread chaos. They believe that only in chaos can they regain their former power and influence.

> **丹恒：**为了混乱。只有混乱才能给他们一线希望，让他们重新攀上权力的巅峰。

**Dan Heng:** Since the Sedition of Imbibitor Lunae, the once-proud dragon race has been powerless, watching their influence wane. Being a native of the Luofu, Miss Lingsha, I believe you understand the implications.

> **丹恒：**自「饮月之乱」结束后，身为骄傲的龙脉族裔，面对日益颓败的局势，却无力回天。灵砂小姐出身罗浮，应该明白我的意思。

**Jing Yuan:** However, I don't think the Preceptors are the true masterminds. If I'm not mistaken, the one behind all this treachery is the Lord Ravager who exploited the weaknesses within the Luofu and instigated the Disciples of Sanctus Medicus, ultimately leading to the Ambrosial Arbor's resurrection — Phantylia.

> **景元：**不过在我看来，持明龙师实在算不上什么幕后黑手。那位看透了罗浮之内的种种裂痕，策动药王秘传叛乱，导致建木重生的绝灭大君——幻胧，她才是这一切背叛的根由。

**Lingsha:** Please remember, General, that everything I've mentioned is mere speculation. We need concrete evidence for a public trial. If you want to interrogate someone within the Vidyadhara's ranks, you'll need irrefutable proof.

> **灵砂：**容妾身提醒将军一句，我刚才所说的一切，至多只能算是推论。刑狱公案，讲究的是一个铁证如山。若要闯入持明的洞天拿人问罪，总得让他们哑口无言才是。

**Jing Yuan:** So, what's your plan, Miss Lingsha?

> **景元：**灵砂小姐有什么打算呢？

**Lingsha:** I'll send an "invitation" to the Preceptors.

> **灵砂：**妾身打算为龙师们送去一份「请柬」。

**Dan Heng:** An invitation?

> **丹恒：**请柬？

**Lingsha:** The Preceptors have been inviting me for a personal meeting since my arrival on the Luofu. Now, I'll send them the remains of these mara-struck soldiers and the route map I found in the prisoner's possession. Then, I'll ask them to meet me at Scalegorge Waterscape.

> **灵砂：**自我来到罗浮，龙师一再向我邀约会晤。我要将这些魔阴身的遗骸，以及幽囚狱犯人逃亡的路线图送去龙师的府邸，然后邀请他们在鳞渊境和我见上一面。

**Lingsha:** I'd like to hear their explanations.

> **灵砂：**我想听听他们作何解释。

**Jing Yuan:** Good idea. If I were to take action, it might alert the true mastermind. I trust you to handle this matter, Miss Lingsha. The internal affairs of the Vidyadhara should remain under their jurisdiction.

> **景元：**好主意。若我出面，只怕打草惊蛇。此事就交给灵砂小姐了，持明内务仍要由持明处置。

**Jing Yuan:** And don't worry even if things don't go smoothly. Once the Wolf Hunt operation is over, the hidden truths will come to light.

> **景元：**就算出师不利也不必担心。一旦猎狼行动结束，许多事情将会真相大白。

**Lingsha:** Speaking of the Wolf Hunt operation, I'm truly worried about the Yaoqing messenger who was taken hostage. Hoolay was starving in The Shackling Prison for centuries... I don't know if the messenger can survive in that wolf's clutches.

> **灵砂：**说起猎狼，妾身很担心那位被劫走的曜青使者。呼雷在幽囚狱中许久未曾摄食、饥馁至今…也不知那位使者能否在他的狼口中安然渡劫。

**Lingsha:** May Reignbow's blessing keep him safe.

> **灵砂：**愿帝弓庇佑，保他平安无虞。

Switching to Jiaoqiu's POV...

Falling into the hands of the borisin, Jiaoqiu is trying to find a chance to survive... but it is not his own chance that he seeks.

## 024　Alone In Peril　刀俎横陈，掌中孤魂

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Exalting Sanctum　|　角色：Jiaoqiu; Mok Tok; Hoolay; Moze　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''A Web of Pasts Rewoven'' completed

### Break free from the borisin's clutches

> *Having fallen into the wolves' clutches and now at the mercy of their blades, the Yaoqing healer Jiaoqiu is trying his best to find an opportunity to stall for time and split up the borisin... Perhaps, in this battle between fox and wolf, Jiaoqiu will be able to find the secret to curing his superior or a way to escape his current predicament.*

Two dual-hours before the Wardance convenes Exalting Sanctum

**Jiaoqiu:** As I said, I'm a healer, but my art of healing requires the culinary arts to be truly effective.

> **椒丘：**我刚才已说过了，鄙人的职业是医士。只是我所擅长的医术需要佐以药膳烹饪才能发挥功效。

**Jiaoqiu:** The magic of my cauldron is its ability to make all kinds of medicines into dishes that patients can eat. When simmered and cooked together, any kind of medicinal herb or ingredient becomes a delightful delicacy.

> **椒丘：**能将万般药物烹做菜式，令患者吃下，这就是鼎镬的妙用。无论什么药材食材，稀里糊涂浸入汤中炖煮，顷刻间都成了珍馐美味了。

**Mok Tok:** Delicacy... but isn't that just one flavor overpowering the others?

> **末度：**珍馐美味…那不就是一种味道盖过了另一种味道吗？

**Jiaoqiu:** Well, you have a point... Let me ask you this: If there's a fussy child who refuses to eat green peppers, what's the best way to make him eat them?

> **椒丘：**你这么说也没错…在下考考你，如果现在有一个小儿吃饭挑食不吃青椒，你有什么办法能让这小儿吃下青椒呢？

**Mok Tok:** Stuff the kid's mouth with the green peppers and cook him alive in a pot.

> **末度：**把这小子嘴里塞上青椒，入锅煮了。

**Jiaoqiu:** Hahahaha! You have quite a sense of humor. I've heard that you borisin have lost many of your taste buds during the self-modification process, so you can't experience complex flavors. Only the "saltiness" of blood and flesh truly awakens your appetite.

> **椒丘：**哈哈哈哈，步离仁兄幽默了。我听说步离人在自我改造的过程中丧失了不少味蕾，无法品尝出复杂的滋味，只有血肉的「咸」能唤起你们的食欲。

**Mok Tok:** It's a shame I don't have any green peppers right now. Otherwise, I'd happily stuff them into your mouth and cook you alive in a pot.

> **末度：**可惜我手头没有青椒，不然真想把你嘴塞上，入锅煮了。

**Jiaoqiu:** I'm just kidding. The answer is quite simple: Chop up the peppers and mix them with minced meat to make meatballs. This way, the flavor of the meat overpowers the taste of the peppers, and even a fussy child can enjoy them.

> **椒丘：**开个玩笑，别急嘛～很简单，将青椒切碎，放入肉馅之中，做成肉丸子即可。肉的味道盖过了青椒的味道，就连挑食的小孩也能吃下青椒了。

**Mok Tok:** Can I just boil your tongue in this hot pot? I know you're trying to buy time until someone comes to your rescue.

> **末度：**这火锅能煮烂你的舌头吗？我知道你的脑袋里装着什么痴心妄想。你想拖延时间，等待救兵。

**Mok Tok:** You said that Jingliu recently returned to the Luofu, right? Well, she did come back... but unfortunately, I also found out that she committed serious crimes and was escorted to another Xianzhou ship.

> **末度：**你说「镜流回到了罗浮」，她确实回来了…可惜我也探听到了更多情报，她身犯重罪，被送往了其他仙舟。

**Mok Tok:** Do you really think you can delay our departure by provoking Lord Hoolay's desire for revenge with your clever little tongue?

> **末度：**你以为凭一点小聪明激起呼雷大人的复仇心，就能拖延我们离开的脚步？

**Mok Tok:** You see borisin as savages who know nothing about the Xianzhou, but in fact, we know all about your tracking tricks... like the jade abacus you're carrying and the cycranes in the sky.

> **末度：**别把步离人当成对仙舟一无所知的蛮子。你随身携带的玉兆，还有天上飞过的机巧鸟…仙舟人能追踪到我们的手段我全都一清二楚。

**Jiaoqiu:** ...

> **椒丘：**……

**Mok Tok:** The more hope you hold in your heart, the greater your pain will be when death comes. I wonder if you can maintain your composure when I tear your throat open.

> **末度：**心中的希望越大，死亡降临时的痛苦就越发强烈。等我撕开你喉咙的那一刻，我想看看你的表情是否还能像刚才那样镇定。

**???:** His composure is only a temporary effect of the medicine, and it will wear off soon, right?

> **？？？：**他的镇定只是一时的药效。但药效很快就要过去了，对吧？

**Mok Tok:** Lord Hoolay.

> **末度：**呼雷大人。

**Hoolay:** You pathetic foxians. For thousands of years, we were the ones who allowed you to live and who granted you civilization, but in the end, you chose to betray us for the "freedom" promised by the Xianzhou people.

> **呼雷：**可悲的狐人，数千年来，是我们允许你们苟活，是我们给了你们文明。但为了仙舟人承诺的自由，你们最终还是选择了背叛。

**Hoolay:** But it's alright. As long as you catch the scent of your masters, you obediently return to us... no matter how far you run away.

> **呼雷：**不过没关系，只要嗅到主人的气味，你们总会乖乖回到我们身边的…无论逃多远。

- ▸ (Refute him by pointing out that they are the runaways)

**Jiaoqiu:** Run away, you said? Well, I'm not the one who's trying to run away right now.

> **椒丘：**逃？现在想要逃跑的人可不是我。

- ▸ (Sow discord between Mok Tok and Hoolay)

**Jiaoqiu:** Hoolay, have you ever wondered why it took them seven centuries to come to your rescue?

> **椒丘：**呼雷，你有没有想过，为何过去整整七百年，这些家伙才想到要来营救你？

**Mok Tok:** ...

> **末度：**……

**Jiaoqiu:** The borisin's era has long passed. Did they ever tell you these harsh facts before giving you hope for returning home, Lord Hoolay?

> **椒丘：**步离人不可一世的时代早就过去了。在给你回家的希望之前，他们有没有把这些可悲的事实告诉你，呼雷大人？

**Jiaoqiu:** Now they're being crushed by the Merlin's Claw of the Yaoqing... by a foxian. They're hiding in remote corners of the cosmos, trembling with fear, like a wounded beast on its last legs, reaching out desperately for any fake hope of salvation, which in this case is you.
