*(If Jiaoqiu sought help with only one person)*

**Mok Tok:** Come and take a good look. Who is this person?

> **末度：**你来瞧瞧，这人是谁？

(If the person is the starskiff pilot)

**Starskiff Pilot:** This Cloud Knight says there's official business that requires my cooperation.

> **计程槎驾驶员：**这位云骑大哥说，有公务需要我配合。

(If the person is the Cloud Knights soldier)

**Cloud Knights Soldier:** Don't worry, this captain will help us.

> **云骑士兵：**别担心，这位长官会帮我们的。

(If the person is the Realm-Keeping orderly)

**Realm-Keeping Orderly:** This Cloud Knight will help you.

> **地衡司勤务：**这位云骑大哥会帮你的。

- ▸ What are you trying to do, Mok Tok?

**Jiaoqiu:** What are you trying to do, Mok Tok? They have nothing to do with this!

> **椒丘：**末度，你想做什么？这一切和他们无关！

**Hoolay:** Nothing to do? I told you to go to the port and check out the situation... but I never gave you permission to talk with others.

**Hoolay:** Remember, this person died because of you.

> **呼雷：**无关？我只是让你去港口看看情况…可从来没有允许你三心二意和人交谈啊。

A sickening crack of bones echoed through the small courtyard. There was no scream, not even the sound of a struggle.

**Jiaoqiu:** You want to prove to me that... you hold everyone's lives in your hands, right, Hoolay!?

*(If Jiaoqiu sought help with two or more people)*

**Mok Tok:** Come and take a good look. Who are these people?

**Cloud Knights Soldier:** Don't worry, this captain will help us.

- ▸ What are you trying to do, Mok Tok?

**Jiaoqiu:** What are you trying to do, Mok Tok? They have nothing to do with this!

**Hoolay:** Nothing to do? I told you to go to the port and check out the situation... but I never gave you permission to talk with others.

**Hoolay:** Remember, these people died because of you.

> **呼雷：**记住，这个人是因你而死的。

A sickening crack of bones echoed through the small courtyard. There was no scream, not even the sound of a struggle.

**Jiaoqiu:** You want to prove to me that... you hold everyone's lives in your hands, right, Hoolay!?

> **椒丘：**你要向我证明…这里所有人的性命统统握在你手里，对吧，呼雷？！

**Hoolay:** Exactly as you said. So, have the Cloud Knights closed the ports?

> **呼雷：**你说的不错。那么，你等来了云骑封锁港口吗？

**Jiaoqiu:** ...No.

> **椒丘：**…没有。

**Hoolay:** They don't want to publicize my escape. I understand. It's no surprise, for fear is more deadly than claws and fangs... especially during a festive time like the Wardance.

> **呼雷：**看起来，他们并不希望将我的逃离公之于众。这不意外，恐惧比爪牙更致命…尤其是在「演武仪典」这样的节庆时刻。

**Hoolay:** What lurks here is not a group of fugitives, but rather a pack of wolves amidst the flock. My cubs are hungry, ready to feast on flesh and blood, reveling in your fear.

> **呼雷：**此刻在这里的不是一群东躲西藏的囚犯，而是走进了羊群的狼。我的狼崽子们正饥肠辘辘，他们渴望吞饮血肉，用你的恐惧来佐餐。

**Hoolay:** Your calm facade will soon crumble. I can tear through it and expose your pathetic bones anytime. There's no escape from me, Jiaoqiu.

> **呼雷：**椒丘，你强装镇定的外壳在我看来不堪一击，就像我随时能撕开你的皮肉，露出底下可悲的白骨。在我面前，你无所遁逃。

**Hoolay:** Of course, you can try your luck and believe that your wits will save you. But remember, you're not the only hostage in this city. If you try anything funny, innocent people will die because of you.

> **呼雷：**你当然可以心存侥幸，以为靠自己的急智能摆脱眼下的状况。但记住，身处闹市，我们不只有你一个人质。你的任何异动，都会让无辜的人因你而死。

**Hoolay:** Now, let's talk about the foxian general you serve. Mok Tok says she's here for me, which means she'll be hunting me herself.

> **呼雷：**现在，我们来谈谈你侍奉的那位曜青狐人将军。末度说她为我而来，也就意味着她会亲自出马追捕我。

**Hoolay:** Before the hunt begins, I need to know my opponent. You can refuse and play tough, or you can cooperate and save us both some time, healer.

> **呼雷：**在狩猎开始前，我要了解我的对手。你可以拒绝，向我展示你的骨气；也可以合作一些，为我们双方节省时间，医士。

- ▸ (Show weakness, making him believe you've surrendered)
- ▸ (Refuse, letting him know you won't talk)

The tall man stretches out a finger and touches your shoulder blade with a movement so fast it's not visible.

It's as if a dagger has cut away a piece of flesh, and the pain pierces down to the bone. You can barely stand.

**Jiaoqiu:** Agh...

> **椒丘：**呜……

**Hoolay:** Good, at least you didn't try to draw attention with a scream. That way, no one will die in vain.

> **呼雷：**很好，你没有蠢到用惨叫吸引别人的注意。这样也不会有人白白送死。

*(If the player refused)*

**Hoolay:** But remember, the next time you refuse me, I'll crush your healing hands, then your knees, then your spine... I'll dismantle you piece by piece until only your silver tongue remains for you to speak.

> **呼雷：**下一次拒绝，我会捏碎你治病救人的双手，之后是髌骨，再然后是脊骨…我会把你一寸一寸敲零割碎，只留下那条三寸不烂之舌，留到你打算开口为止。

**Jiaoqiu:** ...Fine, I'll tell you what you want to know about her, but I expect something in return.

> **椒丘：**…我可以告诉你关于她的一切，但是，要用一个答案换一个答案。

**Hoolay:** What makes you think you're still in a position to negotiate with me?

> **呼雷：**是什么错觉让你以为，你还有资格和我谈交易？

**Jiaoqiu:** You can torture me until I talk, or... let's save us both some time, Warhead.

> **椒丘：**你可以用酷刑来折磨我，直到我开口；也可以…为我们双方节省些时间，战首。

**Jiaoqiu:** Here's the question that's been bothering me: How have you managed to survive and endure centuries of torture? Borisin aren't supposed to live that long or possess such regenerative abilities.

> **椒丘：**有个问题始终困扰着我。为什么受刑七百年，你依旧还能安然无恙地活着？步离人不该有如此长久的生命…也不可能有如此顽强的复原能力。

**Hoolay:** So, is that why the Xianzhou Yaoqing wanted to take me away? To some, I'm a hostage, but to others, I hold the secrets they desire...

> **呼雷：**这就是曜青仙舟想要带走我的原因？对某些人而言，我可以是握在手上的人质。但对有些人而言，他们想要的是我身上的秘密——

**Hoolay:** I still remember, in the early years of my captivity, the foxians came and went... They extracted my blood and marrow, trying to unravel the mystery of Moon Rage. They wanted to conquer their fear of the borisin and free themselves from their bloodline.

> **呼雷：**我依旧还记得，在我被关押之初的那些岁月里，狐人们来了又去…他们从我身上抽取血髓，想破解「月狂」的成因，摆脱对步离人的恐惧，从血脉的根柢上翻身做主。

**Hoolay:** Unfortunately, they failed to uncover the secret, so they resorted to the most brutal punishments they could imagine.

> **呼雷：**可惜，他们无法参透这秘密，只能对我施加自己所能想到的最恶毒的刑罚。

**Hoolay:** Some seek power to destroy their enemies, while others seek power to become their enemies... Which one are you, Jiaoqiu?

> **呼雷：**有些人追求力量是为毁灭他的仇敌，有些人追求力量却是为了变成他的仇敌…椒丘，你是哪一种？

**Hoolay:** Ah, I see! You're the most pitiful of them all, healer of the Yaoqing! You seek power to share it with others and do good.

> **呼雷：**啊，我明白了！曜青的医士，你是最可悲的那一种人——你追求力量，是为了与别人分享它，用它来行善。

**Hoolay:** Well, then let me tell you the answer you seek.

> **呼雷：**那就让我来告诉你，你想知道的答案。

**Hoolay:** In our ancient legends, Duran, the ancestor of the borisin, was dissatisfied with his limited lifespan and power. He yearned to dominate the skies and become the master of all the stars.

> **呼雷：**在古老的传说里，步离人的始祖都蓝不满于有限的生命和力量，他渴望主宰天空，成为群星的主人。

**Hoolay:** To achieve his goal, he sacrificed the lives of countless borisin and foxians, pouring them into the spring bestowed by the Master of Immortality. Through a form of genetic sorcery, the water gave birth to a miracle — the Lunar Embryo.

> **呼雷：**为此他牺牲了无数步离人与狐人的生命，注入长生主恩赐的泉水，在基因巫术的催动下，水中孕育了一个奇迹——「胎动之月」。

**Hoolay:** Duran ascended the birthing bed and obtained what he desired: a heart in the shape of a deep-red full moon. He cut open his chest and replaced his original heart with this crimson moon.

> **呼雷：**攀上「月亮」的产床后，都蓝从中获得了自己梦寐以求的东西，一轮形如赤红满月的「心脏」。都蓝切开自己的胸膛，用这轮赤月替换了自己的心。

- ▸ How typical. Just another borisin myth...

**Hoolay:** Laugh all you want, but only I know the truth behind every word.

> **呼雷：**你尽可以嘲笑它野蛮疯狂。但只有我知道，它真实不虚。

- ▸ I'm not buying your absurd myth!

**Hoolay:** The Ambrosial Arbor is also an absurd myth, but you Xianzhou folk know it's true.

> **呼雷：**建木也是荒诞不经的神话。但你们仙舟人明白，它真实不虚。

**Hoolay:** This moon heart beats within the body of every generation of the Borisin Warhead. In the ritual that decides the new Warhead, the successor cuts open the previous Warhead's chest and consumes the divine heart, thus possessing it as their own!

> **呼雷：**这颗心月世世代代跳动在步离战首们的身体里。因为步离人决出战首的仪式，正是由继任之人剖开前任战首的胸膛，吃下这神肉，让强者拥有它！

**Hoolay:** Devouring is the true essence of life, allowing us to endure and thrive. It gathers the life essence of all the prey we consume, making us stronger and stronger!

> **呼雷：**「吞噬」，这是生命得以延续、茁壮的真谛。它凝聚着被我们吞噬的猎物的生命精华，也让我们变得越发强壮！

**Hoolay:** I thought all hope was lost after seven centuries of torment, but now, this heart is beating once again.

> **呼雷：**受刑七百年，我曾以为一切毫无希望了，但如今，这轮心脏再度跳动起来了。

**Hoolay:** Alright, now it's your turn to tell me... everything about the Merlin's Claw.

> **呼雷：**好了，轮到你告诉我…这位「天击将军」的一切了。

*(Moze covertly watches the situation unfold from a rooftop)*

**Moze:** Jiaoqiu... I've found you.

> **貊泽：**椒丘…我找到你了。

Returning to the Trailblazer's POV...

When you have a chance to make a choice, make one that you know you won't regret.

## 025　The Red Warcry　围三阙一，赤血传檄

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Stargazer Navalia　|　角色：Trailblazer; Feixiao; Yanqing; Moze; Artisanship Commission Craftsman; Cloud Knights Soldier; Mild-Mannered Staff; Agitated Employee; Hoolay; Mok Tok　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''Alone In Peril'' completed

### Head toward Stargazer Navalia and wait for the rest of the borisin

> *Following Feixiao's advice, everybody abandons the large-scale hunt and begins to speculate about their prey's motives and agenda instead. As for Stargazer Navalia, the site where the wolves were first spotted, it shall become the hunting ground for the continued pursuit of the borisin.*

**Yanqing:** I know I'm in no position to question your orders, but if I understand correctly, you already have intelligence about the borisin?

> **彦卿：**将军，虽然我不该质疑您的指示。但如果我没听错，你说你已经获得了关于步离人的一手情报？

**Feixiao:** That's right. My people are busy collecting clues now. What's the situation, Moze?

> **飞霄：**不错。我的部下正在时刻寻找线索。貊泽，目前情况如何。

**Moze:** General, before we completely lost contact with Jiaoqiu, I picked up a signal from his jade abacus. It won't take long before we locate their whereabouts.

> **貊泽：**将军，在椒丘彻底失联前，我捕捉了他身上的玉兆信号，要不了多久应该能查到他们的落脚处。

**Yanqing:** If that's the case, shouldn't we immediately go there and join forces to eliminate the borisin? Why are we returning to Stargazer Navalia?

> **彦卿：**既然这样，咱们难道不该立刻与他汇合，和他一同铲除步离人。怎么又跑到迴星港来了？

**Feixiao:** Looks like your general has only taught you swordplay, not the art of The Hunt.

> **飞霄：**看来你家将军只教了你剑术，却没教你如何「巡猎」。

**Feixiao:** There's an old saying among the borisin: "In the forest, the hunter may easily become the hunted." While we may call ourselves the hunters now, chasing them blindly will only make us become the prey.

> **飞霄：**步离人有句古谚「在森林里，猎人与猎物的身份随时改变」。眼下我们自居猎人，但盲目追着他们跑，到头来我们反倒会成为呼雷的猎物。

- ▸ How can Hoolay... possibly hunt us down?

> ▸ 这家伙…能怎么狩猎我们？

- ▸ But we have the advantage in numbers, right?

> ▸ 呼雷人数可不占优啊？

**Yanqing:** Are you joking, General? He's just a wolf who's been imprisoned for seven centuries. Even with his accomplices, they're no match for us numerous Luofu Cloud Knights. How could he consider us his prey?

> **彦卿：**将军莫非是在和彦卿说笑？一头被关押了七百年的恶狼，就算有党羽协助，相较于罗浮云骑的数量也微不足道，怎么可能把我们当成猎物？

**Feixiao:** Do not underestimate the enemy. Hoolay is not an ordinary borisin that can be easily killed. The advantage in numbers means nothing when we face that monster.

> **飞霄：**两位，收起轻敌之心。呼雷绝不是能被你们轻易斩杀的步离人。人数多寡对这怪物而言毫无意义。

**Feixiao:** A vicious beast, who hasn't tasted blood and flesh in seven centuries, is now lurking on the streets of the Luofu right before the Wardance. This is what makes the situation treacherous and unpredictable.

> **飞霄：**七百年未曾噬人的恶兽，在演武仪典召开前潜入了罗浮街巷，这才是如今的局势最凶险难测的地方。

**Yanqing:** To him, the lives of ordinary people are just meat, ready to be devoured at any moment...

> **彦卿：**寻常百姓的人命，就像随时可能被这家伙一口吞下的肉……

**Feixiao:** Exactly. That's why we must succeed in our first strike, or the situation will spiral out of control.

> **飞霄：**没错，为此，我们必须一击得手，不然，局势将会变得不可收拾。

**Feixiao:** You've heard of this military tactic, right? When you surround your enemy, leave one side open for them to escape. A skilled hunter must be well-prepared, waiting for the prey at the most advantageous position.

> **飞霄：**你们应该听说过兵法中「围三阙一，网开一面」的战术吧？任猎物疯狂逃窜可不行。好猎人要做足准备，在最合适的位置等待猎物上门。

- ▸ Where do those borisin want to go?

> ▸ 步离人想去哪儿呢？

- ▸ Is Stargazer Navalia the most likely place for them to come to?

> ▸ 迴星港就是他们最可能来的地方？

**Feixiao:** What would we do if we were the escapees from The Shackling Prison? While we can disguise ourselves and blend in with the crowd, we don't plan on staying here for long. In this case, what do we need most now?

> **飞霄：**想象一下，咱们是刚逃离幽囚狱的犯人。尽管能伪装变化，混入人群，但我并不打算在此久留，眼下最需要的是什么？

**Yanqing:** ...A starskiff to get us out of the Luofu. The borisin we discovered at Stargazer Navalia were actually preparing a vessel for their escape.

> **彦卿：**…载我们逃离罗浮的星槎。我在迴星港发现的步离人正是在为他们准备逃跑的船只。

**Feixiao:** We predict their next move, and we take them down.

> **飞霄：**猜测猎物可能的去向，接着就该慢慢收网了。

**Feixiao:** Let's start from where you found those borisin and corner our prey.

> **飞霄：**来吧，从你发现步离人踪迹的地方开始，我们让猎物无处可逃。

### Arrive at Stargazer Navalia's shipyard and survey the area

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Yanqing:** This is where we ran into the borisin last time. It looks the same as usual.

> **彦卿：**上次我们跟踪步离人的位置就在这里，看上去跟平时一样。

**Feixiao:** Because I blocked all the relevant news, to make it seem as if nothing had happened.

> **飞霄：**在那之后，我立即命人封锁了消息，并尽量使一切维持原状。

- ▸ Will the borisin notice something?

> ▸ 步离人会不会察觉到了？

**Feixiao:** Losing contact with their accomplices preparing the starskiff will surely put them on guard.

> **飞霄：**准备星槎的同伙没有回音，这应该会让他们有所警惕。

- ▸ Yanqing may have alerted the borisin?

> ▸ 彦卿会不会打草惊蛇了？

**Feixiao:** If Yanqing hadn't scared them off, we would not have many clues.

> **飞霄：**倘若彦卿没有「打草惊蛇」，眼前恐怕还毫无头绪呢。

**Feixiao:** However, the more well-prepared a plan is, the longer it takes to adjust when it's disrupted. If any borisin wants to stick to the original plan despite the risks, they'll surely come back to Stargazer Navalia to check the situation.

> **飞霄：**不过，越是准备周详的计划，一旦被打乱，作出反应的时间也就越长。步离人中如果还有想赌一把，坚持原计划出逃的，一定会回到这里确认迴星港的情况。

**Yanqing:** Are you suggesting that there are borisin here at Stargazer Navalia, right now?

> **彦卿：**你是说迴星港里此刻就有步离人？

**Feixiao:** This is an automated area, so don't you think that there are a bit too many people here today?

> **飞霄：**你难道没有发觉，这片自动运行的区域里，今日的人有些多的不太寻常？

**Feixiao:** First, look at those two Sky-Faring Commission staff members. They've been observing us in secret since we arrived here.

> **飞霄：**首先是那两个天舶司职员。打从我们来到这里，他们就在悄悄观察我们。

**Feixiao:** Then, there's a Cloud Knight soldier over there. Perhaps he's here for patrol, but each patrol team must consist of two members, and obviously, he's not following the rules.

> **飞霄：**接下来是那边的云骑士兵，如果是来巡逻的，那就是没有遵守两人一组的执勤规制。

**Feixiao:** Lastly, there's that craftsman wearing Artisanship Commission clothing. He's unusually focused on checking that device.

> **飞霄：**最后是那个穿着工造司服色的匠人，煞有介事地对着装置看个不停。

**Feixiao:** If you have a target in mind, let's go investigate.

> **飞霄：**想好了目标的话，咱们就去探探底。

### Question suspicious persons

*(Talk to the mild-mannered staff)*

**Agitated Employee:** Who are you? Don't you know that Stargazer Navalia is on lockdown now?

> **急躁的职员：**你们是什么人，不知道迴星港已经被封锁了吗？

**Mild-Mannered Staff:** Hey, chill out. They're just a few lost tourists.

> **温和的职员：**唉，几个不请误入的游客嘛，咱们好好说话。

- ▸ Stargazer Navalia is on lockdown now?

> ▸ 迴星港被封锁了？

**Yanqing:** ...But Stargazer Navalia is the shipbuilding port of the Xianzhou Luofu. If the Sky-Faring Commission called for a lockdown, there should be official documents proving it, right?

> **彦卿：**…迴星港是罗浮仙舟的造船港，如果天舶司要封锁港口，肯定是有公文通知吧？

**Agitated Employee:** Official documents? Of course, there are. The Cloud Knights are aware that some people have infiltrated this port.

> **急躁的职员：**公文？当然有了。云骑军得知有人潜入迴星港。

**Yanqing:** ...That makes perfect sense. So, you're now investigating the borisin?

> **彦卿：**…哦，合情合理，所以你们现在是排查步离人？

**Mild-Mannered Staff:** Yeah, we've received orders from the higher-ups, so we've blocked off this area to prevent any interference with our inspection. Thank you for your understanding.

*(Talk to the mild-mannered staff again, optional)*

**Mild-Mannered Staff:** Yeah, we've received orders from the higher-ups, so we've blocked off this area to prevent any interference with our inspection. Thank you for your understanding.

> **温和的职员：**我们收到了上级指示，为了避免无关人等干扰云骑检查，我们特地将这里封锁了起来，请你们理解我们的工作。

*(Talk to the Artisanship Commision craftsman)*

**Artisanship Commission Craftsman:** Hey, you there! Please refrain from wandering around Stargazer Navalia if you're not... Oh, it's you, Lieutenant Yanqing.

- ▸ Is Stargazer Navalia on lockdown now?

> ▸ （质疑他所处的位置）

**Artisanship Commission Craftsman:** No, not at all... Actually, I'm here to address a peculiar issue.

- ▸ Aren't you wandering around here as well?

> ▸ （询问竞锋舰的情况）

**Artisanship Commission Craftsman:** I'm an official staff member assigned to investigate a peculiar issue.

**Feixiao:** A peculiar issue?

**Artisanship Commission Craftsman:** Yeah, we've had some unscheduled starskiffs being constructed at this dock in Stargazer Navalia. Could there have been an error in the production planning for the assembly line?

**Artisanship Commission Craftsman:** This is just weird. I thought all the pending starskiffs from before the Wardance had been scheduled already.

**Artisanship Commission Craftsman:** I'm sorry, but I need to focus on my work right now. If you have any questions, we can discuss them later.

*(Talk to the Artisanship Commision craftsman again, optional)*

**Artisanship Commission Craftsman:** I'm sorry, but I need to focus on my work right now. If you have any questions, we can discuss them later.

*(Talk to the soldier on duty)*

**Cloud Knights Soldier:** Ahh... Ah! Lieutenant Yanqing! And General Feixiao?

> **云骑士兵：**唉…啊！彦卿骁卫！还有飞霄将军？

**Yanqing:** You're aware of the borisin prison break, right?

> **彦卿：**云骑大哥，你知道步离人越狱的事情了吧？

**Cloud Knights Soldier:** Yeah, I've received the news, and I'll be on the alert!

> **云骑士兵：**接到消息了，我会时刻保持警惕！

**Feixiao:** Looks like you've been keeping an eye on the Skysplitter. Have you noticed anything unusual?

> **飞霄：**根据我的观察，你一直在盯着竞锋舰的方向，所以你发现了什么状况吗？

**Cloud Knights Soldier:** No, everything is normal on the ship. Nothing out of the ordinary!

> **云骑士兵：**竞锋舰一切如常，没有特别的状况！

- ▸ (Question his location)

> ▸ （结束问话）

**(Trailblazer):** This is not a location for a Cloud Knight on guard duty, is it?

> **开拓者：**这地方可不是云骑执勤的位置吧？

**Yanqing:** Well, this spot offers a great view. Are you here to keep an eye on the Skysplitter?

> **彦卿：**哦，这个地方视野不错，你是来这里观察竞锋舰的吧？

**Cloud Knights Soldier:** Yes...

> **云骑士兵：**是……

- ▸ (Ask about the Skysplitter)

> ▸ 难道迴星港被封锁了？

**(Trailblazer):** You've been observing the Skysplitter for a while. Have you noticed anything?

> **开拓者：**你看了这么久的竞锋舰，发现了什么吗？

**Cloud Knights Soldier:** ...Honestly, just watching the ship from here isn't too thrilling. I wish I could go on board and witness Lieutenant Yanqing's contests...

> **云骑士兵：**…只是在这里看着舰船的飞行轨迹确实没什么意思，要是能上船，我最想看的还是彦卿大人的守擂比赛……

**Cloud Knights Soldier:** Speaking of which, Lieutenant Yanqing, aren't you supposed to be on the Skysplitter? Is it alright for you to be here instead?

> **云骑士兵：**说到这个，彦卿大人，您不是应该在竞锋舰上吗？现在不回到竞锋舰上没关系吗？

**Yanqing:** Thank you for your concern, but I have more pressing matters to attend to.

> **彦卿：**谢谢你的支持，我有更重要的事情要去做。

**Cloud Knights Soldier:** Ah...

> **云骑士兵：**啊……

- ▸ (End the questioning)

> ▸ 你们不也在这儿乱逛吗？

*(Talk to the soldier on duty again, optional)*

**Feixiao:** You're on duty, soldier. Stay focused!

> **飞霄：**士兵，你有任务在身，给我集中精神！

**Cloud Knights Soldier:** Yes, ma'am!

> **云骑士兵：**遵命！

### Consolidate intel and review the suspicious points

**Feixiao:** So, it seems that some borisin have returned to Stargazer Navalia in disguise. The answer seems quite obvious...

> **飞霄：**有一伙步离人假扮身份重新回到了迴星港，答案已经显而易见了——

- ▸ The staff members from the Sky-Faring Commission.

> ▸ 应该是天舶司的职员。

**Yanqing:** That's my suspicion as well.

> **彦卿：**我也这么认为。

- ▸ The Cloud Knight soldier.

> ▸ 应该是可疑的云骑士兵。

**Yanqing:** I believe he was just slacking off. In my opinion, the staff members from the Sky-Faring Commission appear more suspicious.

> **彦卿：**那个士兵应该只是有些走神，我认为最可疑的应该是天舶司职员。

- ▸ The craftsman from the Artisanship Commission.

> ▸ 应该是工造司匠人。

**Yanqing:** I think he's genuinely here to investigate the starskiffs, as he claimed. In my opinion, the staff members from the Sky-Faring Commission are the most suspicious.

> **彦卿：**我看这个人反而是为排查星槎而来的。最可疑的应该是天舶司职员。

**Feixiao:** Remember when I mentioned that the Cloud Knights blocked all relevant news, to make it seem as if nothing happened? Stargazer Navalia is not under lockdown.

> **飞霄：**你还记得我说过云骑军封锁了消息，并尽量使一切维持原状吗？这意味着，他们不应该封锁迴星港。

**Yanqing:** But on the contrary, those staff members used "Stargazer Navalia is on lockdown" as an excuse to persuade us to leave. This contradicts the Cloud Knights' plans.

> **彦卿：**而那两位职员却以「迴星港封锁」为理由劝我们离开，这与云骑军原定的计划相悖。

**Feixiao:** Their flimsy disguise has been exposed. Even if they try to hide, there's no way they can escape us.

> **飞霄：**拙劣的伪装到此为止了，就算他们想要隐瞒也不可能逃脱。

### Question the suspicious Sky-Faring Commission staff member

*(Talk to the mild-mannered staff)*

**Mild-Mannered Staff:** Why are you still here? As I said, Stargazer Navalia is on lockdown now.

> **温和的职员：**你们几位怎么还在这儿？我刚才说了，迴星港已经被封锁了。

**Feixiao:** No, I never issued such an order.

> **飞霄：**我可从未下过这样的命令。

**Mild-Mannered Staff:** Who are you?

> **温和的职员：**你是谁？

**Feixiao:** Perhaps you haven't met me on the battlefield, but I'm certain you've heard my name. Now, I'm asking you a question, so answer me! Tell me...

> **飞霄：**你们也许从未在战场上见过我，但一定听过我的名字。不过是谁准许你用问题来回答问题了！告诉我——

**Feixiao:** How many more of your comrades are here? Where were you planning to meet Hoolay?

> **飞霄：**你们还有多少同伙？原定计划打算在何处接应呼雷？

**Agitated Employee:** This woman... She's the general of the Yaoqing! What are you still waiting for!? Attack her!

> **急躁的职员：**那个女人…是曜青的将军！你在等什么，动手！

*(Begin battle against Eclipse Wolftrooper ×2, Sableclaw Wolftrooper ×2)*

### Defeat the disguised Borisin Wolftroopers

*(After all enemies are defeated)*

**Borisin Wolftrooper:** Run! Tell Lord Mok Tok about this!

> **步离狼卒：**快撤！将消息带给末度大人！

*(After the battle)*

**Wolftrooper:** Why don't you kill me?

> **狼卒：**为什么不杀了我？

**Feixiao:** Because I need answers.

> **飞霄：**我还有些问题没有得到回答。

**Wolftrooper:** You were our war slave, so you should know us descendants of Duran...

> **狼卒：**曾经身为战奴的你，应该很熟悉我们这些都蓝子裔……

**Wolftrooper:** You want me to talk? Fine, try whatever you want, but I assure you, you won't get anything from me... only fangs and blood.

> **狼卒：**尽管试试吧，尽管用你知道的那些手段逼我开口。我能回答你的…永远只有鲜血和爪牙。

**Feixiao:** If I were to do that, I wouldn't be any different from you.

> **飞霄：**如果那样做，我和你们也就别无二致了。

**Feixiao:** I am the arrow that pierces the wolf's heart. I grant you a swift death.

> **飞霄：**我是刺入狼心的锋镝，我赐你速死。

**Wolftrooper:** ...That's exactly what I desire. Wolves that leave the pack are prepared for the fate of never returning home.

> **狼卒：**…这正合我意。离群的狼，早就做好了无法返回故乡的准备。

**Wolftrooper:** But sadly, you didn't catch my partner... He will alert them...

> **狼卒：**可惜，你没能抓住我的伙伴…他会向伙伴们示警——

**Feixiao:** Before you close your eyes, you should know that I allowed him to escape, because he will lead the Cloud Knights to Hoolay's hideout.

> **飞霄：**在你阖眼之前，你应该知道，是我允许他逃走。你的伙伴会带着云骑找到呼雷的下落。

**Wolftrooper:** Arrrghhh...

> **狼卒：**啊啊啊——

**Yanqing:** Is this how you... "leave one side open"...? You let that borisin escape so you could track him.

> **彦卿：**这就是「网开一面」么…？放任步离人逃走，然后顺着他的路线追猎。

**Feixiao:** This is the most common hunting tactic used by borisin.

> **飞霄：**这是步离人最常用的狩猎战术。

**Feixiao:** I know the methods of these abominations all too well. They always leave an escape route for their prey, allowing them to flee in fear. Then they chase them like it's a game. Borisin get great satisfaction from the last struggles of their victims...

> **飞霄：**我太了解这些孽物的手段了。他们总会留下逃生的道路，任由猎物仓惶离开。随后便是近乎戏耍般的逐杀追猎，在牺牲者们的惨状中，步离人总会获得莫大的满足……

**Yanqing:** Have you witnessed these things firsthand? That guy called you a... war slave... What exactly have you experienced?

> **彦卿：**这些都是您亲眼见证的事情吗？那个家伙把你称为…「战奴」，所以您到底经历了什么啊？

**Feixiao:** Well, just a hazy past that I can barely recollect. Long ago... I was one of them.

> **飞霄：**一段模糊的，几乎快记不清的过去罢了。在许久以前，我…曾是行走在他们中的一员。

- ▸ Wait, you're a borisin!?

> ▸ 你是步离人？！

**Feixiao:** The word "borisin" was only used to refer to those wolf masters. I'm a foxian who was born in one of the "fallen territories"...

> **飞霄：**步离人这三个字，只能用来称呼那些狼头恩主。我是一个出生在「沦陷地」的狐人……

**Feixiao:** In their eyes, we're mere consumables, cannon fodder used to slow down Xianzhou attacks on the battlefield.

> **飞霄：**在狼群眼中，我们只是流淌着狐血的耗材，是战场冲锋时用来拖住仙舟攻势的炮灰。

- ▸ But you're a foxian, right?

> ▸ 可你是个狐人？

**Feixiao:** Not all foxians are lucky enough to be born on a Xianzhou ship... I grew up in one of the worlds where borisin roam, known as the "fallen territories" by the people of the Yaoqing.

> **飞霄：**不是所有狐人都能有幸出生在仙舟疆域中的…我成长在步离人所放牧的世界。曜青称那些地方为「沦陷地」。

**Feixiao:** To the wolf masters, foxians are nothing but property, cannon fodder used to slow down the Xianzhou attacks on the battlefield.

> **飞霄：**对狼头恩主们而言，狐人只是他们的财产…是战场冲锋时用来拖住仙舟攻势的炮灰。

**Yanqing:** But you... survived. And not only that, you became the general of the Yaoqing! If I get the chance, I'd love to hear stories of your past, General.

> **彦卿：**但将军…活下来了，不但如此，还成为了曜青的将军！如果有机会，我很想听听将军过去的故事。

**Feixiao:** Speaking of stories, you've reminded me that now is not the time for them. Moze, we've sent the "warning" from Stargazer Navalia. What's the situation on your end?

> **飞霄：**说到这个，你可提醒我了，眼下不是讲故事的时候。貊泽，迴星港这边已经放出了「警告」，你这边情况如何？

**Moze:** I've found them. Jiaoqiu is trying to stall Hoolay, and he signaled me... to not reveal myself.

> **貊泽：**我找到了他们的位置，椒丘正在与呼雷周旋，他…示意我不要露面。

**Feixiao:** Trust his judgment, and keep watching. We'll be right there.

> **飞霄：**相信椒丘的判断，继续监视，我们马上就来。

**Yanqing:** I believe the "warning" we sent will stop Hoolay from attacking Stargazer Navalia. What will he do next?

> **彦卿：**放走诱饵，迴星港的示警一定会断去呼雷袭击此处的可能。接着他又该如何应对呢？

**Feixiao:** The hunt is not over yet. Stay safe, Jiaoqiu...

> **飞霄：**狩猎还没结束。椒丘，你可一定要平安无事啊……

### Witness Hoolay's ravings

> *Vowing to bring the wolves' way of survival back to his tribespeople, Hoolay declares that he shall be a wolf walking among sheep, and his blood and his scions shall teach the Xianzhou people the meaning of fear.*

One dual-hour before the Wardance convenes Exalting Sanctum

Patient's Voice: Please... save... me...

Patient's Voice: I want to live... Doctor...

Healer's Voice: Jiaoqiu! Find a medical ingenium and give that kid a shot! Hurry!

**Jiaoqiu:** I... I see. How is the front line?

> **椒丘：**我…我明白了，前线怎么样了？

Cloud Knight's Voice: The borisin beast ships have already landed on the Fanghu... It won't be long before this place is overrun as well.

**Jiaoqiu:** What about General Yueyu... Any news from her? I'm her healer. I should stay by her side at a time like this.

> **椒丘：**月御将军呢…她有消息吗？我是她的医士，这时候我应该留在她的身边。

Cloud Knight's Voice: She asked me to tell you that she's not coming back. She must protect the Cloudpeer Telescope.

Cloud Knight's Voice: General asks you to... to save that kid. The girl fought desperately just to bring us all back here alive!

Cloud Knight's Voice: I've never seen such a brutal fighting style... Her body... it's like she was split open... just like...

Healer's Voice: Her blood pressure is dropping. Do you hear me, Jiaoqiu?

**Jiaoqiu:** Loud and clear... Get me some Tumbledust! We've got to start the operation right now!

> **椒丘：**我听得很清楚…准备一支「颠踬散」。我们必须现在开始手术！

**Jiaoqiu:** I... I will bring her back!

> **椒丘：**我…一定会把她带回来的！

**Hoolay:** ...Is that why you're so determined to learn my secrets? Do you hear me, Jiaoqiu?

> **呼雷：**…这就是你一心想从我身上破解秘密的原因？你能听见我说话吗，椒丘？

**Jiaoqiu:** I do. Loud and clear.

> **椒丘：**我听见了，我听得很清楚。

**Hoolay:** So, she saved you all in that great battle on the Xianzhou Fanghu three decades ago, but to your surprise, you discovered her borisin bloodline while she was at death's door.

> **呼雷：**在三十年前方壶仙舟的那场大战中，她拯救了你们所有人，但却在濒死的时刻意外察觉到了体内流淌着步离血脉。

**Hoolay:** Mok Tok told me that she was a war slave who escaped from the Eclipse Pack. What a twist of fate. Turns out she's from the same clan as I am.

> **呼雷：**末度告诉我，她是从蚀月猎群里逃离的战奴。何等奇妙的因果，她竟和我同出于一个部落。

**Mok Tok:** Now I understand. No wonder she displayed... such astounding power, determination, and cruelty in battle...! Turns out it's all because of her borisin bloodline. Mutt... despicable mutt.

> **末度：**原来如此，难怪那个狐人战斗时所展现的力量，果断和残忍…如此惊人！那全是拜她的步离血脉所赐。杂种…可憎的杂种将军。

**Hoolay:** And she used the gift of her bloodline to destroy the borisin.

> **呼雷：**而她用自己血脉中的馈赠摧毁了步离人。

**Hoolay:** Moon Rage, a blessing for wolves, and a curse for foxes. For borisin, to have their bodies torn apart by Moon Rage and transform into a beast... is the ultimate joy, but for you foxians with poor regenerative abilities, it means certain death.

> **呼雷：**「月狂」，狼之赐福，狐之诅咒。对于步离人来说，在战斗中被月狂撕裂身体，兽化变形…是为无上喜乐。但对你们这些自愈力有限的狐人来说，它是死路一条。

**Hoolay:** With the burning fury in her heart, that foxian general will eventually lose her sanity and indulge in endless bloodshed. The scars on her body will not be caused by enemy weapons, but by the immense power she can't withstand. One day, she will be torn apart and die as a monster.

> **呼雷：**伴随涌上心头的燃烧怒血，这位狐人将军敌我不分，鏖战不休，身躯上绽开的伤痕不是来自敌人的武器，而是她不能承受的巨大力量。终有一日，她将作为怪物，四分五裂地死去。

**Hoolay:** And in return for her saving your life, you intend to give everything to solve this impossible puzzle.

> **呼雷：**而为了回报她的救命之恩，你打算倾尽所能，去挑战这个无法解开的谜题。

**Jiaoqiu:** Hoolay... do you know the saddest thing about being a healer?

> **椒丘：**呼雷…你知道身为医士，最可悲的事情是什么吗？

**Jiaoqiu:** All this time, I've devoted my life to bringing back those who sacrificed their lives to monsters like you. I exhausted myself, and my hands trembled, but I believed everything I did was meaningful...

> **椒丘：**一直以来，我费尽毕生所学，想从你这样的怪物手中夺回那些赴死的生命。我精疲力尽地瘫坐下来、双手颤抖，我在想我所做的一切都是有意义的……

**Jiaoqiu:** But once again, they rushed into battle, and then I heard of their deaths. They died under your claws, in your jaws, amidst the flames of crashing starskiffs, and under the Lux Arrow of Reignbow...

> **椒丘：**可他们又再一次奔向战场。于是，我听到了他们的死讯。他们死在你们的爪牙之下，在星槎坠毁的火焰里，在帝弓的光矢中……

**Jiaoqiu:** Like a useless idiot, I saved a fish named "Life" out of the cauldron called "Death," only to watch it struggle and dive back into the boiling broth.

> **椒丘：**我像是个徒劳的白痴，从名为死亡的鼎镬中，捞起一尾名叫生命的鱼——看着它一个挣扎，又再次跳进了滚沸的汤汁里。

**Jiaoqiu:** So I asked myself, why were they so eager to run toward their death after they had recovered from their wounds? Why wouldn't they value their hard-won life? All the doubts left me feeling lost...

> **椒丘：**我问自己，是什么让他们在伤愈后，又不顾一切地奔赴死地？为什么不珍惜得来不易的生命？这让我感到迷茫、空虚……

**Hoolay:** Heh... I can smell your desperation, all the way down to your bone marrow.

> **呼雷：**呵呵…我在你身上嗅到了深入骨髓的绝望。

**Jiaoqiu:** Eventually, I realized that their deaths held value. They placed the weight of their sacrifice on the living, granting us strength. With a coin forged by their deaths, they exchanged something more in return...

> **椒丘：**最后，我明白了，离去亦有「价值」。他们将离去的重量，压在了我们这些活人的心上，给了我们更多的力量。他们用死亡这枚硬币，换回了更多……

**Jiaoqiu:** Everything I'm doing now, following you so closely, is for just one reason: Witnessing your death with my own two eyes...

> **椒丘：**这就是如今我所做的一切，紧紧跟在你的身边，只为用我的双眼见证一个结果：你的死亡……

**Jiaoqiu:** Even your death has value. It will pave the way for a peaceful Wardance and a fully cured Feixiao.

> **椒丘：**你的死亡，亦有价值。它将换来演武仪典的平安；还有被我治愈的飞霄。

**Hoolay:** Hmph, the thoughts in your pathetic head are hardly surprising.

> **呼雷：**哼，你可悲的脑袋里转动的念头，真是不出所料。

**Jiaoqiu:** Did you already know this?

> **椒丘：**你早就知道？

**Hoolay:** Yes, I did. As borisin, we understand the value of "death" more than anyone. And as a healer who has witnessed so much death, you won't be swayed by fear.

> **呼雷：**不错，因为没有谁比步离人更清楚「死亡」所能带来的价值。而像你这样见惯死亡的医士，简单的恐惧也吓阻不了你。

**Hoolay:** What a shame. What a shame. Your story actually sparked a trace of respect for you in my heart.

> **呼雷：**真遗憾。在听完这个故事的刹那，我心中竟然升起了一丝对你的敬意。

**Jiaoqiu:** Can you even feel "respect"... with your corrupted heart?

> **椒丘：**你那颗流淌着毒血的心脏里…也会升起「尊重」这种情感吗？

**Hoolay:** Of course, because I caught a whiff of my own kind in you. Unfortunately, in the end, you're still just a weak fox.

> **呼雷：**当然，因为我恍惚嗅到了同类的味道。可惜，你终究只是一条软弱无力的狐狸。

**Hoolay:** As the Wolf's Creed goes: Gift the wolf a dead end, where new paths arise. Raise him to a doomed fate, where satiation lies. The cowards, in forgotten corners, meet their unworthy demise. Yet the valorous, in brutal battles, embrace their eternal prize...

> **呼雷：**如同「狼之古训」所说：赐狼以穷途，令其寻得前路。饲狼以死数，令其食能果腹。苟且偷安，无处得生；抵死鏖杀，万世长存。

**Hoolay:** That's why I'm keeping you alive for now. I want to show you how borisin truly respect their enemies.

> **呼雷：**这也是我暂时留着你的原因，我会向你展示步离人对仇敌的最大敬意：

**Hoolay:** We will consume your flesh and blood, nourishing our own. We will crush your hopes and dreams, clearing a path for our hunt! Your feeble souls will witness a new future, a future that belongs to me!

> **呼雷：**吞噬你们的血肉，滋养我们的筋骨，粉碎你们的愿景，开辟我们的猎途；要让你们的死魂灵好好见证，未来属于我！

**Mok Tok:** My Lord! We've received a message. Our arrangement at Stargazer Navalia has been discovered. We must act quickly!

> **末度：**大人！负责接应的同胞发来了消息，迴星港的布置被人发现，我们现在必须立刻动身了！

**Hoolay:** That doesn't surprise me at all.

> **呼雷：**这一切也并没有出乎我的意料。

**Hoolay:** Mok Tok... What's that noise?

> **呼雷：**末度…这是什么声音？

**Mok Tok:** It's the Skysplitter. The ship that the Wardance will be held on is about to set off! The Sky-Faring Commission will clear the air routes! And if our starskiffs try to escape, we'll be spotted.

> **末度：**是竞锋舰。举行演武仪典的星船要起航了。该死，到时候天舶司会清空航路！我们的星槎要是想逃走，一定会被发现的。

**Hoolay:** Calm down, Mok Tok! Look at you now, hiding and fearful... Where is your borisin dignity?

> **呼雷：**冷静，末度！看看你现在的样子，东躲西藏，提心吊胆…哪里还有半点步离人的尊严！

**Mok Tok:** As I said, I'd sacrifice my dignity for your return! As long as you can come back to the borisin packs, there's still hope!

> **末度：**我说过，为了能让您顺利回归，我不需要什么尊严！只要您能返回步离人的猎群，一切就还有希望！

**Hoolay:** Hope? The borisin have forgotten the Wolf's Creed! Weak creatures put their hope in the strong, but the strong fight their way out!

> **呼雷：**希望？步离人早就遗忘了「狼之古训」！软弱的野兽将希望寄托于强者庇护，强大的野兽则会自己挣杀出一条血路！

**Hoolay:** Bringing me back instead of choosing a new master only proves the decline of our pack. And as for the prophet who manipulated you into saving me, she's just a liar trying to use Duran's offspring!

> **呼雷：**如今的你们只想要迎回「呼雷」，而不是决出新主，已经证明了整个族群的没落。而那个操纵你们前来营救我的先知，不过是个试图利用都蓝血裔的骗子！

**Hoolay:** Mok Tok, let me tell you how the borisin will rise to power! We won't hide like rats in the streets of the Xianzhou! We will be ravenous wolves, walking amidst a herd of lambs with our fangs bared.

> **呼雷：**末度，让我来告诉你步离人如何崛起！我们绝不会像老鼠一样躲藏在仙舟的街巷间，你应当是露出獠牙的饿狼，行走在满是羔羊的牧群。

**Mok Tok:** But Great Warhead, our packs are not here! We can't go to war like this!

> **末度：**战首大人，我们的狼群不在这里！我们不能贸然开战！

**Hoolay:** Our packs are not here? Wherever I go, everyone is the pack!

> **呼雷：**狼群不在这里？不，我所行之处，皆为狼群！

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Hoolay:** Mok Tok, we're the apex predators at the top of the hierarchy.

> **呼雷：**末度，我们是位居食物链顶端的强者。

**Hoolay:** As wolves, we create fear. We don't become servants to it. If you're blind to the path, I will be the crimson moon that lights the way for you.

> **呼雷：**身为狼，我们是恐惧的制造者，而不是恐惧的奴隶。如果你无法看见道路，我将成为高悬的赤月，为你们照亮道路的所在——

**Hoolay:** Share my crimson blood with our brethren! Use it to infect those foxians and strike fear into their hearts!

> **呼雷：**和所有的弟兄们一起分享我的赤血。用它来感染狐人，用它来制造恐惧！

**Hoolay:** Now, you devious monkey... Come out and face me!

> **呼雷：**藏形匿影的猴子…给我出来！

**Moze:** ...Jiaoqiu.

> **貊泽：**…椒丘。

**Jiaoqiu:** Moze... run...

> **椒丘：**貊泽…快走——

**Hoolay:** No, he can't run, and neither can you. You've come at the right time, monkey of the Yaoqing. Tell your general...

> **呼雷：**他走不了，你也一样。曜青的猴子，你来的正是时候。告诉你们的将军……

**Hoolay:** Tell her, that I will unleash a massacre here, drowning the Xianzhou Luofu in blood. From this moment on, wolves bearing my blood will hunt on every street, feasting on the followers of that Devilish Archer.

> **呼雷：**告诉她，我将从这儿开始，杀穿罗浮仙舟。从现在开始，分享我狼血的子嗣会在每一处闹市中奔逐狩猎，以妖弓的信众为食。

**Hoolay:** Follow me, my cubs! We shall stride among the prey!

> **呼雷：**狼子们，随我一同，在猎物之间高视阔步！

**Mok Tok and Wolftrooper:** Gift the wolf a dead end, where new paths arise. Raise him to a doomed fate, where satiation lies. The cowards in forgotten corners, meet their unworthy demise. Yet the valorous, in brutal battles, embrace their eternal prize...

> **末度&狼卒：**赐狼以穷途，令其寻得前路。饲狼以死数，令其食能果腹。苟且偷安，无处得生；抵死鏖杀，万世长存……

**Hoolay:** Do you hear that? The rumble of the cannons... it brings back all the memories of past battles within me...

> **呼雷：**听啊，这炮声隆隆——唤起了我心底所有关于战场的回忆……

**Hoolay:** My return will bring back the Wolf's Creed. In my own way, I shall save our weakened pack and restore it to its former glory!

> **呼雷：**我的回归将带回「狼之古训」，我将以我自己的方式挽救堕落的族群，让它重焕荣光！

Switching to March 7th's POV...

On Huaiyan's orders, March 7th and Yunli board the Skysplitter, participating in the Wardance Ceremony as scheduled...

## 026　Novice in Zenithal Duel　竞武云巅，孺子问剑

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Skysplitter　|　角色：March 7th; Yunli; Confused Spectator; Curious Spectator; Sky-Faring Staff; Xikui; Reporter; Yaoqing Contestant; Soldier on Duty; Diting; Animated Spectator; Talkative Spectator　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''The Red Warcry'' completed

### Board the Skysplitter and participate in the Wardance

> *General Huaiyan tasks March 7th and Yunli with boarding the Skysplitter and participating in the Wardance. Apart from striving for victory in the arena, they must also take up the responsibility of defending the arena against potential crises. However, the young swordswoman who will be making her debut can't help but feel anxious at such a prospect...*

The Wardance is about to commence Skysplitter

**March 7th:** Wow! The Skysplitter's gun salute is really loud! So this is what the Wardance is actually like? I feel like my eardrums are going to burst.

> **三月七：**呜哇，竞锋舰上的礼炮声好响啊！这就是「演武仪典」的气氛吗，感觉耳朵都要震聋了。

**Yunli:** Well, how could they expect to attract people to come and watch if they didn't make a huge spectacle? March, you haven't forgotten what you came to do, have you?

> **云璃：**场面不大，又怎么能吸引别人前来观看呢。三月，你没忘记自己是来做什么的吧？

- ▸ What... did I come to do?

> ▸ 我…是来做什么的呢？

**March 7th:** Who am I? Where am I? What am I doing here?

> **三月七：**我是谁？我在哪？我在这儿做什么？

**Yunli:** Look around you, do you remember anything?

> **云璃：**看看周围，想起什么来了吗？

**March 7th:** Ah, memories of falling from the sky into Taikiyan Stadium have come flooding back to me. We... came to compete, didn't we?

> **三月七：**啊，从天而降砸在泰科铵大球馆的回忆又涌了上来，我们…是来比赛的吧。

**Yunli:** Great, you've finally gotten your memory back, and I can put down Old Mettle.

> **云璃：**太好了太好了，你的脑袋终于恢复记忆了，我也放下了高高举起的「老铁」。

- ▸ I am here to be the ringmaster on behalf of Master Yanqing!

> ▸ 我代彦卿师父守擂！

**March 7th:** As a representative of the Xianzhou Luofu, I will defeat all challengers!

> **三月七：**我代表罗浮仙舟，前来打败挑战者！

**Yunli:** That's the spirit, sounds like you're all fired up! If you could just keep your legs from shaking so much, it would be more convincing, March.

> **云璃：**就是这股气势…很有精神嘛！三月，你要是能双腿打直了别发抖，那就看起来更有说服力了。

- ▸ I am here to ensure the safety of the Wardance!

> ▸ 我是来确保演武仪典安全的！

**Yunli:** That was the mission my grandfather entrusted to me. You just relax and keep guard over the ring.

> **云璃：**那是爷爷交托给我的任务，至于你，安心守住擂台就好。

**Yunli:** As the saying goes, "The disciple takes the toil of their master." Remember, you are here under the identity of a Luofu swordmaster, taking the place of your no-good Master Yanqing as the ringmaster.

> **云璃：**俗话说得好，「有事弟子服其劳」。记住喽，你是以罗浮剑士的身份，代替你那个不肖的彦卿师父前来守擂的。

**March 7th:** But what am I supposed to do if my opponent for this round is super talented and just wipes the floor with me in seconds? I'm still a beginner in swordplay! I don't know where I got the confidence when I agreed to help General Huaiyan.

> **三月七：**说起来，要是这一回台上的对手超级厉害，三两下就把我打趴了，那可怎么办呀…毕竟我只算得上剑术初学者。也不知当时哪来的信心，就这么答应了怀炎将军。

**Yunli:** Come on, there's no need to worry about that. If you are defeated, then your opponent shall have to answer to me! Although, if that happens, the honor and the glory of the Xianzhou Luofu shall all be taken by the Zhuming, hahaha...

> **云璃：**嗨呀，不用担心。你要是趴下了，就让为师来会会你的对手咯。只是这么一来，罗浮仙舟的光彩可就全都归朱明啦，哈哈哈哈哈……

**March 7th:** Master Yunli, please, I'm about to compete! Can you say something that will give me a little confidence?

> **三月七：**拜托了，云璃师父，我都快要上场了，你就给我点信心吧！

**Yunli:** March. Think about the great storms that you Nameless of the Express have weathered along your many journeys. There's no doubt that you have faced far more terrifying enemies than your opponents here today, right? Don't you feel a lot more relaxed looking at it that way?

> **云璃：**三月，想一想你们列车无名客一路旅行经历的大风大浪！你一定见识过比演武对手可怕的多的敌人吧？这么一想是不是轻松多了？

- ▸ Master Yunli, I think I should drop out of the contest.

> ▸ 云璃师父，我还是退赛吧。

**March 7th:** There's so much pressure, my stomach is really hurting. Maybe... I should just drop out of the contest?

> **三月七：**压力好大，我感觉自己肚子隐隐作痛，要不…我还是退赛吧？

**Yunli:** You made a promise to my grandfather and now you want to run away? Get out there, now!

> **云璃：**你答应了爷爷还想跑？快走！

- ▸ Why don't we call Yanqing back?

> ▸ 要不，还是把彦卿叫回来吧！

**March 7th:** Why don't we call Master Yanqing back? What kind of master would retire at the height of their career and leave their disciple to fight in their place?

> **三月七：**要不要把彦卿师父给叫回来？这当师父的急流勇退…让弟子垫上刀头，这叫哪门子事啊。

**Yunli:** March. Your master has dropped out of the contest. This is a great opportunity for you to stick your neck out!

> **云璃：**三月，师父退赛，这可是你出人头地的好机会啊。

**March 7th:** Stick my neck out and get my head chopped off, you mean!

> **三月七：**我看是师父给我一个人头落地的好机会吧！

**Yunli:** What is all of this complaining about? You made a promise to my grandfather and now you want to run away? Get out there, now!

> **云璃：**哪儿来得这么多抱怨，你答应了爷爷还想跑？快走！

- ▸ Let's get going, then.

> ▸ 咱们走吧。

**March 7th:** I've trained hard in the art of swordplay over these few days. It's time for me to get in the ring and prove myself. Let's go.

> **三月七：**这些日子我苦练剑术，也该上场证明一下自己了。咱们走。

### Arrive at the Skysplitter's security checkpoint

**March 7th:** Master Yunli, I don't think I'm completely ready...

> **三月七：**云璃师父，我感觉我还没有准备充分……

**Yunli:** March, there is no such thing as completely ready.

> **云璃：**三月，准备永远是不充分的。

**Confused Spectator:** So this is the Skysplitter? It looked quite impressive from the ground where I was on duty, but...

> **迷茫的观众：**这就是竞锋舰吗？虽然执勤的时候在地面上看还是挺气派的……

**Confused Spectator:** But now that I'm on board, it just doesn't seem as... big as I imagined?

> **迷茫的观众：**但今天真的登舰了，感觉没有我想象中的…那么大？

**Curious Spectator:** Oh? And how big did you imagine this "impressive Skysplitter" to be? This big? This big, maybe?

> **好奇的观众：**哦豁，你想象中的「气派的竞锋舰」是什么样子的呢？那么大？还是那么大？

**Confused Spectator:** Let me think... Hmm. I'd expect at least a four-hour journey by starskiff to the spectator seating from here. Otherwise it's simply not impressive enough!

> **迷茫的观众：**我想想…嗯，要是从这里去往观众席，得搭星槎换乘，坐上两个时辰吧！不然不够气派啊！

- ▸ Wouldn't that be too big for no good reason?

> ▸ 那不就是单纯很大而已吗？

**Confused Spectator:** The bigger, the better! If you ask me, the Skysplitter is nowhere near big enough and there are nowhere near enough cannons!

> **迷茫的观众：**大就是好，多就是美！我看这竞锋舰还是不够大，炮台也不够多！

**Curious Spectator:** This warship used to have loads of cannons.

> **好奇的观众：**以前这艘舰船上的炮台还是蛮多的。

- ▸ You're daydreaming about stuff that ain't gonna happen.

> **迷茫的观众：**怎么了嘛，人还得是要有梦想的嘛！要我说啊，竞锋舰就是太小了！

> ▸ 其实我也觉得这里有点小。

**March 7th:** The Skysplitter looks small from a distance, but it's even smaller than I imagined now that I'm on board.

> **三月七：**远看这竞锋舰很小，登上一看，比想象的还要小。

**Confused Spectator:** Hear that? This person gets it!

> **迷茫的观众：**你听听，这是我的同道中人。

**Confused Spectator:** Either way, I thought that the Skysplitter would be absolutely huge, so it's not quite how I imagined it.

> **迷茫的观众：**主要是我想象中的竞锋舰是很大的，但是它和我想象的不太一样。

> **战力评级爱好者：**这是我从湛蓝星那边买来的特产「定分镜」，只要盯着选手瞧，就能给对方的战斗力定个评级。

**March 7th:** Take a look at me, then. What is my combat power rating?

> **三月七：**那你看看我，我的战斗力是多少？

> **战力评级爱好者：**…5。

> **三月七：**怎么可能，你再给我好好看看嘛！是不是满分也是5？

**Yunli:** Poor little March, being bullied by a mechanical scoring system... Don't pay them any mind, let's go!

> **云璃：**可怜的小三月，被机器的评分玩弄于股掌之间。别看了，快走吧！

> ▸ （快速走开）

> **夕葵：**说起来，三月小姐要代彦卿骁卫守擂的事已经公示了。我刚才从选手大厅经过的时候，听见选手们正在议论纷纷，其中最多的问题就是——「三月七是谁？」

**Xikui:** If they ask you this, how will you introduce yourself? Do you have something prepared?

> **夕葵：**他们要是问起，三月小姐该如何自我介绍，您准备好了吗？

- ▸ March 7th, young beauty of the Astral Express.

> ▸ 三月七，星穹列车的美少女。

**March 7th:** March 7th, a young beauty from the Astral Express!

> **三月七：**三月七，一位来自星穹列车的美少女。

**Yunli:** Alright! When people ask you, you might as well tell them this loud and clear.

> **云璃：**好啊，在别人问你的时候你不妨就这么大声说出来。

- ▸ March 7th, Yanqing's disciple.

> ▸ 三月七，彦卿的弟子。

**March 7th:** I am a disciple of Lieutenant Yanqing... That means that I'm also technically a grand-disciple of General Jing Yuan. When you put it like that, I'm actually a pretty big deal!

> **三月七：**我是彦卿骁卫座下弟子…这么一想也算是景元将军的徒孙。如此说来，我来头还挺不小的！

**Yunli:** Ugh! You completely left my name out... How rude!

> **云璃：**可恶，竟然把为师的名头抛到了一边…气死我了。

- ▸ March 7th, the invincible March 7th!

> ▸ 三月七，无敌的三月七！

**March 7th:** March 7th, the invincible March 7th!

> **三月七：**三月七，无敌的三月七！

**Yunli:** Next time you try to bluff like that, at least make it convincing.

> **云璃：**至少下一次虚张声势的时候，底气一定要足。

- ▸ Losers have no right to ask my name.

> ▸ 失败者不配问我的名字。

**March 7th:** Hmph. Losers have no right to know my great name!

> **三月七：**哼，失败者不配知道本姑娘的名字。

**Yunli:** Is this some kind of villain entrance line?

> **云璃：**这是什么反派登场台词吗？

**Xikui:** Hahaha, I see you are both quite relaxed! Impressive, given the imminent danger you face!

> **夕葵：**哈哈哈哈，看来二位还是挺轻松的，临危受命能有这样的状态可不容易。

**Xikui:** Miss Yunli's contestant information was registered for the Wardance a long time ago. However, as a last-minute entry, the Sky-Faring Commission has taken care of the necessary procedures for you, Miss March 7th.

> **夕葵：**演武仪典这边早就登记过云璃小姐的选手信息了。三月七小姐则是临时入选这场比赛，天舶司已经为你准备好相关的手续了。

**Xikui:** Please follow this path. The contestant hall is just up ahead.

> **夕葵：**两位请先沿着这条路上去，选手大厅就在前方。

**March 7th:** Thank you, Miss Xikui.

> **三月七：**谢谢你，夕葵姐姐。

## 027　A Swarm of Carnage　敛魂血轮，率兽食人

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Exalting Sanctum　|　角色：Trailblazer; Feixiao; Yanqing; Moze; Yukong　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''Novice in Zenithal Duel'' completed

### Head to the Exalting Sanctum in search of Hoolay's whereabouts

> *With the bait set, the Merlin's Claw's true hunt begins. Having locked onto Hoolay's whereabouts, the Cloud Knights depart for the Exalting Sanctum. However, Hoolay's promised assault is also set to begin.*

**Cloud Knight Soldier:** General, report! The teams are in position, and the Sky-Faring Commission has taken control of the starskiff lanes.

> **云骑士卒：**报告将军，队伍已各就其位。天舶司对星槎航道的管制也全部完成。

**Feixiao:** Activate all cycranes and have them scan everyone coming in and out of the port.

> **飞霄：**让所有机巧鸟动起来，扫描每个打算出入港口的人。

**Feixiao:** Remain vigilant at all times. Any suspicious movements must be suppressed as quickly as possible before the situation gets out of hand.

> **飞霄：**时刻戒备，一旦有可疑的异动，要在最短的时间内对其进行压制，避免事态扩散。

**Cloud Knight Soldier:** Yes, general!

> **云骑士卒：**是！

**Feixiao:** Yanqing, (Trailblazer), follow me.

> **飞霄：**彦卿，开拓者，随我来。

### Find out Moze's whereabouts

**Feixiao:** Moze's signal is nearby, but we lost contact just a moment ago...

> **飞霄：**貊泽的信号就在附近。但是在一刻之前，他断开了联系……

**Yanqing:** Could that mean...

> **彦卿：**莫非……

**Feixiao:** He will be fine. Cloud Knights, search for the target!

> **飞霄：**他会没事的。云骑，搜索目标！

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Moze:** ...

> **貊泽：**……

**Moze:** All of you, get out of my way...

> **貊泽：**都给我退开……

- ▸ It's Moze...

> ▸ 是貊泽…

- ▸ General, look there...

> ▸ 将军，看那儿…

**Feixiao:** Wait, don't go any closer.

> **飞霄：**等等，别靠近。

**Moze:** General... I failed you. That mad dog left me here as...

> **貊泽：**将军…我失手了。那条疯狗把我留在了这儿，是要向你……

**Feixiao:** A declaration of war.

> **飞霄：**宣战。

**Unfriendly Foxian:** That's right. I have been holding back the urge to slit his throat all this time. Well, Lord Hoolay ordered us to remain here, just to see...

> **神色不善的狐人：**是啊，我可是一直强忍着撕开他喉咙的冲动。毕竟，呼雷大人命我们留在此处，就是想看看……

**Unfriendly Foxian:** ...if you, the great Merlin's Claw, mortal enemy of the borisin, are able to join him in a little... hunting game...

> **神色不善的狐人：**你这位大名鼎鼎的天击将军，步离人的死敌，有没有能耐陪他一同进行…这场狩猎游戏？

**Feixiao:** Hunting is not a game, it is a battle of life and death. Are you ready for your death, abomination!?

> **飞霄：**狩猎可不是什么游戏。它是一场你死我活的战争，准备好受死了吧？孽物！

### Battle the borisin pack

*(Begin battle against Sableclaw Wolftrooper ×1, Eclipse Wolftrooper ×2)*

**Borisin:** Die, you lowly beast! Die!

> **步离人：**贱畜，受死！受死！

**Yanqing:** Save your breath!

> **彦卿：**省省力气，给我收声！

*(After one of the Eclipse Wolftroopers has been defeated)*

**Borisin:** Don't think you've won...

> **步离人：**别得意…

**Borisin:** The Warhead has prepared... a road of no return for all of you...

> **步离人：**战首为你们准备了…一条死路…

*(After the battle)*

**Feixiao:** Moze, are you okay?

> **飞霄：**貊泽，你没事吧？

**Moze:** I... wasn't able to save Jiaoqiu. Hoolay was more cunning and powerful than I imagined...

> **貊泽：**我…没能救回椒丘。呼雷比我想像得更狡诈，也更难对付。

**Feixiao:** Tell me, how is he planning to declare this war against me...? With a one-on-one duel? Or with a hostage exchange, maybe?

> **飞霄：**说说看，他打算怎么向我宣战…是单枪匹马的决斗？还是交换人质？

**Moze:** Hoolay is planning on attacking the bustling streets of the Xianzhou Luofu.

> **貊泽：**呼雷计划袭击罗浮仙舟上的闹市。

**Feixiao:** What did you just say?

> **飞霄：**你说什么？

**Yanqing:** Impossible! Even if there are still undercover borisin that we haven't sniffed out, Hoolay can't have more than a few dozen people. How is he planning on simultaneously attacking the streets of the Xianzhou Luofu?

> **彦卿：**这怎么可能？就算还有潜伏的步离人伪装者没能揪出来，呼雷这一行也不过数十人，他打算同时袭击罗浮仙舟的闹市？

**Moze:** He has an ace up his sleeve. Hoolay's body holds a plaguemark passed down through generations of borisin. I've seen him convert a foxian to a borisin wolftrooper with my own eyes...

> **貊泽：**这才是那家伙的底牌。呼雷的体内潜藏着步离人之间世代传承的寿瘟祸迹。我亲眼看见他将一个狐人转变成了步离狼卒……

**Moze:** This is Hoolay's "declaration of war." His blood... It can cause foxians to rapidly transform and lose their minds. He has passed his blood to his minions and is planning to spread it across the Luofu and stir up panic!

> **貊泽：**这就是呼雷的「宣战」。他的血…能让狐人迅速扭曲变形、陷入疯狂，他将自己的血交给了手下，想在罗浮各处散布，掀起恐慌！

- ▸ You're saying that there will be attacks all over?

> ▸ 也就是说到处都会发生袭击？

**Feixiao:** He wants to "encircle" us in one fell swoop... This beast is truly unpredictable.

> **飞霄：**想以一己之力「包围」我们…还真是个出人意料的怪物。

- ▸ Evacuate all the foxians immediately!

> ▸ 赶快疏散所有狐人吧！

**Yanqing:** Contact the Sky-Faring Commission and the Cloud Knights right away! Have them enforce a traffic ban and order all foxians to remain indoors until further notice!

> **彦卿：**立刻联系天舶司和云骑，启动通行禁令，要求所有的狐人闭门不出。

**Feixiao:** The moment you see your enemy's declaration of war, it means that they have already begun to take action.

> **飞霄：**在看到敌人下战书的那一刻，就意味着对方已经开始行动了。

**Moze:** But... if Hoolay succeeds in getting this panic to spread—

> **貊泽：**可是…一旦呼雷制造的恐慌蔓延开来——

**Feixiao:** Do you remember what I told you before? Hunting is about thinking in the same way as your prey, not just mindlessly chasing after them.

> **飞霄：**还记得我告诉你们的吗？狩猎，应当考虑猎物的所想，而不是一味追逐猎物的踪迹。

**Feixiao:** What Hoolay wants is to see everyone on the Luofu descend into panic. He wants to see us lose our minds and waste our limited manpower scouring every inch of the Luofu for signs of an attack until we exhaust ourselves.

> **飞霄：**呼雷想要的就是罗浮上所有人陷入恐慌，他想看我们自乱阵脚，用有限的人力在罗浮每一寸土地上捕风捉影，寻找袭击到来的征兆，最后筋疲力尽。

**Feixiao:** It's like that party game that the people of the Yaoqing love to play: They hide an object under one of several bowls, then move the bowls around, and have you guess which one the object is underneath.

> **飞霄：**就像曜青人宴饮时爱玩的游戏：用几个碗盖住一样物什，移来换去，最后要你猜猜东西藏在哪个碗底下。

**Feixiao:** The way I see it, this is all nothing more than misdirection...

> **飞霄：**但在我看来，这些都不过是障眼法——

**Feixiao:** No matter what Hoolay does, there is one truth that cannot be changed: He is trapped aboard the Xianzhou Luofu. The only way for him to escape is to find a ship.

> **飞霄：**无论呼雷做什么，都没法改变一个事实：他被困在了罗浮仙舟上。想要逃离此处，唯一的办法就是寻到船只。

- ▸ And the only ship he is able to see is...

> ▸ 他唯一能看到的船…

- ▸ So his only option is the Skysplitter?

> ▸ 只剩下天空中的竞锋舰了？

**(Trailblazer):** Right now... the only ship he'd be able to see is the Skysplitter.

> **开拓者：**眼下…他唯一能看到的船，只有天空中的竞锋舰了。

**Feixiao:** In his eyes, that ship is full of countless hostages. That would be the best place for him to go. So that will also be our final battlefield.

> **飞霄：**对他而言，那上面有数不清的人质。那里会是最理想的去处，也是我们最后的战场。

**Yanqing:** But, if we're wrong about this... If he goes somewhere else, or if he does as he said he would, and his minions infect the foxians everywhere with wolf blood... What do we do then?

> **彦卿：**可如果我们猜错了…他去了别处，又或者就像他所说的——他的爪牙会带上狼血在各处感染狐人掀起混乱…我们又该怎么办？

**Yanqing:** Are we just supposed to abandon those "less likely" locations?

> **彦卿：**难道我们要放弃那些「可能性更小」的地方吗？

**Feixiao:** This is another lesson that you must learn outside of swordplay: Weighing your options and making a decision. There are always questions that we will ask ourselves but never be able to answer.

> **飞霄：**这就是你在剑术之外要学的另一课：「权衡」和「取舍」，我们永远在扪心自问却又永远无解的问题。

**Feixiao:** Before making a decision, we must destroy any hesitation that we are holding onto. Once we make a decision, we must fight off any regrets.

> **飞霄：**在做出决定前，我们要杀死内心的犹豫；在做出决定后，我们要和悔恨缠斗。

**Feixiao:** There is a chance that we will make the wrong decision and allow our allies to be sacrificed in vain. There is a chance that we will predict our enemy's movements correctly, yet underestimate their strength and be overwhelmed in battle...

> **飞霄：**我们可能会选错方向，令无辜的战友白白牺牲；我们可能猜对了敌人的动向，却错估了对手的实力，折戟败北……

**Feixiao:** But being lost in hesitation and failing to take any action will always be more harmful than taking the wrong path. We must make a decision, no matter what.

> **飞霄：**但犹豫不前比犯错更加致命。无论如何，我们都要作出决定。

**Feixiao:** Everyone, listen up. I will personally take charge of Starskiff Haven, evacuate the people, and prepare for any possible attacks. I will do my best to ensure that things are safe on the ground.

> **飞霄：**各位，听我命令。我会坐镇星槎海，疏散人群，应对所有可能到来的袭击。我会倾全力迎战，确保地面的安全。

**Feixiao:** (Trailblazer), please lend us your strength as you did when the Ambrosial Arbor was reborn. I need you and Moze to go in search of Jiaoqiu.

> **飞霄：**开拓者，请你像建木重生时那样，助仙舟一臂之力。我要拜托你和貊泽在这儿搜寻椒丘的下落。

- ▸ Leave it to me!

> ▸ 交给我吧！

**Yanqing:** General, please entrust me with the safety of the Skysplitter! I was originally meant to participate in the tournament, so now it is only right that I return to the Skysplitter.

> **彦卿：**将军，请将竞锋舰的安全交给我！我本该出现在擂台上，现在我也必须重新回到竞锋舰上去。

**Feixiao:** If Hoolay does come to attack the Skysplitter... Lieutenant Yanqing, I need you to hold him there until I am able to arrive.

> **飞霄：**如果呼雷袭击了竞锋舰…彦卿骁卫，请你务必尽力困住他，直到我赶来为止。

**Yanqing:** Please rest assured, General. If he dares step foot on the Skysplitter, I, Yanqing, swear on the blade in my hands that he shall not escape!

> **彦卿：**将军应当对我有信心。若他敢登上竞锋舰，彦卿以手中之剑起誓，绝不让他逃离！

As the Cloud Knights set off to various locations, they swiftly began to cordon off areas and start inspections. At that time, the Cloud Knights are waiting in formation in the Exalting Sanctum...

**Feixiao:** Cloud Knights! The borisin have arrogantly decided to challenge us on Xianzhou territory! They vow that they will unleash a bloodbath along the streets of the Xianzhou.

> **飞霄：**诸位！在仙舟的疆域上，步离人竟然狂妄地向我们发起挑战，放言要血洗仙舟的闹市街巷！

**Feixiao:** The unarmed civilians of the Xianzhou and countless visitors from afar are under our protection. Let me ask you, as Cloud Knights, will we allow the borisin to succeed with their plan!?

> **飞霄：**我们的身后，是手无寸铁的仙舟百姓和迢迢而来的旅客。试问，身为云骑军人，我们能让步离人的计划得逞吗？！

**Cloud Knight:** Never!

> **云骑军：**绝不允许！

**Feixiao:** Be on your guard!

> **飞霄：**随时戒备！

**Feixiao:** Calling Starskiff Haven. Have all Cloud Knights be on full alert for borisin attacks!

> **飞霄：**呼叫星槎海，所有云骑全面戒备，防范步离人的袭击！

Yukong's Communicator: Feixiao, the ports of Starskiff Haven are crawling with wolf-like creatures. They're moving fast—

**Feixiao:** Hold them back, I will be there immediately!

> **飞霄：**撑住，我马上就到！

Switching to Feixiao's POV...

"Surround three and leave one." The Merlin's Claw initiates the hunt against the pack of wolves...

## 028　Sweep by Merlin's Claw　天击长驱，镇绥无虞

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Central Starskiff Haven　|　角色：Feixiao; Yukong; Trailblazer; Moze　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''A Swarm of Carnage'' completed

### Aid Starskiff Haven and thwart Hoolay's assaults

> *In response to Hoolay's seeming promise of an assault, the Merlin's Claw decides to split her troops into three squads. As for herself, she shall shoulder the heavy responsibility of ensuring the safety of ground locations like Starskiff Haven and Exalting Sanctum. Wait... Does she intend to accomplish this all by herself?*

*(Cutscene begins)*

*(Open Feixiao: Technique Tutorial)*

### Eliminate all the borisin who are invading Starskiff Haven

**Yukong:** There you are, Feixiao!

> **驭空：**你来了啊，飞霄！

**Feixiao:** Madam Yukong! After thirty years, we find ourselves fighting side-by-side once more!

> **飞霄：**驭空姐姐，时隔三十年，咱们又再次并肩战斗了。

**Feixiao:** It's been a while since I've let loose. Let's get this started!

> **飞霄：**好久没松松筋骨了，开始吧！

**Yukong:** Remember you are still on the Luofu. Try not to go too wild!

> **驭空：**眼下是在罗浮仙舟上，你可给我收着点拳脚！

### Meet up with Yukong

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Feixiao:** We have cleared out the enemies in Starskiff Haven.

> **飞霄：**星槎海的战场打扫完毕了。

**Yukong:** But it seems that there are fierce battles being waged at both the Exalting Sanctum and Aurum Alley. I will prepare a starskiff—

> **驭空：**但长乐天和金人巷似乎还陷入苦战中，我这就准备星槎——

**Feixiao:** We cannot allow the fighting to continue. This is now a race against time to stop this farce that Hoolay has created.

> **飞霄：**久战不利，眼下必须争分夺秒地挫败呼雷制造的闹剧。

**Feixiao:** I know Jiaoqiu always wants me to know my limits and not get carried away, but... now is the time to bring this to a swift end.

> **飞霄：**虽然椒丘总是希望我适可而止、别动真格，但现在…该是真正「速战速决」的时候了。

**Feixiao:** Contact the Exalting Sanctum. Tell them, if they see a light in the sky, all units are to retreat immediately!

> **飞霄：**呼叫长乐天，如果看到空中有光，立刻全员散开！

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Feixiao:** Madam Yukong, lend me your bow!

> **飞霄：**驭空姐姐，借你弓一用！

**(Trailblazer):** ...What's that?

> **开拓者：**…那是什么？

**Moze:** Everyone, out of the way!

> **貊泽：**散！都散开！

*(Cutscene ends)*

Returning to the Trailblazer's POV...

When you have a chance to make a choice, make one that you know you won't regret.

## 029　Last Stand of a Lone Wolf　解裂残躯，独狼负隅

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Post-Battle Aurum Alley　|　角色：Trailblazer; Moze; Mok Tok; Hoolay; Feixiao　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''Sweep by Merlin's Claw'' completed

### At this moment, in the war-torn Aurum Alley...

> *In Aurum Alley, you and Moze stand above the aftermath of war, searching for survivors... as well as the whereabouts of Jiaoqiu.*

At the same time, in the Aurum Alley Feixiao's skyborne wind arrows accurately find their mark in the borisin...

**Moze:** Tsk, what a mess. I just knew it would end up this way...

> **貊泽：**啧，真是难搞啊。我就知道事情会变成这样……

**Moze:** Every time she hits the battlefield she makes a mess like this.

> **貊泽：**这家伙每次上战场总喜欢搞出这么大的场面。

**Moze:** She didn't even leave a single survivor to get intel from. One of these men must have known about Hoolay and Jiaoqiu's whereabouts.

> **貊泽：**这下连半个能问出情报的活口都不剩了。这些家伙里一定有人知道呼雷和椒丘的下落……

- ▸ What happened just now?

> ▸ 刚才那是怎么回事？

**Moze:** That was one of Feixiao's "arrows." Who knows what she used as an arrow to fire all the way over here.

> **貊泽：**是飞霄射出的「箭」，天知道这一回她是拿什么东西当作箭矢射到了这儿。

- ▸ That general of yours is way out of line!

> ▸ 椒丘还活着吗…

**Moze:** She... She's just very passionate. She sees every place as a battlefield where she can unleash hell.

> **貊泽：**她…热血上头就是这样，把所有地方当成可以乱来的战场。

- ▸ Has that general of yours always been like this?

> ▸ 你家将军未免太离谱了吧！

**Moze:** I wouldn't say "always"... But "often" would be fair. As soon as the heat of battle gets to her, she sees every place as a battlefield where she can unleash hell.

> **貊泽：**谈不上「一直」，只是「经常」罢了。一旦打到兴头上，她就会把所有地方当成可以乱来的战场。

- ▸ I want to see her fire another arrow with my own eyes...

> ▸ 你家将军一直是这样吗？

Moze sizes you up with a cold, indifferent gaze. You can almost hear the unspoken words in his expressionless face: "Is it not better to cherish your life instead?

- ▸ Is Jiaoqiu still alive...?

> ▸ 我想亲眼看她再射一箭…

**Moze:** ...He has to be.

> **貊泽：**…椒丘必须活着。

**Moze:** A retainer like myself is nothing more than an insignificant shadow who stands by the general's side. But Jiaoqiu is different. Without his careful treatment, the general wouldn't even be here today.

> **貊泽：**像我这样的侍卫，不过是将军身旁一个微不足道的影子。但椒丘不同，若没有他的悉心治疗，便不会有今日的将军。

**Moze:** Hopefully, he can use that silver tongue of his to buy himself enough time...

> **貊泽：**我从没这么希望他能靠自己的嘴皮子再拖延些时间……

**Moze:** (Trailblazer), take a good look around. If you can find one or two borisin that aren't dead yet, let me know.

> **貊泽：**开拓者，四下看看，如果能找到一两个还活着的步离人就告诉我。

**Moze:** I don't care how tight their lips are sealed. I always have a way to crack them open and get the information we need.

> **貊泽：**不管他们嘴有多硬，我都有办法撬开，让他们吐露些情报出来。

### Assess the situation on the battlefield and search for survivors

> *Atop the Palace of Astrum, the Merlin's Claw brings the battle to an end with a single attack. Meanwhile, Moze and you emerge from the battle's aftermath in Aurum Alley and begin the search for survivors... as well as for news about Jiaoqiu's whereabouts.*

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Mok Tok:** ...What a terrifying attack.

> **末度：**…好可怕的一击。

**Mok Tok:** Who could have imagined... that such astonishing strength would lie in the hands of a foxian war slave...

> **末度：**谁能想到…如此惊人的力量竟然掌握在一个狐人战奴的手里。

**Mok Tok:** So decisive, so... brutal. She is more like a descendant of Duran than we who have fallen to our current state. No wonder the Warhead has his eyes on her.

> **末度：**如此果断，又如此…残暴。比起如今沦落到这般地步的我们，她才更像是都蓝的子裔。难怪战首会对她感兴趣。

- ▸ That's...

> ▸ 这家伙是…

**Moze:** The sneak that infiltrated The Shackling Prison. Mok Tok... You are harder to kill than a cockroach.

> **貊泽：**那个闯入幽囚狱的劫犯。末度…你还真是一只无论如何都摁不死的臭虫啊。

- ▸ The fact that you still draw breath after an attack like that...

> ▸ 受了那样一击，竟然还活着…

**Moze:** I remember you, Mok Tok... You are harder to kill than a cockroach.

> **貊泽：**我记得你，末度…你还真是一只无论如何都摁不死的臭虫啊。

**Moze:** But, the fact that you have willingly shown yourself saves us some time.

> **貊泽：**你能主动现身，也省去了我们一番功夫。

**Moze:** It is not too late for you to surrender. Tell me... where are Hoolay and Jiaoqiu?

> **貊泽：**现在投降还不算晚。告诉我，呼雷…还有，椒丘去了哪儿？

**Mok Tok:** Save your energy, Yaoqing monkey. If there is one principle that we beasts know well, it is that we might have to break off an arm to break free from a cage. Today, I will be that arm.

> **末度：**省省力气吧，曜青的猴子。所有的野兽都明白一个道理，为了摆脱陷阱，就算扯断臂膀也在所不惜。今天，我将成为这只臂膀。

**Mok Tok:** I lived as a hunter for many years, yet I have only followed the Warhead for this recent, brief period. However, his existence has brought light back to our once-blind eyes, like the moon of Verdantia. He has shown me the way.

> **末度：**在我漫长的狩猎生涯中，虽然只有眼前这短短的片刻追随了战首。但他的存在却像青丘的月亮般照亮了我们盲目已久的双眼，让我看见了道路。

**Mok Tok:** The descendants of Duran have abandoned the Wolf's Creed. In order to ensure our survival, we took solace in the shadows and fed on one another. We were no longer wolves. We were no more than rats, living a dirty and pathetic life.

> **末度：**都蓝子裔背弃了「狼之古训」。为了苟延生命，我们在黑暗中东躲西藏，互相啃噬；我们不再是狼，反而变得像老鼠般龌龊、可悲。

**Mok Tok:** Thanks to the gift granted to us by Lord Hoolay, I have shared his vision and his blood... The descendants of Duran should live for the victory of the pack, and they should die for it as well!

> **末度：**得蒙呼雷大人的恩赐，我分享了他的眼界与狼血——都蓝的子裔，应当为狼群的胜利而生，也要为狼群的胜利而死！

**Mok Tok:** Come, Yaoqing man. Show me your fangs! We will fight to the death!

> **末度：**来啊，曜青人，亮出你的爪牙！我和你，不死不休！

**Moze:** How honorable, you mangy dog.

> **貊泽：**狗东西，还挺有荣誉感。

**Moze:** But you seem to have misunderstood. There will be no fair one-on-one duel. Let's see how tough you are once we've put you down.

> **貊泽：**但你会错意了，在这里没有什么一对一的公平决斗。等我们俩放倒了你，希望你还能一如既往地嘴硬。

*(Begin battle against Eclipse Wolftrooper ×1, Sableclaw Wolftrooper ×4)*

**Mok Tok:** The Warhead's blood... It boils in my veins...

> **末度：**战首的血…在我体内沸腾…

**Mok Tok:** Long live Hoolay! The borisin resurgence is at hand!

> **末度：**呼雷万岁！步离人的复兴必将到来！

**Moze:** The borisin resurgence?

> **貊泽：**「步离人的复兴」？

**Moze:** You think you can still pillage and murder freely as you did a thousand years before?

> **貊泽：**你们以为自己还能像千年前那样到处侵略和屠戮？

**Moze:** The Xianzhou Yaoqing will ensure that this is nothing more than your dying fantasy!

> **貊泽：**曜青仙舟将确保那只是你们死前最不切实际的幻想！

*(After Mok Tok is defeated)*

*(Mok Tok resurrects and immediately enters Moon Rage state)*

**Mok Tok:** Fight me! You think a scrape like this will send me running!?

> **末度：**来啊，这点小伤！我绝不退却！

**Mok Tok:** I must... hold on...

> **末度：**我必须…撑下去…

*(After Mok Tok is defeated again)*

**Mok Tok:** The borisin resurgence... shall come...

> **末度：**步离人的复兴…会来的…

*(After the battle)*

**Moze:** No matter how heroic your sacrifice... there is no honor in your battles or deaths, borisin.

> **貊泽：**无论牺牲得有多壮烈…步离人，你们的战斗和死亡都没有一丝荣誉可言。

**Hoolay:** It seems that Mok Tok got what he wished for.

> **呼雷：**看来，末度得偿所愿了。

**Moze:** Hoolay... your "declaration of war" is over.

> **貊泽：**呼雷…你的「宣战」计划完蛋了。

**Moze:** From now on, no matter where you run, General Feixiao will catch you and send you to the same fate as Mok Tok...

> **貊泽：**接下来，不管你逃到何处，飞霄将军都会逮到你，送你步上末度的后尘。

**Hoolay:** Your tongue is sharper than your claws.

> **呼雷：**你的嘴皮子可比爪子锋利多了。

**Hoolay:** Don't stand in my way, child. Have your general speak with me. Our little hunting game is not over yet.

> **呼雷：**别碍事，小子，让你的将军来和我说话，我和她的狩猎游戏还没结束呢。

**Feixiao:** Stand down, Moze. I am here, Hoolay. What do you wish to say?

> **飞霄：**退下吧，貊泽。我就在这儿，呼雷，你还有什么想说的？

**Hoolay:** The Merlin's Claw, though we have never met on the battlefield, I have heard many interesting things about you from both my men and your healer.

> **呼雷：**天击将军，虽然咱们从未在战场上谋面。但这一路走来，我从属下和你的医士口中听到了不少关于你的有趣传闻。

**Hoolay:** The people of the Xianzhou must be quite brave to allow a foxian war slave with borisin blood to ascend to the position of general.

> **呼雷：**仙舟人真够胆，竟然放任一个流着步离之血的狐人战奴攀上将军的宝座。

**Hoolay:** Has no one ever questioned that lineage of yours? Or could it be that your grand achievements were enough to convince everyone to keep their lips sealed?

> **呼雷：**难道就没有人质疑过你的血统吗？还是因为你显赫的功绩让所有人暂时闭上了嘴？

**Feixiao:** Claiming to have family ties with the enemy sounds to me like an attempt at asking for mercy... Hoolay, are you begging me for mercy?

> **飞霄：**在战场上和自己的敌人攀亲道故，算得上是一种委婉的求饶…呼雷，你是在向我求饶吗？

**Hoolay:** I see that it is not only the ruthless blood of a wolf that courses through your veins, but also the cunning blood of a fox.

> **呼雷：**看来，你的血里不仅有狼的残忍，还兼具了狐的狡诈。

**Hoolay:** When you think about it, this was a gift from the borisin. Anything that we gift, we are also free to reclaim...

> **呼雷：**说到底，这是来自步离人的馈赠。我们赠予的，我们也当有权收回——

**Hoolay:** The Merlin's Claw, I shall extend a final invitation to you: I will be waiting for you on the Skysplitter.

> **呼雷：**天击将军，我向你发出最后的邀请：我会在竞锋舰上等候你的到来。

**Hoolay:** Before you arrive, I will slaughter all in sight, allowing the Crimson Moon that has fallen dim for over seven hundred years to once more come to life. I will illuminate this ship with the sheen of blood and show everyone just how weak and powerless the Xianzhou is.

> **呼雷：**在你到来之前，我将大开杀戒，让这七百年来黯淡已久的「赤月」再度跳动点燃，用血光照亮这艘众人瞩目的船，让世人认清仙舟有多么软弱无能。

**Hoolay:** Then, I will pilot this ship through every blockade and barrier you have prepared and begin my journey home. This ship shall be the flagship for the borisin resurgence!

> **呼雷：**然后我会驾驭这艘舰船，冲破一切封锁和阻拦，踏上归乡的道路，让它成为步离人再兴的旗舰。

**Hoolay:** Before I finish these tasks of mine, you have a chance to stop me, or die at my hands. This is the path that I have prepared for you.

> **呼雷：**在我完成这一切之前，你有机会阻止我，或是死在我的手中，这将是我为你准备的道路。

**Feixiao:** I accept your challenge, Hoolay. Because the very moment you stepped aboard the Skysplitter, you stepped onto your path to ruin.

> **飞霄：**我接受你的挑战，呼雷。因为从踏上竞锋舰的这一刻起，你已经走上了一条绝路。

Switching to Yunli's POV...

On the Skysplitter, the atmosphere of an impending storm makes Yunli anxious and uneasy...

## 030　I Shall Turn the Tide　挽澜扶危，舍我其谁

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Skysplitter　|　角色：Yunli; March 7th; Soldier on Duty; Yanqing; Hoolay; Huaiyan; Jing Yuan　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''Last Stand of a Lone Wolf'' completed

### Check the state of security aboard the Skysplitter

> *Aboard the Skysplitter, the tense atmosphere and anxiety from waiting are starting to make Yunli uneasy. She decides to conduct a thorough patrol of the Skysplitter before the tournament begins to ensure that nothing untoward is happening.*

The Wardance is about to commence Skysplitter

**Yunli:** I'm entrusting you not only with the honor of the ring, but also with the security of the Skysplitter.

> **云璃：**「我要托付你们的绝不只是擂台上的胜负荣辱，还有竞锋舰的安全。」

**Yunli:** That's easy for my grandfather to say, but this mission doesn't seem so simple anymore.

> **云璃：**爷爷说的倒是轻松，尽给我出些难题。

**Yunli:** The first round of the tournament is about to begin... I wonder how Yanqing and the others are doing?

> **云璃：**第一场擂赛马上就要开始了…不知彦卿那边是否一切顺利？

**Yunli:** According to the plan, I should seek out the Cloud Knight soldiers aboard the Skysplitter and see if they've discovered anything.

> **云璃：**按照计划，我也该找竞锋舰上的云骑士兵问问，看看他们有什么发现。

### Seek information from the Cloud Knights

**Yunli:** Maybe I should take a moment to meet with March before the tournament starts?

> **云璃：**擂赛开始前，要不抽空跟三月见个面？

**Yunli:** March is in the contestant's lounge right now. I wonder how her preparations are coming along...

> **云璃：**三月现在就待在选手休息室里，也不知道她准备得如何了……

### (Optional) Check up on March 7th

*(Investigate the door, optional)*

**Yunli:** March, it's me, Yunli. Are you okay?

> **云璃：**三月，是我，云璃。你还好吗？

**March 7th:** Aghhhhhhhh...!

> **三月七：**呜哇啊啊啊啊——

**March 7th:** Ooh, ow-ow-owww. Just a moment, I'm coming!

> **三月七：**哎哟，疼疼疼疼，稍等一下，我马上过来。

**Yunli:** Ah... She's still a nervous wreck.

> **云璃：**哎…慌慌张张的。

**Yunli:** You still seem nervous.

> **云璃：**感觉你有点紧张了。

**March 7th:** I wasn't this nervous before... It's just being alone in this lounge, I can't stop thinking about everything. I mean, not only are we taking part in the Wardance, but we have such an important mission to carry out as well...

> **三月七：**原本我也没有这么紧张的，但一个人待在休息室里就会越想越多。毕竟这一次参加演武仪典，咱们的身上还背负着这么重的任务……

**Yunli:** March, calm down.

> **云璃：**三月，冷静一点。

- ▸ Don't worry about your mission. I am here to help you.

> ▸ 别担心你的任务，还有我帮你。

**Yunli:** Don't worry about your mission. You have me here too, right? I know my grandfather said that you must "represent the Luofu as the ringmaster," but don't let that intimidate you.

> **云璃：**别担心你的任务。你不是还有我吗。别被爷爷「代表罗浮守擂」的出战名头给唬住了。

**Yunli:** Ask yourself: You are a Nameless of the Astral Express. You have always been free to come and go as you please. So, why did you board the Skysplitter with me when my grandfather gave us this mission?

> **云璃：**问问你自己，你是星穹列车无名客，一向来去自由，但为什么在爷爷把任务交给我们后，你和我一起登上了这竞锋舰？

**March 7th:** Why...? Because I also want the Wardance to go smoothly.

> **三月七：**要说为什么的话…因为我也希望，演武仪典能顺利召开。

**March 7th:** For us Nameless, even though we're just visitors at every stop... It was here that I met my two masters and made many new friends. I want the Wardance to go ahead without any problems, for everyone's sake.

> **三月七：**对无名客来说，虽然每一站我们都只是过客…可在这里我结识了两位师父，也认识了许多朋友。为了大家，我希望这场「演武仪典」能一切顺利。

**Yunli:** Good answer! The tournament is about to begin, so I must go carry out the mission that Grandpa gave me.

> **云璃：**答的好！擂赛马上就要开始了，我必须去完成爷爷交付的任务了。

**Yunli:** March, may your blade be sharp and your victory glorious.

> **云璃：**三月，祝你宝剑常锋，登擂夺胜。

**March 7th:** ...Thank you, Master Yunli.

> **三月七：**…谢谢你，云璃师父。

### Seek information from the Cloud Knights

**Yunli:** According to Grandpa's plan, I should seek out the Cloud Knight soldiers aboard the Skysplitter and see if there've been any new developments...

> **云璃：**好不容易让她没有那么紧张了，现在就不去打扰她了。

**Yunli:** It took enough time to calm her nerves, it's best I don't disturb her again now.

> **云璃：**按照爷爷的安排，现在应该问问竞锋舰上的云骑士兵，有没有新的情况……

*(Talk to the soldier on duty)*

**Soldier on Duty:** Is that you, Miss Yunli? If there is anything that I can help you with, please let me know.

> **执勤士兵：**这不是云璃小姐吗？如果有什么事情需要帮助的，请告诉在下。

- ▸ How are the Cloud Knights doing?

> ▸ 云骑的布置情况怎么样了？

- ▸ Have you noticed any "wolves"?

> ▸ 你有发现有「狼」的情况吗？

**Soldier on Duty:** We haven't noticed anything out of the ordinary yet.

> **执勤士兵：**眼下我们还没有注意到异常情况。

**Yunli:** Very good. Although, there is one thing I want to confirm with you...

> **云璃：**做得很好，不过我还得和你再次确认一下——

- ▸ Do you have a way of identifying borisin?

> ▸ 你们有识别步离人的手段吗？

**Soldier on Duty:** Unfortunately, we don't have any means to distinguish between borisin and foxians.

> **执勤士兵：**很遗憾，我们目前没有能区分步离人与狐人的手段。

**Soldier on Duty:** However, you can rest assured that the Cloud Knights are working hard to investigate the source of the forged identities...

> **执勤士兵：**不过可以肯定的是，关于伪造身份，云骑正在抓紧排查溯源……

- ▸ Tell me about how the Cloud Knights are currently deployed.

> ▸ 讲讲眼下云骑的部署规划？

**Soldier on Duty:** All Cloud Knights on the Skysplitter have their own responsibilities and wouldn't dare neglect their posts.

> **执勤士兵：**竞锋舰上全体云骑各司其职，不敢怠慢。

**Yunli:** March 7th and I could already tell. It's clear that every Cloud Knight soldier we've met on the Skysplitter is an elite warrior.

> **云璃：**我和三月七已经感受到了，来到竞锋舰上的云骑士兵们个个都是精锐。

- ▸ I would like to confirm if there have been any irregularities in boarding.

> ▸ 我想确认有无异常登舰的情况。

**Soldier on Duty:** Your timing is perfect, Miss Yunli. We actually just received an emergency boarding request.

> **执勤士兵：**云璃小姐来得正是时候，就在刚才，我们收到了一个紧急登舰请求。

**Yunli:** An emergency boarding request?

> **云璃：**紧急登舰请求？

**Soldier on Duty:** It wasn't on the Skysplitter starskiff registry, but all the documentation checked out.

> **执勤士兵：**它不在抵达竞锋舰的星槎航班表上，不过星槎上本身通牒文件都是正式的。

**Yunli:** Who would be boarding the ship at this time? Where are they? I'll go check it out.

> **云璃：**谁会在这个时候登舰？位置在哪，我去检查一下。

**Soldier on Duty:** The starskiff is using the contestant docking bay. Would you like me to lead the way?

**Yunli:** No need. You have your own duties to take care of. I'll be fine by myself.

### Inspect the suspicious starskiff

> *Aboard the Skysplitter, the tense atmosphere and anxiety from waiting are starting to make Yunli uneasy. She decides to conduct a thorough patrol of the Skysplitter before the tournament begins to ensure that nothing untoward is happening. The late arrival of a starskiff arouses Yunli's wariness.*

**Yunli:** (I have to check on this immediately.)

> **云璃：**不用了，你还有执勤的任务，我一个人去就行。

*(Attempt to leave the area, optional)*

**Yunli:** (I have to check on this immediately.)

> **云璃：**（我得赶紧去确认一下。）

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Yunli:** Routine inspection! All passengers please prepare for an inspection.

> **云璃：**例行检查！请登舰乘客立刻接受检查。

**Yunli:** (So, this is the suspicious starskiff? Where did the passengers go...?)

> **云璃：**（这就是可疑的星槎？乘船的人呢……）

**Yunli:** I'm going to count down from ten. If you do not come out, I will destroy the ship.

> **云璃：**我数十个数，再不出来，我就把船砸了哦？

**Yunli:** Ten!

> **云璃：**十！

**Yunli:** Nine, eight, seven, six, five, four... Time is running out!

> **云璃：**九、八、七、六、五、四…时间要到了哦！

**Yunli:** Three! Two!

> **云璃：**三！二！

**Yunli:** One!

> **云璃：**一！

**???:** Wait a second!

> **？？？：**等等！

**Yanqing:** It's me.

> **彦卿：**是我。

**Yanqing:** It's just a routine inspection. You weren't actually going to blow up the ship, were you? Is that also some kind of Zhuming custom...?

> **彦卿：**例行检查而已，你不会真的要把船砸了吧？难道这也是你们朱明传统……

- ▸ What are you doing back here!?

> ▸ 你怎么回来了！？

- ▸ You're not a borisin in disguise, are you?

> ▸ 该不会是步离人伪装的吧？

**Yanqing:** Wait! Even though the borisin can disguise themselves, they can't turn into someone as short as me! Put your sword away.

> **彦卿：**等等，就算步离人变形伪装，也变不了我这样的小个子吧！快把剑收起来。

**Yunli:** Didn't little Yanqing claim he was going to give up being the ringmaster and go on the hunt instead? Help shoulder the general's burdens?

> **云璃：**咱们的彦卿小弟不是说要放弃守擂，选择猎狼，为将军分忧解难吗？

**Yanqing:** The borisin on the ground suddenly launched an attack. General Feixiao was concerned that this was all a diversion by Hoolay, so I requested to come to the Skysplitter and provide support.

> **彦卿：**地面上步离人突然展开了袭击，飞霄将军担心这是呼雷的障眼法，于是我就主动请命赶来驰援竞锋舰。

**Yunli:** I can't believe you ended up back here on the Skysplitter after running around all over the place. But, you're too late to enter the tournament now.

> **云璃：**没想到你兜兜转转，又回到了竞锋舰上，不过此刻擂台你是上不了啦。

**Yunli:** Following Grandpa's plan, our disciple March is taking your place as ringmaster... Aren't you proud of her?

> **云璃：**按照爷爷的安排，咱们俩的好徒弟三月会代替你登台守擂——是不是感到很骄傲啊？

**Yanqing:** When I first heard the news, I was definitely proud, but also a little concerned...

> **彦卿：**乍一听到这么有冲击性的事实，还真是又自豪，又让人担心来着。

**Yunli:** More manpower is always a good thing, since the Skysplitter is so large. Oh, and by the way, you should know that—

> **云璃：**多一个人手总是好事，竞锋舰这么大，对了，你应该知道——

**Yanqing:** What's happening!?

> **彦卿：**怎么回事？！

**Yunli:** Does it feel like the Skysplitter has slowed down? Could it be...

> **云璃：**竞锋舰的速度似乎在变慢？莫非……

**Yunli:** Come with me, we need to check the engine room! It's the heart of the ship, so we need to prioritize its safety!

> **云璃：**跟我走，咱们去引擎室瞧瞧！作为整艘舰船的核心设施，我们优先确保那儿的安全！

### Search the engine room and discover the cause of the malfunction

> *Aboard the Skysplitter, the tense atmosphere and anxiety from waiting are starting to make Yunli uneasy. > **云璃：**古怪，这附近怎么一个人都不见了？

> **？？？：**你们也应该和他们躺在一起……

> **伪装的狐人：**放心吧小娃娃，这里的空间还很宽敞，有足够藏下你们尸体的地方。

**Yanqing:** Even though I truly hoped that the Wardance would go ahead uninterrupted and that I was worrying for no reason...

> **彦卿：**虽说打心眼里希望这场「演武仪典」能一切平安无事，让我虚惊一场……

**Yunli:** Now that these guys have finally shown up, it's a huge weight off my mind. Bring it on, you abominations!

> **云璃：**但这帮家伙终于还是来了啊，我心尖上压着的这块石头总算也能落地了！来吧，孽物！

*(Begin battle against Eclipse Wolftrooper ×2, Sableclaw Wolftrooper ×2)*

**Yanqing:** If we found two wolves in the engine room, that means that even more abominations have infiltrated other areas... Hoolay is already aboard the Skysplitter!

> **彦卿：**引擎舱里发现了两只狼，就意味着其他的地方会有更多孽物潜入…呼雷已经登上了竞锋舰！

**Yunli:** There's no time to talk about this. The Wardance has already started! If my guess is correct, the borisin will most likely use the ceremony to begin their slaughter.

> **云璃：**没时间解释了，「演武仪典」应该已经开始了！如果我猜的没错，在步离人登舰之后，他们极有可能在仪典上大肆杀戮。
