### Witness the two generals' conversation

> *A while ago at the Seat of Divine Foresight, the generals were discussing the Wardance's security...*

Three dual-hours before the Wardance convenes Seat of Divine Foresight.

**Huaiyan:** Surround your enemy, but leave one side open... This is your strategy for ensuring that the Wardance will be able to be held as usual?

> **怀炎：**「围三阙一」…这就是你所说的，能确保演武仪典如常进行的计划吗？

**Jing Yuan:** Precisely. When Hoolay escaped, we should have canceled the Wardance immediately. No one can predict where a wild beast like him will attack, yet festivals and celebrations like this are likely to become targets.

> **景元：**正是。呼雷出逃，本该立刻终止演武仪典的举行。谁也不能预料这头凶兽会袭向哪里，似仪典这样的节庆，反而更容易成为他逞凶的目标。

**Jing Yuan:** However, preparations for the Wardance were in the works for so long, I couldn't bear to announce its cancellation. So, I thought, if the show must go on, then let it go on. But that doesn't mean that the Wardance we hold today has to be the real event.

> **景元：**不过，演武仪典筹备良久，势在必行，宣布中断又让我心疼得很。我转念一想，既然不得不开，那还是开吧。只是今日召开的又何必是真正的演武仪典呢？

**Jing Yuan:** All we need to do is swap all the spectators and contestants aboard the ship with Cloud Knight soldiers, and the Skysplitter, soaring high above, will become the ultimate battleground for our hunt.

> **景元：**只要将登舰观众与选手全数替换成云骑士兵，高悬在空中的那座竞锋舰，将会成为绝佳的猎狼战场。

**Huaiyan:** ...But what do we do if Hoolay is not planning to attack the Wardance?

> **怀炎：**…可要是呼雷没有打算袭击演武仪典，又该怎么办呢？

**Jing Yuan:** Then we just need to draw his attention to the Skysplitter. We'll have the Sky-Faring Commission clear all transport lanes and cut down starskiff traffic. Then, when the time comes, there will be only one ship in the sky, and he will have no reason not to attack.

> **景元：**那就尽可能让他看见竞锋舰。天舶司会清空航道，减少其他星槎通航。这样一来，当天上只剩下一艘船时，他便有了不得不去的理由。

**Jing Yuan:** General Feixiao will be in charge of scaring the wolves and cutting off their other means of escape, making him think that this will be his best way out.

> **景元：**而飞霄将军会负责敲山震狼，切断步离人逃亡的路线，让他自以为找到了去处。

**Jing Yuan:** Given that the borisin already have insiders on the Luofu, I will be in charge of putting pressure on them, making them focus solely on keeping themselves safe.

> **景元：**考虑到步离人在罗浮上有内应，我会负责前去施加压力，令内应们疲于自保。

> **景元：**能有炎老担当这最后的保险，晚辈就放心了。

Not long ago...

**Jiaoqiu:** ...

**Hoolay:** Secrets are a weapon that a hunter cannot live without. Those that have no secrets are no more than prey, cut open and waiting for death.

> **椒丘：**这么说来，在你眼中，我已经是一头毫无秘密可言、随时待死的猎物了？

> **呼雷：**难道你还有其他的路可逃么？椒丘，我已将你的伪装和防御层层撕开，你和你所侍奉的将军，你们深藏的秘密，我已了如指掌。

> **椒丘：**可是战首也将自己的秘密告诉了我，我已了如指掌。

> **呼雷：**你永远也用不上它了。你会和它一同埋葬在这儿。不过，你始终是个幸运的家伙……

I've already told you the answer.

**Jiaoqiu:** ...Those who have no secrets... are no more than prey... cut open and waiting for death...

> **椒丘：**…没有秘密的人…只是被撕开皮肉，悲惨等死的猎物。

> **椒丘：**尽情痛饮我的鲜血吧…呼雷。只可惜，我不是一个没有秘密的人啊。我还藏着一个微不足道的秘密……

**Jiaoqiu:** If the most lethal poison known to this world... is able to save innocent lives... Then it can also be called a great medicine...

> **椒丘：**世间至毒…若能换回无辜的生命，便也可被称作良药……

**Jiaoqiu:** I will do my best to cure you... Feixiao, I kept my promise. At the cost of this insignificant life...

> **椒丘：**「我将尽力医治」…飞霄，我兑现承诺了。用这条微不足道的性命……

**Jiaoqiu:** ...bring victory to the Yaoqing.

> **椒丘：**…为「曜青」带来胜利。

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Hoolay:** So... you want to play dirty?

> **呼雷：**真是…不择手段啊。

> **呼雷：**空气…变冷了？

**Yanqing:** My sword... is even colder.

> **彦卿：**我的剑…更冷。

*(Cutscene ends)*

*(Begin battle against Borisin Warhead: Hoolay ×20)*

> **云璃：**呼雷的动作变迟钝了？

**Yanqing:** This is our chance!

> **彦卿：**别放过眼前的机会！

**March 7th:** It's time for us to put this dog down!

> **三月七：**咱们一起，让他屁股向后，狗头着地！

*(After Hoolay's HP drops to 80%)*

**Hoolay:** Jiaoqiu, you—!

> **呼雷：**椒丘，你竟然…！

**Hoolay:** A pathetic little poison like this... You think this is enough to turn the tide of battle?

> **呼雷：**一剂毒药罢了…以为靠这样的手段能扭转战局？

*(After Hoolay's HP drops to 50%)*

> **呼雷：**我确实变慢了…

**Hoolay:** But that does not mean you can defeat me...

> **呼雷：**可这不代表你们能赢……

*(After Hoolay's HP drops to 20%)*

> **彦卿：**你确实很难杀死，但绝不代表你无法被制服！

**Yanqing:** Hoolay! Take a good look. The power of the Cloud Knights is in my blade!

> **彦卿：**呼雷！看仔细了，云骑天威，尽在此剑！

*(After Hoolay's HP drops to 1%)*

*(Cutscene begins)*

> **呼雷：**怎么可能？区区幼崽…怎么可能打败我？！

## 032　Under the Spell　霜刃飞光，天狐迷狂

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Skysplitter　|　角色：Yanqing; March 7th; Feixiao; Yunli; Hoolay; Moze　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''Comrade in Arms'' completed

### Deal with Hoolay

> *When steel finally touches flesh, the young swordmaster emerges victorious at last, thanks to the swordsmanship skills that she has gained through diligent training and countless defeats. It is time to put the wolves behind bars once more.*

### (Optional) Discuss the current situation with your companions

*(Talk to Feixiao, optional)*

**Feixiao:** This hunt is finally over. Thank you for holding out so long. The Cloud Knights are withdrawing as we speak.

> **飞霄：**亏你们几个能撑到现在。这场狩猎总算是圆满收场了，云骑军正在善后撤离中。

**Feixiao:** Lieutenant Yanqing, how are you holding up?

> **飞霄：**彦卿骁卫，你身体还撑得住吗？

- ▸ I could take on ten more!

> **彦卿：**我好得很…一点事都没有！就算再来十个呼雷……

> **飞霄：**别太勉强了，年轻人，你看起来伤得不轻啊。等丹鼎司的医士到场，让他们好好给你治治。

- ▸ General, how are things on the ground?

**Yanqing:** General... is everything okay on the ground?

> **彦卿：**将军…地面上一切还好吗？

**Feixiao:** Everything's fine. We were able to suppress the borisin attacks. When I sensed that Hoolay was not on the ground, I raced to get here as quickly as I could.

> **飞霄：**嗯，我尽力镇压了步离人的袭扰。当我察觉到呼雷不在地面上时，我就争分夺秒地赶来这儿了。

- ▸ You saved my life there.

**Yanqing:** I... I thought I was done for.

> **彦卿：**我…我以为我要死了。

**Feixiao:** I wouldn't want General Jing Yuan to lose the disciple that brings him so much pride. When I sensed that Hoolay was not on the ground, I raced to get here as quickly as I could.

> **飞霄：**我可不想让景元将军失去他的得意弟子。当我察觉到呼雷不在地面上时，我就争分夺秒地赶来这儿了。

- ▸ I can't believe you didn't tell me the plan...

**Yanqing:** ...General, I can't believe you didn't tell me about the plan for the Skysplitter!

> **彦卿：**…将军，你竟然没把竞锋舰上的计划告诉我！

**Feixiao:** Don't blame me, this was your general's idea. When he learned that you gave up being the ringmaster to join my Wolf Hunt team, he insisted that I hide the plan from you. Perhaps he thought your impatience would mess things up.

**Feixiao:** Well? You may have run all over the place, but you ended up back here, did you not?

> **飞霄：**没想到，生平第一次，我在战场上迟到了。不，也许该说是你们几个年轻人的剑太快了些。

**Feixiao:** Who would have imagined that I would arrive late to the battlefield for the first time in my life? ...No, perhaps you young ones' blades were just too fast for me.

> **飞霄：**我本以为最好的战况不过是你们几个能联手拖住这头凶兽，自保不失。但…你们合力击败了呼雷，真是后生可畏。

**Feixiao:** I originally thought, in the best-case scenario, you would team up and hold this beast back while protecting yourselves. However... You joined forces and defeated Hoolay! The youth are truly to be feared and respected!

**Feixiao:** Come, we need to lock up Hoolay once again before anything unexpected happens.

> **飞霄：**不必挣扎了，呼雷。回到幽囚狱，你有足够的时间被懊悔折磨。

*(Talk to Yunli or March 7th, optional)*

**March 7th:** I—I was so scared! I thought you were a goner there! We're so lucky the general made it in time!

**Yunli:** Yanqing, that was amazing! The final blow you dealt to Hoolay, how have I never seen that before?

**Yunli:** I hate to say it, but if you can teach me that move, I'll maybe admit that you're just a tiny little bit stronger than me...

- ▸ Was it really us who defeated Hoolay?

**March 7th:** It must have been our awe-inspiring righteousness that struck fear into his heart!

> **三月七：**怎么办？咱们几个是打还是逃？

**Yunli:** You really are an overthinker! What's the point of worrying about these things? Who cares why he started to weaken? All that matters is that we won!

## 033　Salvation by Starlance　欃枪烈烈，心兽灭却

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Skysplitter　|　角色：March 7th; Yanqing; Yunli; Feixiao; Jiaoqiu; Hoolay; Huaiyan; Jing Yuan; Neergul; Lan

### Witness Hoolay's devious plans

> *For the Father Wolf who leads the pack, the possibility of returning empty-handed must always be taken into consideration when planning each and every hunt...*

**Hoolay:** I have looked forward to this for so long. Our little hunting game has finally reached its climax... I told you before that I had a path prepared for you...

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Hoolay:** You've doomed yourselves...

**Hoolay:** As I meet my demise, so shall the entire Xianzhou Luofu!

**Hoolay:** The "crimson moon" in my chest will make this place shine with blood!

**Hoolay:** I will drive all foxians to madness in their fear... craving slaughter... without end!

**Hoolay:** What now, Feixiao...?

**Yunli:** ...What is this!?

**Feixiao:** This is the "heart" of the Borisin Warhead, a product of the plaguemark.

**Feixiao:** I'll leave the next part to you.

**Yanqing:** General, I don't understand...

**Feixiao:** We cannot allow this thing to spread. I will do all within my power to consume this "crimson moon."

**Feixiao:** Once the situation changes... Lieutenant Yanqing, you are to enforce the Cloud Knight's military rules. Understood?

**Yanqing:** General?

*(Cutscene ends)*

### Stall the Merlin's Claw who has sunk into madness

> *In response to Hoolay's dying blow, the Merlin's Claw has no choice but to use everything in her power to confront the Crimson Moon hanging in the sky. As though she had returned to the battlefields of yore, she initiates a bloody massacre that has no end in sight. There is only one way to bring her out of madness: Stall for time.*

*(Begin battle against Maddened Feixiao ×20)*

**Feixiao:** Die, borisin!

**Yunli:** Wh—What!? We clearly don't have wolf ears!

*(During Feixiao's first action)*

**Feixiao:** I will not allow any of you... to leave the Skysplitter!

**Yunli:** Does she think we are her enemies?

**Yanqing:** Hoolay just wanted to take down the general, even at the cost of his own life!

**Yanqing:** Snap out of it, General!

*(Feixiao uses Passing Tempest)*

**March 7th:** Ugh! Just when I thought we'd get a minute to catch our breath!

**March 7th:** What do we do? Do we fight, or run?

**Yanqing:** After all this, do you still think running is an option?

*(When Feixiao is reduced to 1% HP and takes action)*

**Feixiao:** Stop trying to resist me!

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Yunli:** We can't let her get away!

**March 7th:** ...But do we stand any chance at all?

**Yanqing:** She said "I'll leave the next part to you."

**Yanqing:** She put her faith in us!

*(Cutscene ends)*

### Even now, Feixiao is still struggling on the border between madness and sanity...

**Jiaoqiu:** I promise I will cure you... Feixiao.

> **椒丘：**我一定会医好你的…飞霄。

**Feixiao:** No one can live forever, Jiaoqiu... This time, I must break my promise.

> **飞霄：**没有人能永远活着，椒丘…这一次，我要食言了。

**Feixiao:** Is this... the Skysplitter? Or somewhere else?

> **飞霄：**这里是…竞锋舰？还是其他什么地方？

**Unclear Voice:** Lieutenant Yanqing, you are to enforce the Cloud Knights' military rules. Understood?

> **模糊的声音：**彦卿骁卫，你来执行云骑的军规。明白吗？

**Unclear Voice:** We can't let her get away!

> **模糊的声音：**不能让她离开这儿！

**Unclear Voice:** Snap out of it, General!

> **模糊的声音：**快醒醒啊，将军！

**???:** You always leave the hardest battles for yourself.

> **？？？：**你总是把更艰难的战斗留给自己。

**???:** Even now, you would never run away.

> **？？？：**就像现在这样，从来不知逃避为何物。

**Hoolay:** Isn't it strange? That as your enemy, I understand you even better than your own allies?

> **呼雷：**很奇怪吧，作为敌人，我比你的战友更了解你。

**Feixiao:** Hoolay...?

> **飞霄：**呼雷…？

**Hoolay:** This rage... I remember the taste. I know that it was never victory that it thirsted for, but the hunt itself.

> **呼雷：**这怒火…真让人怀念啊。我知道，它寻求的从来不是胜利，而是狩猎本身。

**Hoolay:** Isn't that right, Feixiao?

> **呼雷：**没错吧，飞霄？

**Feixiao:** You shouldn't be here. I saw you die with my own eyes...

> **飞霄：**你不该存在的。我亲眼看到你死了……

**Hoolay:** Just consider me a figment of your imagination.

> **呼雷：**就把我当成幻觉好了。

**Hoolay:** Before every hunt, I make preparations for defeat and retreat. But not this time. This time... defeat was exactly the ending I was racing towards.

> **呼雷：**在每一次狩猎前，我都会做好失败而归的准备。但这次不一样，这一次…失败就是我奔赴的终点。

**Hoolay:** When I learned your story from Jiaoqiu, I realized... that he had always been hiding a secret... and that not even the healer himself understood the meaning of this secret.

> **呼雷：**当我从椒丘口中了解你的故事后，我发现了，他一直在隐瞒一个秘密…而这个秘密的意义，甚至连那位医士本人也未能察觉。

**Hoolay:** What an ironic fate, don't you think? All this time, waiting for this day to come, I never imagined that this was how it would turn out.

> **呼雷：**命运真是讽刺啊。我一直在等待今天，可没想到它会以这种形式到来。

**Hoolay:** ...But, I accept it.

> **呼雷：**…但我愿意接受它。

**Hoolay:** Now, let us break through the iron-clad defenses of the Arbiter-General's heart and see what secrets she has hidden in those deepest, darkest corners.

> **呼雷：**至于现在，就让我们掘开帝弓天将坚如铁石的心防，看看在她最幽暗的角落里，藏了些什么吧？

### Chase after the apparition Hoolay left behind

> *On the empty battlefield, a tenacious and freshly-dead foe makes an appearance once more. The veteran hunter is primed to go on a special hunt... but this time, she is the prey.*

*(Floating words)*

The weak

Sympathy

Justice

Xianzhou's cover-up

Doubt

The universe in its original form

*(Moving past the holograms)*

**Hoolay:** The familiar smell of death, like a vulture...

> **呼雷：**熟悉的死亡气息，就像兀鹰……

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Hoolay:** I'm sure that the people of the Xianzhou have shown you these familiar scenes countless times. But they only showed you what they wanted you to see.

> **呼雷：**这些熟悉的场面，仙舟人应该向你展示过无数次了。但这一切不过是他们想让你看到的。

**Hoolay:** They have you fight to save the weak. But, what they never told you is that when all the pretenses of kindness and compassion are stripped away, this is how the universe is meant to be.

> **呼雷：**他们让你为拯救弱者而战。但他们却有没有告诉你，剥开一切温情伪装后，这就是宇宙本来该有的样子。

**Hoolay:** When the Xianzhou is weak, they play the victim so they can win your sympathy. When they are strong, they make you their avenger and have you fight in the name of justice.

> **呼雷：**当仙舟弱小时，他们将自己伪装成受害者来博取你的同情；当仙舟强大时，他们却封你为复仇者要你秉持正义。

**Hoolay:** I've found it... This is your shadow of "doubt."

> **呼雷：**我找到了…这是你的「怀疑」。

### Destroy the wraith of suspicion

*(Attack the hologram)*

**Feixiao:** Your so-called survival of the fittest is nothing but an excuse.

> **飞霄：**你所谓的弱肉强食，不过是狡辩。

**Feixiao:** You cannot weaken my resolve, Hoolay.

> **飞霄：**你不可能动摇我，呼雷。

### Chase after the apparition Hoolay left behind

**Voice of Huaiyan:** Blinded by the fury of your Moon Rage, you slaughtered your allies... Merlin's Claw, you have betrayed the Alliance!

> **怀炎的声音：**于「月狂」之怒中肆行杀戮，令友军枉死…天击将军，你辜负了联盟的信任！

*(Floating words)*

Moon Rage

Death

Betrayal

Enemy of Xianzhou

Fear

Betrayal of trust

You're also an abomination

Monster

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Voice of Huaiyan:** Your actions are no different from those of the abominations we stand against!

> **怀炎的声音：**你的行止，与那些孽物毫无差别。

**Voice of Jing Yuan:** At the end of the day, it is the blood of the abominations that runs through your veins... One day, you will inevitably become an enemy of the Alliance. Cloud Knights, formation!

> **景元的声音：**到底是流着孽物之血…假以时日，她必定会成为联盟的敌人。云骑，列阵！

**Hoolay:** You ran into the arms of your new masters' army without hesitation. You thought that you had found freedom, respect, and recognition.

> **呼雷：**你义无反顾地投身于新主人的军队，你以为自己得到了自由、尊重和认可。

**Hoolay:** But you were wrong. They know all too well that a wolf will always be a wolf.

> **呼雷：**但你错了，他们很明白，狼永远是狼。

**Hoolay:** Now, your greatest fear is about to come true: You have become an enemy of the Xianzhou.

> **呼雷：**现在，你最大的恐惧就要成真了：成为仙舟的敌人。

**Hoolay:** This... This is your "fear."

> **呼雷：**这一处…是你的「恐惧」。

### Destroy the wraith of fear

*(Attack the hologram)*

**Feixiao:** You aim to instill terror within me...

> **飞霄：**你想在我内心种下恐惧……

**Feixiao:** Yet, all of this is nothing but a... phantom.

> **飞霄：**但这些，不过是…幻影罢了。

### Chase after the apparition Hoolay left behind

**Borisin:** You have returned... Lord Feixiao! The Warhead has returned!

> **步离人：**您回来了…飞霄大人！是战首回来了！

**Borisin:** Long live the Warhead! Lord Feixiao will grant us our future!

> **步离人：**战首万岁！飞霄大人将赐我们未来！

*(Floating words)*

Borisin Warhead

Your future

Return to the pack

A new path

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Hoolay:** It's time to return to the pack, Feixiao.

> **呼雷：**该是回巢的时候了，飞霄。

**Hoolay:** No matter how deep the hatred you bear for the borisin... No matter how much we may consider each other enemies... I will still show you this path that you never imagined was available to you, because you made a choice...

> **呼雷：**无论你对步离人怀有多么深重的仇恨，也无论你和我之间怀有多少敌意。我都会为你展示一条你从未设想的道路，因为你做出了选择——

**Hoolay:** You devoured the Crimson Moon. You are worthy to become the borisin's Warhead.

> **呼雷：**你吞下了「赤月」，你有资格成为步离人的战首。

**Feixiao:** What... did you say?

> **飞霄：**你说…什么？

**Hoolay:** The descendants of Duran who came to rescue me hoped with all their hearts that once I regained my freedom, I would be able to turn the tide of battle and change the fate of our people, allowing the borisin to return to their former glory! But this was just a dream used by someone with ulterior motives to manipulate the borisin.

> **呼雷：**他们宁可将求存的希望寄托在虚无缥缈的救主身上，却连一丝垂死搏杀的勇气和力量都没有。这样的步离人，不如让他们尽数灭亡吧！

**Hoolay:** They would rather place their hopes of survival on an imaginary savior than gain the courage and strength to fight to the death. Borisin like that deserve no more than to be sent to their deaths!

> **呼雷：**而我，一个被关押七百年的囚徒，连荣耀战死都求之不得的战犯，一旦回归族群就会成为受人摆布的傀儡。我绝不接受这样的命运！

**Hoolay:** And I, a prisoner who was held captive for over seven hundred years, a criminal of war who was deprived even an honorable death in battle, would become a puppet upon my return, at the mercy of others. This is a fate that I will never accept!

> **呼雷：**对于真正的强者而言，「狐人」或「步离」只是一个名字，真正的强者能决定自己的道路。

**Hoolay:** For those who are truly strong, "foxian" and "borisin" are no more than labels. Someone who is truly strong chooses their own path.

> **呼雷：**我将为自己开辟一条全新的道路，将决定猎群命运的权力交给你——曾经的步离战奴，未来的狐人之主。

**Hoolay:** I will create that new path for myself. I will pass the right to decide the destiny of the borisin on to you... former borisin war slave and future foxian ruler.

> **呼雷：**你的医士，椒丘，他如此忠诚地侍奉你，为了治愈你的月狂不惜一死。我告诉过他，解救之道在我手中。

### Destroy the wraith of desire

*(Attack the hologram)*

**Hoolay:** Your healer, Jiaoqiu, served you with such loyalty. He was even willing to die in order to cure your Moon Rage. I told him before that the only way to save you lay in my hands.

> **「呼雷」：**只要你点一点头，你将立刻从这场「赤月」带来的疯狂噩梦中醒来，你的身躯将焕然一新。从此，不再有疯狂困扰着你，怀疑只是心头随手拂去的尘埃，恐惧也将荡然无存。

**"Hoolay":** If you just nod your head, you will awaken from this nightmare that the Crimson Moon has inflicted on you, and your body will be born anew. From that moment, you will no longer be trapped by your rage, your doubt will be swept from your mind like dust, and your fear will naturally disappear.

**Feixiao:** So, this was the "path" that you prepared for me... To become a new you?

> **飞霄：**这就是你为我准备的「道路」…成为另一个你？

**Hoolay:** That's right, we are so similar after all. We are both monsters that were born for war and will die for war.

> **呼雷：**没错，毕竟你和我是如此相似。我们都是为战而生，也是为战而死的怪物。

**Feixiao:** ...I'm sure my answer doesn't need much explanation.

> **飞霄：**…答案不用我多说吧。

**Feixiao:** I refuse.

> **飞霄：**我拒绝。

**Hoolay:** I told you before, "foxian" and "borisin" are no more than labels! You can be whoever you want to be. You can do whatever you want to do!

> **呼雷：**我说过，狐人或步离只是一个名字。你想做谁，就可以做谁。你想做什么，就可以做什么。

**Hoolay:** Accept these truths and the borisin will bow to you. Destroy them, reduce them to dust, or teach them to become servants of the Xianzhou... You are free to do as you please.

> **呼雷：**接纳这一切，步离人将会臣服于你。毁灭他们，让他们尸骨无存，或是教化他们成为仙舟的附庸…一切凭你喜欢。

**Hoolay:** Carve your name into the Xianzhou's history books, or into the List of Archenemies. You have the final say.

> **呼雷：**你的名字将被镌刻在仙舟的史册，或是大敌名录里，一切都由你做主。

**Feixiao:** You do not understand, Hoolay. You have no idea what it is that I truly desire.

> **飞霄：**你不明白，呼雷，你根本不明白，我想要的是什么。

**Feixiao:** When I was just a child, I stained my hands with blood for the first time, in order to escape the whips and shackles of the wolf masters.

> **飞霄：**在我还是个孩子的时候，为了逃离狼头的皮鞭和枷锁，我的双手第一次沾上了血。

**Saran:** Neergul, don't stop, keep running...!

> **萨兰：**凝梨，别停下，快跑…！

**Neergul:** I can't run anymore... Saran, are they catching up?

> **凝梨：**我跑不动了…萨兰，他们追上来了吗？

**Saran:** They're still behind us, don't stop! Neergul, look up! Look at that light in the sky!

> **萨兰：**他们还在，不要停！凝梨，抬头，看天上的光！

**Saran:** That's a "shooting star"! It's shining down on us! If we just tell it our wish, we can have our freedom!

> **萨兰：**那是「流星」！流星在照耀我们，只要向它许愿，就能得到自由！

**Saran:** We will all be able to escape! We'll win our freedom and then share it with our koolur brothers and sisters!

> **萨兰：**我们所有人都能逃出去！我们会得到自由，然后把它分给更多的窟卢兄弟姐妹！

**Saran:** So, you have to run, quickly! We can do it, we can escape!

> **萨兰：**所以，快走吧！我们可以逃走的！

From the dense darkness behind, comes the fierce howls of wolves and the cries of wild beasts.

**Saran:** Neergul! Run with me!

> **萨兰：**凝梨！跟我一起跑啊！

...

**Feixiao:** We ran and ran with all the strength we had, but we ended up losing each other.

> **飞霄：**我们跑啊跑啊，用尽全力，不知彼此奔向了何方。

**Feixiao:** I escaped the hunting pack and encountered a woman that could fly... a Cloud Knight.

> **飞霄：**我逃出了猎群的追捕，遇到了一个会飞的女人…一名云骑军。

**Hoolay:** I imagine you kept your promise.

> **呼雷：**我猜你信守了自己的诺言。

**Feixiao:** Unfortunately, when I led her back in order to free my koolur companions, there was nothing left there but a bottomless pit.

> **飞霄：**很可惜，当我带着她重回故地，想要解放窟卢的同伴时，我看到大地上只留下一个望不见底的深壑。

**Hoolay:** The light of the Devilish Archer... A star of calamity that strikes the ground and leaves only devastation in its wake.

> **呼雷：**妖弓的光芒…灾星坠地，万物俱灭。

**Feixiao:** In the years that followed, I followed the trail of that light alongside my Cloud Knight allies, and saw it fall countless more times.

> **飞霄：**在随后的岁月里，我和云骑战友无数次追逐那道光的轨迹，又无数次看着它落下。

**Feixiao:** Gradually I came to understand. That light was not some shooting star that carried wishes and aspirations. Each Lux Arrow meant that countless lives in a helpless world were about to be obliterated.

> **飞霄：**渐渐地我明白了，那根本不是什么承载愿望的流星。每一道光矢的出现，意味着一个无可救药的世界和无数生灵的湮灭。

**Feixiao:** I prayed to the God of the Skybow that I would never have to witness one of these "shooting stars" fall again.

> **飞霄：**我向天弓之神祈愿，请祂不要再让我目睹「流星」坠下。

**Feixiao:** But... THEY ignored my prayers.

> **飞霄：**可是…神从未回应我。

**Hoolay:** That's right, THEY never answer us. THEY just let us loose in this world to massacre each other. That is why the fierce battle between us has raged to this day.

> **呼雷：**是啊，祂们从不回应，放任我等在世间彼此仇杀，这就是我们鏖战至今的理由。

**Hoolay:** If the gods, who never bleed nor cry, who watch silently from above, refuse to listen to the prayers of mortals, then we have no choice but to fight for our own desires!

> **呼雷：**若是无血无泪、高高在上的神明不垂听凡人的祈愿，那我们就要为自己的渴望而战！

**Hoolay:** Do you understand now, Saran... You and I are similar in more ways than you think.

> **呼雷：**你明白了吗，萨兰…你我的相似之处，远比你想得更多。

**Feixiao:** ...

> **飞霄：**……

**Hoolay:** You are a beast that was born for war and will die for war!

> **呼雷：**你是一头为战而生，为战而死的野兽！

**Feixiao:** ...Perhaps.

> **飞霄：**…也许吧。

**Feixiao:** So, what is it that you yearn for, Hoolay? Why is it that you fight?

> **飞霄：**所以，你为之而战的渴望是什么，呼雷？

**Hoolay:** I fight so that I can turn the stars above into a great wilderness for the borisin to roam.

> **呼雷：**为了让天上的众星，成为步离人放牧的原野。

**Hoolay:** What about you, Saran?

> **呼雷：**你呢，萨兰？

**Feixiao:** I fight so that one day, I will never have to witness another "shooting star" falling to the ground... So that just one more person can live and return to the Yaoqing alongside me.

> **飞霄：**为了终有一日，不再有人目睹「流星」落下…为了能多一个人活着，和我一起回到曜青。

**Hoolay:** You would resist becoming the person you were supposed to be, lower yourself from a free wolf to a domesticated fox just for... the sake of one person?

> **呼雷：**宁可抗拒你本应成为的样子，从自由的狼沦落为受人豢养的狐，只为…多一个人？

**Hoolay:** Then have it your way! I will use your fear and doubt as my nourishment. I will seize you and devour you! I will replace you and... become "Feixiao"!

> **呼雷：**那就如你所愿！我会以你的恐惧和怀疑为养料，抓住你，吞下你——我会取代你…成为「飞霄」！

**Feixiao:** We are so similar, both monsters that were born for war and will die for war... You were right when you said that, Hoolay.

> **飞霄：**「我们如此相似，是为战而生，为战而死的怪物」…你说的一点也没错，呼雷。

**Feixiao:** So, I shall pray to the heavens... even if THEY do not respond to me. I pray that the arrows of The Hunt will pierce the heart of every monster!

> **飞霄：**所以，向天祈愿吧——尽管祂们从不回应。愿「巡猎」的锋镝，贯穿怪物的心脏！

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Feixiao:** Do you see me... "shooting star"?

> **飞霄：**你看到我了吗…「流星」？

**Feixiao:** "The Knights are sworn to protect the Xianzhou."

> **飞霄：**「誓如云翳障空，卫蔽仙舟。」

**Feixiao:** I will uphold this vow... until my very last day.

> **飞霄：**我将践行此誓…直到生命最后一日。

*(Cutscene ends)*

**Shadow of "Feixiao":** Since the day I was born, the skies have cried tears of blood, and all living creatures have howled and lamented.

> **「飞霄」之影：**自我降生之日起，天空泣血，万物嚎哭。

**Shadow of "Feixiao":** I once commanded the strong and hunted the weak.

**Shadow of "Feixiao":** A wolf lurks in the heart of every borisin and foxian...

In Scalegorge Waterscape, Dan Heng and Lingsha are scheduled to meet with the Vidyadhara Preceptors...

## 034　Ebb of Past Sins　射浪偃潮，旧恨云消

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Scalegorge Waterscape　|　角色：Lingsha; Dan Heng; Taoran; Bailu; Jing Yuan　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''Under the Spell'' completed

### Go to Scalegorge Waterscape and meet with the Vidyadhara Preceptors

> *The tide of Scalegorge Waterscape flows ceaselessly, just like the aspiration of the Vidyadhara Preceptors. The arrival of Dan Heng and Lingsha will be the key to deciding the direction of this flow.*

**Lingsha:** I'd like to hear their explanations.

> **灵砂：**我想听听他们作何解释。

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Taoran:** Welcome, Miss Lingsha. I am Preceptor Taoran.

> **涛然：**龙师涛然，在此恭迎灵砂小姐。

**Lingsha:** Greetings, my honored Preceptor. It's been a while since we last touched base. I've brought a friend with me today. I believe you're already familiar with each other.

> **灵砂：**灵砂拜见龙师长老，久疏问候。妾身这一次来，还带上了一位朋友，说起来他应当不算外人。

**Dan Heng:** Taoran... Elder Taoran? I remember when I was exiled, you were on the verge of passing on. What's with this new look?

> **丹恒：**涛然…长老？我被放逐时，你已接近转世寿限。可…如今你怎么成了这般模样？

**Taoran:** All of this is thanks to your past life. Had you not been so rash, I wouldn't have had to walk such a dangerous road to free our clan, becoming this specter of my former self.

> **涛然：**这一切全都是拜您的前世之身所赐啊。若不是你当年任意妄为，我又何须为了担起举族解脱之道另寻险径，将自己变作这般不人不鬼的模样。

**Dan Heng:** ...

> **丹恒：**……

**Taoran:** Dan Heng, you've returned twice, but it seems that meeting us fossils wasn't on your agenda. Regrettable, indeed. Now here you are to meet me anyway — life does love its ironies, doesn't it?

> **涛然：**丹恒先生，您两度归来，却始终没肯赏脸与我们这些老东西会晤，实在遗憾。如今却要在这个场合下相见，真是造化弄人。

**Dan Heng:** I didn't come here with Miss Lingsha today to reminisce.

> **丹恒：**我今天随灵砂小姐来，并非为了叙旧。

**Taoran:** Indeed. When the Cauldron Master extends an invitation, it's only proper for the Preceptors to attend.

> **涛然：**说的是。司鼎大人送来请柬，龙师哪有不赴约的道理。

**Lingsha:** As the newly appointed Cauldron Master, there's been much to take care of within the Alchemy Commission. It's only now that I've had the chance to speak with you. Please forgive the delay, Elder.

> **灵砂：**灵砂忝任司鼎一职，丹鼎司里百废待兴，直到今天才能与您相谈，长老见谅。

**Lingsha:** I sent over some evidence regarding the prison break in The Shackling Prison. I trust you've already looked it over.

> **灵砂：**幽囚狱劫狱之事，我寻得一些「物证」送来，龙师一定看过了。

**Taoran:** Yes, you sent us the remains of mara-struck soldiers, a map of The Shackling Prison, and a Semblance Reversion Essence pill.

> **涛然：**嗯，你将「魔阴身的遗骸」、「幽囚狱的地图」和一枚「还尘驻形丹」送到了府上。

**Lingsha:** These are the remains of assassins who used cloudhymn magic to conceal their tracks. They aided in the borisin's escape, allowing them unimpeded passage through The Shackling Prison.

> **灵砂：**那遗骸是用云吟术隐藏行迹的刺客，在幽囚狱中助步离人一路畅行无阻。

**Lingsha:** That marked-up map is a map of prisoners' escape routes. Vidyadhara craftsmen once made blueprints for the prison's construction, so I imagine, Mr. Taoran, you have a similar map in your possession. This pill —

> **灵砂：**那张勾了线的地图则是「幽囚狱犯人逃亡的路线图」。持明巧匠曾为幽囚狱建造出谋划策，想必涛然先生手中也藏有一份同样的地图。这药丸——

**Taoran:** Is Semblance Reversion Essence. Indeed, I helped plot the prison break in The Shackling Prison.

> **涛然：**是「还尘驻形丹」。不错，幽囚狱劫狱之事，我曾出力擘画。

**Lingsha:** So the Disciples of Sanctus Medicus joining forces with the Lord Ravager to stir up trouble in the Alchemy Commission, the introduction of the Stellaron, and the Ambrosial Arbor's resurrection — all have something to do with the Vidyadhara?

> **灵砂：**所以丹鼎司中出现药王秘传与绝灭大君，祸乱丹鼎司，引入星核，令建木重生也和持明脱不了干系？

**Taoran:** That's right.

> **涛然：**不错。

- ▸ So you admit it?

> ▸ 你承认？

**Taoran:** Are you surprised? After all, both of you were sent here by the Seat of Divine Foresight as messengers, to hear my confession, weren't you?

> **涛然：**很意外吗？两位受神策府的指派，作为使者前来，不就是想听我俯首自白吗？

- ▸ This is not unexpected, but it still brings me pain.

> ▸ 虽不意外，但我仍感痛心。

**Taoran:** You were sent here by the general to hear my confession, weren't you? If there's even a shred of empathy in you, you should understand the reasons behind everything I've done.

> **涛然：**你受将军指派前来，不就是想听我俯首自白吗？若你心中存有一丝不忍，就该明白我做这一切的动机。

**Taoran:** Ever since the Sedition of Imbibitor Lunae, the population of the Vidyadhara has dwindled. With the high elder exiled, a few of us Preceptors had to do what we could to turn the tide. My methods might have been drastic, perhaps even incomprehensible, but it was all for one cause — the survival of the Vidyadhara.

> **涛然：**自饮月之乱后，持明人丁日稀。龙尊流放，我和几位龙师不得不勉力支绌，挽狂澜于既倒。方法或许有些激进，或许不被理解，但说到底都只为一件事——「持明的存续」。

**Taoran:** Yet, the Xianzhou natives zealously forbid any plaguemarks and did nothing while the Vidyadhara suffered. Dan Heng, Lingsha, as Vidyadhara, you should understand the desperation behind my actions. All I've tried to do is to ensure our survival.

> **涛然：**奈何仙舟人一意禁绝寿瘟祸迹，对持明的苦难袖手旁观。丹恒，灵砂，身为持明的你们，应该了解我的苦心。我所做的不过是求生二字罢了。

**Lingsha:** I'm well aware of the challenges the Vidyadhara face. Fighting for survival isn't a sin.

> **灵砂：**我何尝不知持明所面临的问题。求生也并非罪过。

**Lingsha:** But Elder, your actions have gone beyond mere survival... You've become a beast driven solely by the Propagation, devoid of even a hint of empathy.

> **灵砂：**但是长老的所为，超出了求生者应行的界限…犹如只知「繁育」的兽，却没有一丝人心慈爱可言。

**Taoran:** Oh? Despite calling ourselves a "noble draconic bloodline," at our core, we're merely bipedal beasts. The survival of our kind is paramount. If I hadn't resorted to beastly actions, the Vidyadhara might not even exist!

> **涛然：**喔？即便自称「高贵的龙脉」，我们自始至终也不过是直立行走的动物。种族存续是根本之事，我若不做兽行，持明怕是连人都做不得了！

**Taoran:** The sages show no mercy, as the old wisdom goes. When the high elder abandoned our people's survival, I had to shoulder the world's sins alone!

> **涛然：**古之所谓「圣人不仁」，龙尊放下了维系一族存续的职责，便只能由我将天下大恶归于己身！

**Taoran:** Lingsha, Dan Heng, the past is behind us, but the future of the Vidyadhara still lies within your grasp. The situation on the Xianzhou Luofu has changed rapidly. As Vidyadhara, we must unite in purpose and avoid the mistakes of the previous Imbibitor Lunae.

> **涛然：**灵砂、丹恒，往者已矣，但持明的未来仍然握在你们的手里。如今罗浮仙舟上形势遽变，我等持明原该团结一心，切莫再蹈前世饮月之过。

**Lingsha:** I was initially skeptical about the Vidyadhara's betrayal of the Alliance... But after hearing your confession, I realize that there is no point in talking about trust.

> **灵砂：**我本对罗浮持明背叛联盟一事将信将疑…但听完长老剖陈己罪，我觉得奢谈信任，并无意义。

**Lingsha:** Breaking the covenant of our alliance will bring war upon the Vidyadhara. Even if we are able to have offspring again, what difference will it make?

> **灵砂：**触犯了联盟的天条，将为持明全族带来刀兵之灾。就算重获了繁衍的可能，又能如何？

**Taoran:** Once my plan succeeds, the alliance will see the Vidyadhara as their saviors, because both parties ultimately share the same interests.

> **涛然：**恰恰相反，我所行之事，一旦功成，联盟将视持明为救星。因为联盟与持明的最终的利益是一致的。

**Lingsha:** Oh? So now that "survival" won't convince us, you want to talk about "interests"?

> **灵砂：**喔？谈论「求存」不成，长老要谈「利益」了吗？

**Taoran:** For a thousand years, the Alliance has been engaged in brutal warfare against the Denizens of Abundance, yet victory has remained beyond reach. Have you ever wondered why? Should the Alliance emerge victorious, they will inevitably become the next Denizens of Abundance. However, if they suffer defeat, the Alliance will face extinction.

> **涛然：**联盟与丰饶民血战千年，但一直未决出胜负，你们考虑过为什么吗？因为若联盟得胜，必将成为下一个丰饶民；若作战失利，联盟将陷入灭绝的地步。

**Taoran:** Thus, the Alliance has deceitfully upheld this precarious balance. However, I have a way to escape from this dilemma.

> **涛然：**故而长久以来，联盟虚伪地维持着这其中危如累卵的平衡。但我却有超脱困局之道。

**Taoran:** The Alliance's path to salvation lies within the secret of dragon transmutation!

> **涛然：**联盟的解救之道，就藏在「化龙秘法」中！

**Taoran:** Imagine if there were a way to transform other life forms into Vidyadhara. The Alliance would then possess an endless stream of soldiers and would not need to worry about casualties.

> **涛然：**想象一下，如果有一种方法能将其他生命转化为持明。联盟便有了源源不绝的士兵，无需为死伤减员所苦。

**Taoran:** Once the war ended, these soldiers would cease reproduction, eliminating concerns of overpopulation. This is the way to salvation for the Xianzhou Alliance, the ultimate path to end the universe's suffering under the immortality plague. Only we, the Vidyadhara, can achieve this!

> **涛然：**一旦战争结束，这些士兵也不会继续繁衍，无需担忧其泛滥成灾。这就是「仙舟联盟」的解救之法，令寰宇断绝寿瘟之苦的上上善道。唯我持明可以做到！

**Lingsha:** So, you relied on this rhetoric to manipulate the ignorant members of the Vidyadhara into serving you?

> **灵砂：**…长老就是靠这个说辞，说动持明族中那些懵懂无知的人为你卖命吗？

**Lingsha:** I am deeply disappointed in you, Elder. Your thoughts and intentions are completely devoid of empathy. Cloud Knights!

> **灵砂：**灵砂，对您失望之极。您的所思所想已是非人。云骑！

**Taoran:** Hold it!

> **涛然：**慢着！

**Dan Heng:** Miss Bailu!

> **丹恒：**白露小姐！

**Bailu:** Mr. Dan Heng, and Sister Lingsha! Everyone's... here?

> **白露：**丹恒先生，还有灵砂姐姐！大家…都在这儿啊？

**Taoran:** You think I don't know why you're really here? Jing Yuan sent you here to test me, didn't he?

> **涛然：**你以为我不知道，你们送信的来意？是景元要你们来试探我的吧？

**Taoran:** According to the oath between the Alliance and the Vidyadhara, it's forbidden to spill Vidyadhara blood on their own territory. Are you all ready to violate this oath in the presence of the Luofu's high elder?

> **涛然：**按联盟与持明的盟誓，不得在持明领地中令持明流血受伤。各位要在罗浮龙尊的见证下破弃盟誓？

**Lingsha:** Dragon Lady, there's no need to panic. Elder, the Vidyadhara sacred grounds and the high elder are not to be used as pawns in exchange for your life!

> **灵砂：**龙女大人切莫惊慌。长老，持明圣地和龙尊不是握在你手中的保命符！

**Dan Heng:** You talk a big game, as if you have the nation and people's best interests at heart. But when it really matters, you use a small girl as your shield. It's ridiculously pitiful.

> **丹恒：**长老一路高谈阔论，看似忧国忧民，头头是道，结果最后关头还是将一个小女孩来当作保命符，实在是可笑可悯。

**Taoran:** I've said it before, survival is never a sin. We are all Vidyadhara, so you should understand my intentions. And as a Vidyadhara, I will leave for you a dignified solution.

> **涛然：**我已经说过了，求生从来不是罪过。各位与我都是持明，本该理解我的苦心。我身为持明，也为诸位留下和而不同的体面法子。

**Taoran:** Leave the Scalegorge Waterscape immediately, and appeal to the top echelons of the Alliance. Let the Six Charioteers hold a trial and sentence me to exuviation and rebirth.

> **涛然：**各位现在退出鳞渊境，上书联盟高层，由六御公审降罪，处我以褪鳞之刑，转世重生。

- ▸ Exuviation?

> ▸ 褪鳞之刑？

- ▸ You wish for rebirth?

> ▸ 你想转世重生？

**Dan Heng:** I see right through your plans, Mr. Taoran. Vidyadhara rebirth erases past crimes... but I'm well aware of the Preceptors' dirty tricks during rebirth. You might not be you, but you will still be you.

> **丹恒：**涛然先生的如意算盘我倒是一清二楚。持明转生，前世之罪一笔勾销…但龙师们在转世过程中所做的龌龊手脚我也不是不清楚。你虽不是你，但你还会是你。

**Dan Heng:** I won't forgive myself for past sins under the pretext of "knowing nothing," nor will I allow you to evade accountability.

> **丹恒：**我不会借口「一无所知」而轻易为自己宽宥往罪，也不会任由你们乘隙脱责。

**Taoran:** Dan Heng, I've always disliked your inability to see the bigger picture. Rebirth hasn't changed you one bit.

> **涛然：**丹恒，我自来讨厌你的不识时务，转世重生，你一点也没变。

**Dan Heng:** Miss Bailu, as high elder of the Luofu Vidyadhara, please share your thoughts at this moment.

> **丹恒：**白露小姐，您贵为罗浮持明的龙尊，请告诉我，您此刻的想法。

**Bailu:** I... I don't want to stay here. I'm tired of being pushed around! I'm not some puppet to be controlled! Please, take me away with you!

> **白露：**我…我不想留在这儿。我不想再被人差来遣去！我不是任人摆布的娃娃！请你们带我走！

**Dan Heng:** I understand.

> **丹恒：**我明白了。

**Dan Heng:** As the oath dictates, members of the Alliance are forbidden from harming Vidyadhara here. But I've long since severed ties with the Alliance. Right now I'm simply a Nameless, free to come and go as I please.

> **丹恒：**按盟誓所言，联盟之人不得在此杀伤持明。但我早已不是联盟的子民，我只是一个来去自由的无名客。

**Dan Heng:** The oath of the Xianzhou means nothing to the spear in my hand!

> **丹恒：**仙舟的盟誓，管不了我手中的枪！

*(Cutscene begins)*

### Defeat the Vidyadhara Preceptors' lackeys

*(Begin battle against Disciples of Sanctus Medicus: Shape Shifter ×1, Abundance Sprite: Golden Hound ×2)*

**Taoran:** Get them! Do whatever it takes! Make sure they don't leave here alive!

> **涛然：**动手！尽你们所能！我要这几个人有来无回！

### Settle the crimes of the Vidyadhara Preceptors

*(After the battle)*

**Lingsha:** Dragon Lady, are you alright?

> **灵砂：**龙女大人，你没事吧。

**Bailu:** I... I'm fine. Thank you for rescuing me.

> **白露：**我…一切还好。谢谢几位把我救了回来。

**Taoran:** You... you actually came here in person!?

> **涛然：**你…你居然亲自来了？！

**Jing Yuan:** Otherwise, I wouldn't be able to hear your lofty opinions.

> **景元：**若不如此，便不能听到长老的高论。

**Jing Yuan:** The public trial you seek with the Six Charioteers is inevitable. In addition, I'll write to the Seiche Queller of the Fanghu. With her hatred for evil, I'm sure she will make a just decision.

> **景元：**你要的六御公审，自是逃不脱的。除此之外，我会致函方壶的伏波将军，以她嫉恶如仇的性子，想必会做出更加公允的处置吧。

**Lingsha:** If it's her, she will earn the respect and assent of all the Vidyadhara on the Luofu.

> **灵砂：**如果是那位大人，定能让罗浮上下的持明族心服口服。

**Taoran:** ...In terms of martial prowess, you indeed have the upper hand. But if you're under the impression that this advantage can be used to settle the score with us Preceptors, that's quite an overestimation.

> **涛然：**…论武力将军确实更胜一筹。但若是将军以为能就此对我们龙师进行一番清算，倒也不必想得如此美好。

**Taoran:** Given the Vidyadhara's foundation within the Alliance, do you really think you can do anything to us?

> **涛然：**凭着持明在联盟中的根基，你真以为能对我们做些什么？

**Taoran:** As I mentioned, I'll take all the blame and become the scapegoat.

> **涛然：**就像我一开始说的那样。我会揽下所有罪过…成为替罪羊。

**Taoran:** With the secrets I possess, the interrogation alone will take an eternity. And in the end, through various exchanges of interests, I am certain to survive. You understand this better than anyone, as it's the very art of "trade-offs" you love to wield.

> **涛然：**凭我所知的秘密，光是审讯就要花上许久许久。到最后出于种种利益交换，我一定会活下来。这一点你比我更明白，毕竟这就是你最爱玩弄的「权衡」之术嘛。

**Taoran:** Lastly, a word of caution to you... I heard that Hoolay has escaped from prison and is headed directly for the Skysplitter. The bloodshed at the Wardance is bound to be gruesome. Perhaps, even before my trial, your impeachment will have you in dire straits first?

> **涛然：**最后我要提醒将军…我听闻呼雷脱狱，直奔竞锋舰而去，血洗演武仪典的惨状恐怕不难想象。也许在我被判罪之前，联盟的弹劾会先让将军焦头烂额吧？

**Jing Yuan:** Regrettably, Mr. Taoran, the Skysplitter only hosted Cloud Knights today, no audience.

> **景元：**很遗憾，涛然先生，今日的竞锋舰上只有云骑，没有观众。

**Jing Yuan:** And just moments ago, Hoolay was executed by the Cloud Knights.

> **景元：**就在刚才，呼雷，已在云骑围攻下授首了。

**Taoran:** ...

> **涛然：**……

**Taoran:** Heh... Haha... Hahahahaha...

> **涛然：**呵呵…哈哈…哈哈哈哈哈哈……

Returning to the Trailblazer's POV...

When you have a chance to make a choice, make one that you know you won't regret.

## 035　A Fugue From Beyond　玉阙黄钟，天外归鸿

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Alchemy Commission (Location)　|　角色：Trailblazer; Bailu; March 7th; Lingsha; Yunli; Feixiao; Moze; Jiaoqiu; Jing Yuan; Huaiyan; Yao Guang; Ruan Mei; Fugue　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''Ebb of Past Sins'' completed

### Visit the wounded in the Alchemy Commission

> *As the dust finally settles, you and March 7th arrange to head to the Alchemy Commission and visit those who were heavily wounded in the great battle aboard the Skysplitter...*

Operation "Wolfhunt Showdown" has ended. The messengers from the Realm-Keeping Commission are calming down the populace and the momentary disruption has subsided.

Meanwhile, in the Alchemy Commission, (Trailblazer) is mourning for someone...

**Bailu:** Waaah... Miss March...

> **白露：**呜呜呜…三月七小姐……

**(Trailblazer):** ...

> **开拓者：**……

- ▸ March 7th... I didn't think you'd...

> ▸ 三月七…没想到你会…

- ▸ There's so much we didn't have the chance to say...

> ▸ 有太多话还没来得及讲…

**Bailu:** Miss March! My dear Miss March!

> **白露：**三月小姐！我的三月小姐！

**March 7th:** I was only in the bathroom for a little bit, and you've already arranged my death? (Trailblazer), can't you play some normal games with Dragon Lady?

> **三月七：**我去个盥洗室的工夫，你们都给我安排好了是吧？开拓者，你能不能陪龙女大人玩点阳间的游戏？

- ▸ This kind of make-believe adds a genuine touch!

> ▸ 这样过家家比较真情实感！

**March 7th:** Thanks a lot for that!

> **三月七：**我谢谢你哦！

- ▸ This was Bailu's idea!

> ▸ 这是白露的主意！

**Bailu:** You really can't keep your mouth shut, can you?

> **白露：**你这人嘴巴太松了吧！

- ▸ Help! It's a zombie!

> ▸ 救命啊！诈尸了！

**March 7th:** Are you amused?

> **三月七：**好玩吗？

**March 7th:** Alright! Enough playing. It's time to visit my masters.

> **三月七：**好啦！别玩了，该去探望我的师父们了。

**Bailu:** The infirmary is right over there. You can go by yourselves. Come back and visit me when you have time!

> **白露：**病房就在那边，你们自己过去吧～下次有时间再来找我玩哦！

**March 7th:** Next time, let's not play overly realistic make-believe with (Trailblazer), okay?

> **三月七：**下次别再和开拓者玩这种超真实过家家了好不好？

**(Trailblazer):** Well, there's not much I could do. General Feixiao didn't really give me any chance to shine during this Wolf Hunt operation.

> **开拓者：**没办法嘛～这次猎狼行动，飞霄将军没给我什么发挥的空间。

**(Trailblazer):** If I had been in the ring, I could've knocked down ten Hoolays with just one strike.

> **开拓者：**要换我在擂台上，一棒下去，就算十个呼雷也要给我趴下。

**March 7th:** You sound exactly like Master Yanqing. But for now, both he and Yunli have been forced to go to the Alchemy Commission for some well-needed rest.

> **三月七：**你的话倒是和彦卿师父说得一毛一样。不过嘛，眼下他和云璃全都被送进了丹鼎司好好强制休养了。

**March 7th:** I'm the least accomplished in swordplay, but I came out unscathed. I really ought to thank my masters for taking all the blows on my behalf.

> **三月七：**唉，剑术造诣最低的我却没什么事，得好好感谢二位师父替我分担成吨的伤害才行啊。

### Ask Lingsha about Yanqing's condition

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Lingsha:** Look who's here. The beloved (Trailblazer) and the charming March 7th.

> **灵砂：**呵呵，看看这是谁来了。人见人爱的开拓者，以及花见花开的小三月。

**Lingsha:** You came to the Alchemy Commission today... Oh, I get it. (Trailblazer) is here with March to visit her masters, aren't you?

> **灵砂：**今天你们来丹鼎司…哦，我明白了，开拓者想必是来陪三月探望两位师父的吧？

**March 7th:** Exactly. How are my masters doing at the moment?

> **三月七：**没错没错，二位师父目前还好吧？

**Yunli:** Unfortunately, only one master is here. I'm doing fine.

> **云璃：**可惜，在这里只有一位师父，我还好哦。

- ▸ Did Master Yunli run out on her own?

> ▸ 云璃师父擅自跑出来的？

**Yunli:** I'm just following the doctor's advice. Miss Bailu said I could take a stroll, so here I am.

> **云璃：**我也是听从医嘱的人，白露小姐说我可以随便走走，我才出来走走的。

**Yunli:** Your Master Yanqing wasn't as lucky. He's probably still in bed...

> **云璃：**你们的彦卿师父就没有那么幸运了，估计现在还躺着吧……

- ▸ Master Yanqing... didn't make it in the end?

> ▸ 彦卿…终究是没撑住吗？

**March 7th:** Hey, watch your words! Don't you jinx my master!

> **三月七：**喂，小心说话！你可不要咒我师父喔！

**Yunli:** His injuries weren't that severe... but well, he'll still need some more time to rest.

> **云璃：**他的伤倒是没有重到那个程度…不过嘛，更长的静养时间还是少不了的。

**Yunli:** Considering the intense battle with Hoolay and then with General Feixiao... ahem, the rigorous sparring session with her! His small body couldn't take it anymore.

> **云璃：**毕竟又是激战呼雷，又是和飞霄将军…咳，狠狠切磋了一下武艺！他那副小身板，早就顶不住了。

**March 7th:** Master Yunli, didn't you also engage in combat?

> **三月七：**云璃师父，你不也是大战了一场吗？

**Yunli:** Haha, the little babies from the Luofu just don't have the resilience that us kids from the Zhuming have!

> **云璃：**哼哼，罗浮的小娃娃呀，体质就是没咱们朱明的孩子这么棒！

**Lingsha:** I heard from Moze about the tough battle against the Wolftroopers. (Trailblazer), you fought alongside Moze. Did you get hurt?

> **灵砂：**对了，听貊泽说起他们这队人马被狼卒围困，打了一场苦战。开拓者，你和貊泽并肩作战，有没有受伤？

- ▸ ...The enemies were mostly taken care of by General Feixiao.

> ▸ …敌人基本被飞霄将军解决了。

**Yunli:** I heard a few versions of the story from the Cloud Knights who were there. The number of Wolftroopers went from a few dozen to thousands, and the number keeps rising. It's becoming more absurd by the minute!

> **云璃：**我听在场的云骑说了。但从几十个狼卒到数千个狼卒有十来种版本的传言…目前数量还在继续增长中，越听越离谱！

- ▸ Now that I think about it, I didn't really have a chance to get hurt.

> ▸ 仔细想来，似乎没有机会受伤。

**Yunli:** I can guess why: General Feixiao is just too strong.

> **云璃：**原因大概能够猜到了，一定是飞霄将军太强了。

**Lingsha:** Yunli, go and lie down!

> **灵砂：**云璃，你给我好好躺着去！

**Yunli:** Got it, Lingsha.

> **云璃：**我知道了，灵砂姐姐。

**March 7th:** What about that smiley fox healer and the guy with the hood...

> **三月七：**对了，那个眯眯眼的狐狸医士，还有那个戴兜帽的家伙……

**Lingsha:** Thank goodness (Trailblazer) and the others found Jiaoqiu. He lost a lot of blood and needs to rest properly. He and Moze are seriously wounded. I've had to forcibly confine them to the Alchemy Commission.

> **灵砂：**幸好开拓者他们找到了重伤的椒丘，他失血太多，需要好好静养。他和貊泽都身负重伤，被妾身狠狠地关了禁闭，一个都别想离开丹鼎司。

**Lingsha:** All three from the Yaoqing are critically injured, but fortunately, the Xianzhou Luofu has a miraculous Healer Lady. It's a silver lining in this misfortune.

> **灵砂：**这三人伤势都危及性命，得亏了咱们罗浮仙舟还有一位神奇的衔药龙女，已是不幸中的万幸了。

**March 7th:** Ah, it looks like the entire delegation from the Xianzhou Yaoqing has gathered at the Alchemy Commission. Why don't we go see General Feixiao and bring her some fruit?

> **三月七：**啊，看来这曜青仙舟的使团，整个都在丹鼎司里集合了。要不我们买些水果，去看望一下飞霄将军？

**Lingsha:** I would normally say no because they need to rest... but, alas, what can you do when the two of them have a leader who is just as restless?

> **灵砂：**飞霄将军刚从静养病房里溜走了，她自以为身手了得。整个丹鼎司上下动静我可一清二楚。

**Lingsha:** General Feixiao just slipped out of the recovery room. She thinks she's so sneaky, but I know everything that happens in the Alchemy Commission.

> **灵砂：**你们若想探望，可以去波月古海边转转，我瞧见她向那儿去了。

**Lingsha:** If you're looking to meet her, the Lunarescent Depths might be your best bet. I spotted her heading that way earlier.

> **灵砂：**我刚才在波月古海边散步时，见到飞霄将军正在陪他们闲聊。你们若想探望，可以去那边转转。

**Jiaoqiu:** The sound of those footsteps... They must belong to General Feixiao. Out and about despite the doctor's orders?

> **椒丘：**哈，听脚步声…这不是飞霄将军吗？怎么不听医嘱好好养伤，一个人又跑出来了。

**Feixiao:** What a coincidence! Someone else who refuses to listen to the doctor's orders.

> **飞霄：**真巧啊，这里还有一位不听医嘱的人。

**Jiaoqiu:** Well, being a doctor myself, I don't think my knowledge of my own body is inferior to the Dragon Lady's.

> **椒丘：**我自己就是医生嘛，对身体情况的了解，未必逊色于那位龙女大人。

**Feixiao:** The healer does not heal himself. Don't try to act tough in front of me.

> **飞霄：**医者不自医，在我面前你就别逞强了。

**Feixiao:** ... I'm sorry, Jiaoqiu. I didn't expect you to use poison that way. What a miscalculation. If only I'd found you sooner... If only I hadn't sent you to The Shackling Prison...

> **飞霄：**…抱歉，椒丘。我没想到你会用「以毒饲狼」的做法，多么失策，要是我能早一点找到你们…要是我没有派你去幽囚狱……

**Jiaoqiu:** The way you're speaking, is this really the Feixiao I know? Or could it be a borisin assassin, imitating your voice to take my life?

> **椒丘：**这话说的，当真是我认识的那个飞霄吗？难不成是步离人的刺客，佯装成你的声音来取我性命？

**Feixiao:** ...

> **飞霄：**……

**Feixiao:** Jiaoqiu, your eyes... You can't see anymore, can you?

> **飞霄：**椒丘，你的眼睛…看不见了，是吗？

**Jiaoqiu:** I can still clearly hear the sound of the waves. That's enough for me.

> **椒丘：**现在我还能听见海浪的声音，它们很清晰…这便足够了。

**Jiaoqiu:** Don't blame yourself. You know what I'm more concerned about. Has your body had any changes since you swallowed the Crimson Moon?

> **椒丘：**不必自责，你知道我更关心什么：在吞下「赤月」后，你的身体有什么变化吗？

**Feixiao:** I'm not sure. There don't appear to be any changes... However, the many doubts that once plagued me have dissipated.

> **飞霄：**我不知道，它似乎什么都没有改变…但以前困扰我的许多疑虑烟消云散了……

**Jiaoqiu:** I never thought I'd live to hear you discuss your health again... But thanks to Moze and that child, the price I've paid seems trivial compared to what we've achieved.

> **椒丘：**我本以为自己无法再活着听你谈论自己的身体了…但，感谢貊泽和那个孩子。相对于如今的结果，我所支付的代价实在微不足道……

**Jiaoqiu:** I hold no grievances, Feixiao. I am content.

> **椒丘：**我毫无怨言，飞霄，我很满足。

**Feixiao:** ...I don't know how to offer words of comfort, nor do I know much about curing others' ailments.

> **飞霄：**…我不懂该怎么说些宽慰人心的话，对怎么治好别人的病也一窍不通。

**Feixiao:** I am just a warrior, so my pledge to you is simple...

> **飞霄：**我只是一介武人，所以，我也只能给你一种承诺……

**Moze:** ...An eye for an eye.

> **貊泽：**…「以牙还牙，以眼还眼」。

**Jiaoqiu:** So you were here too.

> **椒丘：**原来你也在啊。

**Feixiao:** In this vast universe, there's bound to be someone who can heal your eyes. I'll find them. But until then...

> **飞霄：**宇宙浩瀚，一定有人能治好你的双眼，我会找到他。而在那之前……

**Jiaoqiu:** The threat behind all this chaos needs to be dealt with.

> **椒丘：**这场风波背后的威胁也需有人清理。

**Jiaoqiu:** You must already have some ideas. Let them out.

> **椒丘：**想必你心里已有了念头。说出来吧。

**Feixiao:** The resurgence of the Sanctus Medicus and the gathering of the borisin... are merely distractions orchestrated by an unseen force, meant to divide the Alliance's attention.

> **飞霄：**药王残党的出世，步离人的群集…那只隐于幕后的黑手搅动风云，要的便是联盟首尾难顾，最终如一支流矢，无所中的。

**Feixiao:** Unfortunately, they picked the wrong fight this time. Once we return to the Yaoqing, I'll personally lead the Verdant Knights into battle.

> **飞霄：**可惜这一次，他们惹错了人。待返回曜青后，我将亲自率领青丘军出征……

**Feixiao:** ...I vow to take down a Lord Ravager and teach the Ruin Legion the true meaning of The Hunt.

> **飞霄：**…誓要击落一名绝灭大君，令烬灭军团明白「巡猎」的意义。

**Jiaoqiu:** ...

> **椒丘：**……

**Jiaoqiu:** I knew it... You've always been so impatient.

> **椒丘：**我就知道…你一向是个停不下来的急性子啊。

### Join the Generals for their strategy meeting

> *At this moment, the three generals are gathered in the Seat of Divine Foresight to discuss the future strategy of the Alliance...*

Seat of Divine Foresight

**Feixiao:** I apologize for my tardiness, once again.

> **飞霄：**抱歉，和来时一样，我又迟到了。

**Feixiao:** The Alchemy Commission detained me for some time for a health examination. They released me only after ensuring I was in good condition.

> **飞霄：**丹鼎司留我查验身体状况，花了不少时间，待确认无事后他们才将我放了回来。

**Huaiyan:** General, seeing you safe and sound puts our minds at ease.

> **怀炎：**将军安然无恙，老朽和景元悬着的心可算是放下了。

**Huaiyan:** Hoolay's escape caused significant upheaval and forced the Wardance to be put on hold — a truly unforeseen disaster. Fortunately, the younger generation showed their valor. They bravely tackled the crisis, putting an end to the disaster and providing a glimmer of hope in a dire situation.

> **怀炎：**此番呼雷逃狱之事，引发不小的动荡，也令演武仪典被迫中断，当真是一场无妄之灾。好在后生可畏，几个年轻人赴汤蹈火，弭平了这场大灾，也算是不幸中的万幸。

**Jing Yuan:** Before Master Diviner Fu Xuan's departure for the Yuque, I consulted with her about the Wardance. She left a note stating, "The hexagram oscillates between thunder and heaven, a sign of great power," assuring us that we would successfully navigate any challenges.

> **景元：**太卜符玄在前往玉阙仙舟述职前，我曾向她问卜仪典之事。她留下字条说「卦象涨落于震乾之间，是大壮之相」，纵有波折也能安然度过。

**Jing Yuan:** She advised me to trust the younger generation's abilities and let them lead. Her predictions, it appears, have come true.

> **景元：**她要我相信年轻人的能力，放手任其施为。如此说来，卜测确实应验了。

**Jing Yuan:** It's just... the Xianzhou Luofu's longstanding duty of keeping Hoolay imprisoned ended with his death on the Skysplitter. This event is likely to bring much criticism from the Alliance's upper ranks.

> **景元：**只是…罗浮仙舟数百年来负责囚禁呼雷，押解移交前，他却死在了竞锋舰的擂台上。这件事传入联盟高层的耳朵里，又会惹来不少非议。

**Feixiao:** However, Hoolay's demise might actually be a blessing in disguise. Broadcasting this news could derail the borisin's resurgence and quash the ambitions of those who wished to take advantage of his return.

> **飞霄：**呼雷之死未必是坏事。消息传开，既能重挫步离人卷土重来的企图，也能绝了有心人利用他的野心。

**Feixiao:** Hand over Hoolay's remains to the Xianzhou Yaoqing, and I'll handle the explanations to the Alliance.

> **飞霄：**将呼雷的遗体移交曜青仙舟即可，至于向联盟解释的事情，由我来办就好。

**Feixiao:** But, what about the Wardance? While the wolf-hunt mission was a success, the events on the Skysplitter remain a secret. However, the news of the borisin's attacks on the streets will spread like wildfire.

> **飞霄：**倒是「演武仪典」该怎么说？虽然猎狼计划顺利完成，竞锋舰上的事无人知晓。但步离人袭击闹市的消息一定会不胫而走。

**Huaiyan:** The Wardance was interrupted, so it must be reconvened. As for the people who witnessed the attacks, beyond placating them, we also need to show them the Xianzhou Cloud Knights' fearless dedication.

> **怀炎：**仪典中断，就务必重开。至于目击袭击的观众，我们不仅要多加安抚，也要让他们看到仙舟云骑的舍生忘死。

**Huaiyan:** "On the Wardance's opening day, rogue borisin attempted to stir up panic but were swiftly neutralized by the Merlin's Claw and the Cloud Knights." A genuine tale of heroism that will captivate and calm the public.

> **怀炎：**「仪典首日，有步离凶徒妄图制造恐慌，幸得前来观礼的天击将军率云骑化解危机，击毙狂徒。」这样的英雄故事不但货真价实，传开了也人人爱看，最能平息谣言非议。

**Jing Yuan:** As Elder Huaiyan suggests, the Luofu will do its best to heal the wounded and compensate those who were affected. We aim to reopen the Wardance in the coming weeks.

> **景元：**那就依炎老的意思。罗浮会尽力抚恤死伤，裨补损失，务求在数周之内重开演武仪典。

**Huaiyan:** This settles the immediate concerns on the Luofu, but my thoughts linger on the orchestrator behind these events.

> **怀炎：**如此一来，罗浮内的事情算是议定了。不过我更担心这桩祸事背后所涉及的那只黑手——

**Huaiyan:** From the onset of the Ambrosial Arbor Crisis, the Xianzhou Luofu has experienced numerous disturbances, directly linked to the Lord Ravager Phantylia.

> **怀炎：**从建木灾异开始，罗浮仙舟所经历种种变乱，全都与绝灭大君「幻胧」息息相关。

**Feixiao:** Her attempt to infiltrate the Xianzhou with the borisin and sway the Luofu Preceptors to join the Sanctus Medicus' insurrection was unsuccessful. But I have a feeling that she is indifferent to the success of her plans.

> **飞霄：**操纵步离人渗透仙舟，唆使罗浮龙师加入药王秘传乱党…虽然从结果来看，她的图谋暂时无法奏效。但我有种感觉…也许她并不在意自己的计划是否能成功。

**Jing Yuan:** Rather than outright victory, Phantylia's motive seems to lie in breeding discord and chaos among allies. Should the Alliance fail to mend these rifts, it risks disintegrating into a pile of sand.

> **景元：**由过往行事的风格来看，比起武力上的胜利，幻胧更希望能在盟友之间凿开不和的裂缝，播撒混乱的种子。如果联盟无法弥合这些裂痕，最终就会崩解成一盘散沙。

**Huaiyan:** The goal of the Xianzhou Alliance has not changed for thousands of years. Perhaps we should point the weapons in our hands toward this new adversary. I plan to bring this matter before the marshal following the Wardance.

> **怀炎：**仙舟联盟数千年未曾变改的目标，我们手中所握的锋镝，也许应该顺势而动，指向新的敌人了。待到仪典结束，我打算向元帅进言上奏此事。

**Jing Yuan:** With Elder Huaiyan's insight, one wonders how the marshal will react.

> **景元：**以炎老的洞见，元帅会如何应对？

**Huaiyan:** It's clear we're engaged in a game of chess not seen in thousands of Amber Eras, a game where even the strategists become pawns in battle, where the stakes are as high as the fates of countless stars in the sky.

> **怀炎：**到了这一步，棋盘上所摆的乃是数千琥珀纪以来未曾有过的棋局。在这样的棋局里，就连棋手们也会成为上阵拼杀的卒子，胜负所系乃是天上万千星辰的明灭。

**Huaiyan:** Predicting the marshal's decision is beyond me, yet she's well aware of our concerns. She has tasked General Yao Guang with performing extrapolations day and night to gauge the situation comprehensively.

> **怀炎：**老朽…无法断言元帅的决定。但我们所担忧的一切，元帅并非无所察觉。她已命令爻光将军日夜演算，占决大略。

**Huaiyan:** We gathered today not just to tackle the Luofu's predicaments but also to address a matter requiring our collective presence.

> **怀炎：**今天之所以召各位汇聚此处，不仅仅是为了商议罗浮的难题，还有一件事需要我们三人同时在场——

**Huaiyan:** Yao Guang has sent a message from the Xianzhou Yuque, informing us that she wishes to share the results of recent calculations she made within the matrix.

> **怀炎：**爻光从玉阙仙舟发来了她的通讯印信，她要将近些时日在阵法中参详计算的结果与我们分享。

**Huaiyan:** Jing Yuan, I'd like to borrow the Seat of Divine Foresight's "chess board" for this purpose.

> **怀炎：**景元，我要借神策府那方「棋盘」一用。

An ancient "bell" has been struck in the depths of the Xianzhou Yuque, its soundwaves rippling over the valleys and hills of gravity.

Thus, light bursts forth from the chessboard's grid, weaving into a seal symbolizing the Seer Strategist's authority.

**Yao Guang:** Hello everyone, it's been a long time! Over three decades have passed since we last convened, and I've dearly missed each of you generals during this time ☰☰.

> **爻光：**各位，好久不见啊！距离上一次列席会议已过去三十多年，这段时间我可是一直日夜思念着几位将军啊☰☰。

**Feixiao:** Was the Seer Strategist thinking of us, or thinking of the fates we're destined for?

> **飞霄：**戎韬将军是念叨我们几人，还是「念叨」我们几人的命数走向啊？

**Yao Guang:** The Merlin's Claw, as blunt as always, ☳☶ though not everyone's cup of tea, huh?

> **爻光：**哈哈，天击将军还是这般快人快语，☳☶可不讨人喜欢喔。

**Yao Guang:** You once told Jing Yuan, "You're lucky that I'm the one who came this time. If it were the Seer Strategist, this conversation might not be so friendly." I'm intrigued — did that conversation really come to pass?

> **爻光：**此前还对景元先生说什么「幸好此行是我前来，若是换做戎韬将军，问话或许就不会这么友善了」——我可是瞧得一清二楚，不知这场对话成真了没有？

**Jing Yuan:** I've never heard anyone mention that the Deca-Light Reflection Barrier could eavesdrop on even the quietest whispers. It appears that the marshal sent not just two generals to the Luofu but an uninvited third guest as well.

> **景元：**我可从没听人说起过，「十方光映法界」连嚼舌根的密谈都能算得一清二楚。看来元帅不止派了两位将军前来罗浮观礼，还有第三位客人不请自来啊。

**Yao Guang:** People have been saying that Mr. Jing Yuan's intellect shines as brightly as the Seer Strategist's. With the Luofu caught in such strife, the Yuque couldn't just stand by and watch.

> **爻光：**毕竟联盟总有人说景元先生「智光昭昭，不逊『戎韬』」，罗浮遭此劫难，玉阙自然不会坐视不管☰☲。

**Huaiyan:** Seer Strategist, our time with the Yellowbell is limited. Let's get straight to the point.

> **怀炎：**戎韬将军，黄钟系统能维持的时间有限。长话短说吧。

**Yao Guang:** Alright. Thanks to Mr. Jing Yuan, the interrogation of the two prisoners on the Yuque has concluded.

> **爻光：**好啊。托景元先生的福，那两位囚犯，玉阙已审讯完毕了。

**Yao Guang:** Mr. Jing Yuan's speculations were correct. Of the crimes they confessed to, planting a Stellaron on the Luofu was a complete fabrication, meant to mislead.

> **爻光：**他们自认的罪行中，「将星核送入罗浮」这一项与景元先生的推测一致，不过是子虚乌有、自编自造的障眼法。

**Yao Guang:** Their real aim was to secure a meeting with the marshal to present their Godslayer Protocol — a strategy they firmly believe in.

> **爻光：**而真正的目标…是想借此得到面见元帅的机会，献上他们擘画的「与神相争之策」——这一点千真万确。

**Jing Yuan:** And what was this grand strategy proposed by Jingliu and that traveler?

> **景元：**镜流与那名异邦人，献出了什么策略？

**Yao Guang:** The narrative they painted was vast and difficult to put into words ☳☳. It all revolves around the coffin of the blond traveler...

> **爻光：**可谓是一番难以言说、壮丽绝伦的图景☳☳。奥秘就在于那金发异邦人的棺椁……

**Yao Guang:** ...to be precise, it's the remnants of the Propagation.

> **爻光：**…竟是「繁育」的孑遗。

**Feixiao:** The remains of the Swarm Author?

> **飞霄：**螟蝗祸祖的遗骸？

**Yao Guang:** More accurately, it's a fragment of the divine body. In the future they depicted, this fragment serves as the final nail, sealing the fate of the Plagues Author and securing its doom.

> **爻光：**准确地说，是其神体的一部分。在两位囚犯描绘的未来中，它便是能将「寿瘟祸祖」牢牢钉上末日之途的钉子。

**Yao Guang:** Before reaching that point, battling the gods necessitates a larger alliance. Hence, they sought out an ally for the Xianzhou.

> **爻光：**而在这之前——要与神相争，更大的「联盟」不可或缺。为此，他们还向仙舟引介了一位盟友。

**Yao Guang:** I assume you've all heard of the famed Genius Society member #81, Ruan Mei, haven't you? The "eye" of the Yuque has just seen this uninvited guest arrive on the Xianzhou Luofu.

> **爻光：**想必诸位都听过天才俱乐部#81阮•梅的大名吧？玉阙的「眼」看到了，这位不请自来的客人已经抵达了罗浮仙舟。

**Yao Guang:** ...Three, two, one. Right on time! Now, as for the best way to receive our distinguished guest... I'll leave that in your capable hands.

> **爻光：**…三、二、一。时间刚好！至于如何接待这位贵客…请允许我将这烫手山芋丢给各位咯。

**???:** Do you remember your name?

...

**???:** My name is... Tingyun. I serve as an amicassador of the Whistling Flames merchant guild on the Xianzhou Luofu...

*(Visitor Verification)*

(Yunli has become a visitor to the Express. You might come across Yunli when you return to the Express)

*(Mission completed)*
